# UDU corpus, life event `nature`

**2648 rules.** Personality and mind. Temperament, psychology, motivation, strengths, flaws, mental health, addictions.

Feeds protocol E07 and E03. Grouped by nakshatra, then in corpus order. Each line carries its rule ID, its placement, its source book and page, and its polarity and confidence, in the form `polarity/confidence` where + is benefic, - is malefic, ~ is mixed.

## Ashvini — nature (133)

- **ASH-0023** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · ASH p19 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini (birth nakshatra)
  **THEN** Talent for starting new projects and new beginnings, healing, initiation, leadership, travelling and activism.
- **ASH-0073** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · ASH p55 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashvini pada 2
  **THEN** Happy and prosperous.
- **ASH-0155** · nak Ashvini · ASH p73 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, stylish, truth-loving, swift, impulsive, respectable, deep-thinking, seeking the new, loveable, helpful, charming, lucky, rejuvenating, fast, attractive, honest, elegant and a healer - but short-tempered and a maker or breaker of relationships.
- **ASH-0159** · nak Ashvini · ASH p76 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent (Ketu's latent force active in Aries)
  **THEN** Spontaneous energy with innovative and creative ideas that inspire others; chooses action requiring courage, innovation, initiation, adventure and free movement in open space.
- **ASH-0160** · nak Ashvini · ASH p76 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Contingency approach to action which creates trouble for others and sometimes for the native himself.
- **ASH-0161** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · ASH p76 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in the first 3 deg 20 min of Ashvini
  **THEN** Falls in Gandanta as well as Gandmula dosha - inauspicious.
- **ASH-0166** · nak Ashvini · ASH p77 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Impulsive, disruptive and seeking open space; unstable mind that will not be limited or restricted by anyone.
- **ASH-0169** · nak Ashvini · ASH p77 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Impulsiveness, mood swings and hurriedness produce instability and detachment from a person, event or place.
- **ASH-0171** · nak Ashvini · ASH p77 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Childlike energy that enjoys the action rather than the result, with very restricted understanding of the external environment through inexperience; kept busy with experiment, search and innovation.
- **ASH-0173** · nak Ashvini · ASH p78 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Revival capability makes the native collect faulty, junk or old things and restore them to working condition.
- **ASH-0178** · nak Ashvini · ASH p78 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Places a strong priority on independence and may rebel against authority or freedom-impairing constraints; restless like a horse, constantly seeking excitement and change.
- **ASH-0179** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · ASH p79 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1 (Aries navamsha; Sun, Mars and Ketu fire intensified)
  **THEN** Faces challenges with courage, confidence, aggression and enthusiasm; unpredictable fiery behaviour, indifferent to rivals, fights on with full energy and stamina.
- **ASH-0182** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · ASH p79 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 2 (Taurus navamsha, Venus-ruled)
  **THEN** Materialistic with love of beauty and physical pleasure, youthful appearance, active, resourceful and pragmatic; energies focused in the proper direction giving better execution.
- **ASH-0187** · nak Ashvini, pada 3 · ASH p79 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 3
  **THEN** Intelligent, good decision-maker and powerful communicator; uses energy through words, pen or weapons, works with arms and hands, walks with arms out in front, and enjoys winning people over with scientific knowledge.
- **ASH-0189** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · ASH p79 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 4
  **THEN** Emotionally and sympathetically connected to people, feels their pain at root level; very high mental strength and satisfaction in caring for, supporting and healing others.
- **ASH-0191** · nak Ashvini, house 1 · ASH p80 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Great energy, passion and a dynamic outlook; a go-getter with natural zeal for action, free-spirited, magnetic appearance, unstable mind but helpful nature.
- **ASH-0195** · nak Ashvini, planet Sun · ASH p81 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Ashvini (its exaltation point at 10 degrees falls here)
  **THEN** Focused, egoistic and idiosyncratic in expression; very energetic, taking bold action without fear or restriction, a powerhouse but with no consistency in thought and action.
- **ASH-0197** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · ASH p82 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini
  **THEN** Quick, agile, adaptable mind that thinks on its feet; action-oriented rather than result-oriented, so the next step is hard to predict; a natural initiator with magnetic charm.
- **ASH-0199** · nak Ashvini, planet Mars · ASH p82 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ashvini
  **THEN** Driven by horsepower, does not hesitate to initiate and responds courageously; appears calm but acts with great force, emotion and aggression.
- **ASH-0202** · nak Ashvini, planet Mercury · ASH p83 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ashvini
  **THEN** Ashvini fuels an unstable thought process; thoughts divided by contradictory ideas, serious conflict in fixing a view, and difficulty in decision-making.
- **ASH-0207** · nak Ashvini, planet Venus · ASH p85 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Ashvini, female native
  **THEN** Experiences her femininity freely and feels rejuvenated, energised, refreshed and healed.
- **ASH-0210** · nak Ashvini, planet Saturn · ASH p86 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini
  **THEN** Saturn limits Ashvini's energy and the native feels depressed, suffocated and dull; he behaves like a teenager in old age, acting childishly and romantically late in life.
- **ASH-0212** · nak Ashvini, planet Saturn · ASH p87 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini
  **THEN** Structured, disciplined and methodical, tenacious and persistent, cautious rather than impulsive, mature and dependable early in life, with karmic lessons about self-control, accountability and leadership.
- **ASH-0214** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · ASH p88 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashvini
  **THEN** Amplified ambition and desire for fame and attention, strong determination to overcome constraints, charismatic influence - but impulsivity and snap judgments, with karmic themes of ambition, desire and risk-taking to resolve.
- **AVA-2-0027** · nak Ashvini · AVA-2 p16 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini placement anywhere in a chart
  **THEN** Nature is fast, instinctive and curative - it rushes in where others pause and it mends; the governing word of its predictions is urgency.
- **AVA-2-0028** · nak Ashvini · AVA-2 p16 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini's shidhra vyapani shakti operating unrefined
  **THEN** Ashvini heals, rescues and pioneers, but also leaps before it looks, tires of what it has begun and leaves the finishing to others.
- **AVA-2-0032** · nak Ashvini · AVA-2 p16 · -/stated
  **IF** A planet in Ashvini lacks dignity or good aspect
  **THEN** The same speed that pioneers turns to rashness, accident and quarrel; the same detachment that liberates becomes desertion. Ashvini colours a placement; it never repairs a broken one.
- **AVA-2-0033** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · AVA-2 p17 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1, 00°00'-03°20', Aries navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** Nothing softens Ashvini here; the nakshatra arrives in its rawest form - acts before it reasons, moves first, speaks first, risks first and corrects course afterwards.
- **AVA-2-0034** · nak Ashvini · AVA-2 p17 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Ashvini compared
  **THEN** The first burns, the second builds, the third talks, the fourth feels.
- **AVA-2-0035** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · AVA-2 p17 · +/classical
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1
  **THEN** Physically active and hard to tire; bold, competitive, quick to decide, impatient with delay; ambitious for a name, independent, strong-willed, honest to bluntness.
- **AVA-2-0041** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · AVA-2 p17 · -/classical
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1 unrefined (Carana Phala)
  **THEN** A thieving impulse, tale-carrying, wavering mind, damaging company, appetite for sense pleasure, fault-finding and enjoyment taken at another's cost.
- **AVA-2-0042** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · AVA-2 p17 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1 at its best
  **THEN** Courage others rely on, command in a crisis, physical strength, fast execution and the rare ability to open a venture from nothing and make it stand.
- **AVA-2-0043** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · AVA-2 p18 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 2, 03°20'-06°40', Taurus navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** Venus grounds the fire; the rush turns into production and the quarter wants results it can touch - money, comfort, beauty, property. Practical, resourceful, graceful, fond of splendour, ambitious with a steady hand, far more stable than pada 1 and often good-looking.
- **AVA-2-0050** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · AVA-2 p18 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 2 unrefined
  **THEN** Indulgence, dependence on comfort, family bitterness, delicate health and difficulty holding discipline once life turns pleasant.
- **AVA-2-0051** · nak Ashvini, pada 3 · AVA-2 p18 · +/classical
  **IF** Ashvini pada 3, 06°40'-10°00', Gemini navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** The classics call this the speedy jack of the zodiac: comprehension is instant; communicative, humorous, mentally agile, adaptable, curious, skilled in conversation and a genuine lover of words.
- **AVA-2-0058** · nak Ashvini, pada 3 · AVA-2 p19 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 3 unrefined
  **THEN** Over-cleverness, cutting speech, scattered learning and manipulation dressed up as advice.
- **AVA-2-0059** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · AVA-2 p19 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 4, 10°00'-13°20', Cancer navamsha, Moon
  **THEN** The physician's instinct turns inward; this quarter senses what people need before they say it and acts through feeling. Empathetic, intuitive, tuned to public mood, wise, truthful, clever with money, long-lived, fond of travel, well-liked by women.
- **AVA-2-0065** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · AVA-2 p20 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 4 unrefined
  **THEN** Decisions made on mood, emotional aggression, sadness that settles in and stays, pleasure-seeking and over-attachment.
- **AVA-2-0067** · nak Ashvini, planet Sun · AVA-2 p20 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Ashvini aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Hardness of temper, at the extreme a cruel streak; reddish eyes.
- **AVA-2-0072** · nak Ashvini, pada 1, planet Sun · AVA-2 p21 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Ashvini pada 1
  **THEN** Stout build, persuasive in argument, self-confident; long life, wealth and happiness through children - a strong and expressive placement.
- **AVA-2-0102** · nak Ashvini, planet Mars · AVA-2 p22 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ashvini aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Love of display and company that costs reputation.
- **AVA-2-0123** · nak Ashvini, pada 1, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p23 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ashvini pada 1, unaspected
  **THEN** Disbelief, low placement, appetite for wine and company, deceit and refusal to cooperate.
- **AVA-2-0124** · nak Ashvini, pada 1, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p23 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ashvini pada 1 aspected by the Moon
  **THEN** Damages conduct badly.
- **AVA-2-0134** · nak Ashvini, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p23 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Ashvini aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Poor manners and a taste for pointless disputes.
- **AVA-2-0156** · nak Ashvini, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p24 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Outspoken and helpful, yet distant from people.
- **AVA-2-0166** · nak Ashvini, pada 4, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p25 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini pada 4 aspected by the Moon
  **THEN** Harms conduct and appearance.
- **AVA-2-0179** · nak Ashvini, house 1 · AVA-2 p26 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Ashvini
  **THEN** Quick, blunt, restless, youthful in looks and manner, unwilling to wait; lean body, childlike alertness, a scar near the head common; life proceeds by fresh starts - new ventures, cities, versions of the self.
- **AVA-2-0181** · nak Ashvini, house 1 · AVA-2 p26 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord in Ashvini, unrefined
  **THEN** A life made entirely of beginnings, with anger that flares and projects abandoned halfway.
- **AVA-2-0187** · nak Ashvini, house 3 · AVA-2 p27 · -/stated
  **IF** Third lord in Ashvini, unrefined
  **THEN** Risk without planning, over-talking and unfinished effort.
- **AVA-2-0218** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · AVA-2 p30 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini
  **THEN** The mind becomes quick, alert and easily bored; thought jumps, comprehension is immediate, patience short; feeling rises and fades fast, making the person warm and unpredictable within the same hour.
- **AVA-2-0234** · nak Ashvini, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p32 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini
  **THEN** Sets up a permanent argument inside the native between hesitation and haste; the lesson is disciplined action.
- **AVA-2-0243** · nak Ashvini, house 1 · AVA-2 p34 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Ashvini
  **THEN** Identity gets built around movement - alive while starting, travelling, fixing or rescuing, unwell while waiting; vitality high, recovery fast; head and face carry a mark and age sits lightly. Dashas of planets touching this degree open fresh chapters; the lifelong lesson is completion.
- **AVA-2-0247** · nak Ashvini, house 3 · AVA-2 p34 · +/stated
  **IF** Third bhava madhya in Ashvini
  **THEN** Ashvini's natural room: courage sits at the centre of the house of effort and the first response to any obstacle is to act; short journeys are frequent and often decided the same day; hands are skilful with tools, instruments, vehicles or a keyboard. Courage is rarely the problem; planning is.
- **NAT-0036** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · NAT p76 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini
  **THEN** Speed, vitality and drive for new beginnings; unrestrained energy that needs disciplined mastery to be useful.
- **NAT-0037** · nak Ashvini · NAT p76 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Energy of conception, spontaneity and pure potential; the stage before a person or idea has fully taken form.
- **NAT-0039** · nak Ashvini · NAT p77 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini natives, per the Asvini Kumaras' denial of divine status
  **THEN** Great talent yet a persistent hunger for acknowledgment; frustration when efforts go unnoticed or unappreciated.
- **NAT-0040** · nak Ashvini · NAT p78 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Capacity of perception that sees past outer appearance to the deeper truth.
- **NAT-0041** · nak Ashvini · NAT p79 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini natives facing obstacles
  **THEN** Often need the intervention or guidance of an external figure to overcome struggles and emerge stronger; strength comes from wisdom, not force.
- **NAT-0044** · nak Ashvini · NAT p80 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini's drive left unchecked and undisciplined
  **THEN** Recklessness, restlessness, impatience and impulsivity.
- **NAT-0045** · nak Ashvini · NAT p80 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini's horse tunnel-vision
  **THEN** Relentless single-minded focus that charges forward undeterred; but narrowed perception, missed details and disregard of alternative views and long-term consequences.
- **NAT-0046** · nak Ashvini · NAT p80 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini symbolised by the horse's head rather than the whole horse
  **THEN** Power lies in intelligence, decisiveness and leadership, not in brute motion.
- **NAT-0047** · nak Ashvini · NAT p81 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent (archetype of the Bold Initiator)
  **THEN** Innate urge to act without waiting or seeking approval; fearless momentum, unshakable confidence and insatiable pursuit of new beginnings.
- **NAT-0048** · nak Ashvini · NAT p81 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Sharp instincts, unconventional solutions and an uncanny ability to seize opportunities before others see them; natural trailblazer, pioneer and problem-solver.
- **NAT-0049** · nak Ashvini · NAT p81 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini's momentum without focus
  **THEN** Impulsive, scattered and unsustainable action.
- **NAT-0050** · nak Ashvini · NAT p82 · ~/stated
  **IF** Every Ashvini native at some point meets a hurdle speed alone cannot clear
  **THEN** Requires a stabilising force — a mentor, grounding philosophy or deep self-reflection — to refine raw energy into mastery.
- **NAT-0051** · nak Ashvini · NAT p82 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini native initiates change and brings healing
  **THEN** Contributions are absorbed too swiftly for acknowledgment; produces restlessness and a compulsion to keep proving worth.
- **NAT-0052** · nak Ashvini · NAT p83 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Moves first and adapts along the way; will not wait for ideal conditions or external permission, believing clarity emerges through action.
- **NAT-0055** · nak Ashvini · NAT p84 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini acting on impulse or surface-level judgement
  **THEN** Missteps and unnecessary obstacles; insight must be developed alongside instinct.
- **NAT-0056** · nak Ashvini · NAT p84 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini movement not aligned with purpose
  **THEN** Motion that merely distracts and does not transform; only purposeful action creates real change.
- **NAT-0069** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · NAT p88 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 1 (0°00'-3°20' Aries), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Boldness, courage and natural leadership instinct; swiftness channelled into decisive purposeful action, with risk of impulsiveness and burnout.
- **NAT-0070** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · NAT p88 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 2 (3°20'-6°40' Aries), Taurus navamsa, Venus-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Restless energy stabilised into sustainable growth; creativity, harmony and practical material achievement, with a caution against overindulgence in comforts.
- **NAT-0071** · nak Ashvini, pada 3 · NAT p88 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 3 (6°40'-10°00' Aries), Gemini navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Quick-wittedness, adaptability, vibrant communication and collaborative creative ventures; risk of scattered focus and restlessness.
- **NAT-0072** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · NAT p89 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini pada 4 (10°00'-13°20' Aries), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa

- **NAT-0075** · nak Ashvini · NAT p90 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini's rajasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Overactivity, distraction and difficulty completing what has been started.
- **NAT-0911** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · NAT p565 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini
  **THEN** Fuels swift action, healing potential and an intuitive ability to initiate change.

  **IF** Sun in Ashvini aspected by Mercury

  **IF** Sun in Ashvini aspected by Venus

  **IF** Moon in Ashvini aspected by the Sun
  **THEN** Helping yet cruel nature, and the comforts of a good position.

  **IF** Mercury in Ashvini aspected by Venus

  **IF** Saturn in Ashvini aspected by Mars

- **RBS-1-1101** · nak Ashvini · RBS-1 p160 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashvini, the horse nakshatra

- **RBS-1-1103** · nak Ashvini · RBS-1 p161 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashvini
  **THEN** Quick and impulsive, takes brave decisions to the point of attempting the impossible so that others call them foolish or suicidal; straightforward in nature.
- **RBS-1-1104** · nak Ashvini · RBS-1 p161 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashvini, a young, energetic but adamant nakshatra
  **THEN** Good beginners but poor finishers, comfortable only in short activities, helpful but not for long, lacking patience to concentrate, jumping into work without advice or knowledge.

  **IF** Male born in Ashvini
  **THEN** Faithful, sacrifices anything for loved ones, keeps patience in the greatest peril, is the best adviser to those in agony, a firm believer in God, and keeps his surroundings clean.
- **RBS-1-1109** · nak Ashvini · RBS-1 p162 · -/stated

- **RBS-2-0547** · nak Ashvini, planet Ketu · RBS-2 p141 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashvini Nakshatra, ruled by Ketu, symbol the horse's head
  **THEN** Extremely good-looking, powerful and bright; martial, honest, action-oriented and self-motivated, always chasing a dream, marked by dynamism and speed.
- **RBS-2-0926** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p256 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashvini Nakshatra, ruled by Ketu with the horse's head symbol
  **THEN** Indicates the beginning of anything, and gives anxiousness, hyper-alertness and hyper-sensitivity to the surrounding environment.
- **RBS-2-0928** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p256 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini native facing a decision

  **IF** Ashvini native at work

- **RBS-2-0932** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p257 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashvini Nakshatra
  **THEN** Daring, handsome, truthful, prosperous, self-sufficient, intelligent, popular, knowledgeable, rich, well mannered, outspoken, an explorer with sportsmanship, vitality, courage, dynamism and initiative, and a contented family life.
- **RBS-2-0933** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p257 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Ashvini Nakshatra

- **RBS-2-0934** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p257 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Ashvini Nakshatra
  **THEN** Rash impulsiveness taking unnecessary risks and making blunders that create danger and trouble, with revengefulness and stubbornness.

- **RBS-2-0938** · nak Ashvini · RBS-2 p257 · ~/stated

  **IF** Moon in Ashvini pada 1 (Aries 0°00' to 3°20')
  **THEN** Unsteady mind, sinful attachments, indulgence in sensual pleasures and a tendency to criticise others; excessive qualities of the Nakshatra, with a goat-like face, small eyes and nose, ordinary features, slim body and heavy voice.
- **RBS-2-0953** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · RBS-2 p259 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini pada 2 (Aries 3°20' to 6°40')
  **THEN** Scriptural knowledge, many talents, a jack of all trades, handsome and a good learner; comparatively stable, directing effort in the right direction; muscular body, small forehead, big eyes and soft voice.
- **RBS-2-0955** · nak Ashvini, pada 3 · RBS-2 p259 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini pada 3 (Aries 6°40' to 10°00')
  **THEN** Mathematical talent, earning by physical labour, a good teacher or advertiser, suffering from piles, with a villainous streak; intelligent with good decision-making, fair complexion, thin hair, beautiful eyes and nose, slender limbs and strong communication skills.
- **RBS-2-0957** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · RBS-2 p260 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini pada 4 (Aries 10°00' to 13°20')
  **THEN** Effeminate, wise, truthful, rich, liked by the opposite sex, with knowledge of astrology; emotional and helping, often in the medical profession; slender body, small nose and questioning child-like eyes.
- **TAM-RA-0003** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p40 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Warrior qualities are exaggerated: the native is a high-risk taker, highly impulsive, and reacts quickly, which invites trouble and misery.
- **TAM-RA-0008** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p41 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Native craves action, movement, quickness and constant change, and therefore gets bored quickly.
- **TAM-RA-0012** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p43 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Native becomes either a terrorist or a patriot - the risk-taking and willingness to die are identical, only the cause differs.
- **TAM-RA-0013** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p44 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Gives immense willpower and a man fired up with a definite purpose and mission, full of action and dynamism.
- **TAM-RA-0015** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p46 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Of the three Ketu-ruled nakshatras Ashwini is the most dynamic, dashing and quick in action; Rahu fuels this and the native executes courageous acts he never imagined.
- **TAM-RA-0016** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p47 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Great bravery, courage and spirit are born; the subtle desire is to be in action and do the unimaginable.
- **TAM-RA-0019** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p47 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashwini, the Dev-gan nakshatra of Ketu
  **THEN** Brings forth a Rajyogi - a royal ascetic.
- **TAM-RA-0023** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p48 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is the Robinhood of the poor - stands against corrupt rule, risks everything, and jumps first and thinks afterwards.
- **TAM-RA-0024** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p48 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini receiving positive aspects in the birth chart
  **THEN** Native risks everything without thinking and crosses all limits to do the unimaginable.
- **TAM-RA-0032** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p53 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Native likes what is fast, quick and instant; quick to judge and quick to express feelings and emotions.
- **TAM-RA-0033** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p53 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ketu's Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Makes the person markedly more spontaneous, expressing from the heart rather than the mind.
- **TAM-RA-0034** · nak Ashvini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p55 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashwini nakshatra
  **THEN** Native never gives up; however many times he is knocked down he stands up and starts again.
- **TRI-0001** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent in the chart
  **THEN** Spontaneous, impulsive nature; converts impulse to action without delay; quickest of all nakshatras in initiating.
- **TRI-0002** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Rashness and haste that defeats the purpose; hurry causes delay.
- **TRI-0003** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Lively intelligence, quick comprehension, happy-go-lucky disposition, brimming with life.
- **TRI-0006** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Charm, elegance, style and extravagance in mental and physical makeup, from the Ashvini Kumaras.
- **TRI-0007** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Strong spirit of adventure, fearlessness, satisfaction from heroic pursuits.
- **TRI-0008** · nak Ashvini · TRI p12 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent, less evolved native
  **THEN** Foolhardiness; fails to learn from mistakes and repeats them; lack of reflection that does not improve with age.
- **TRI-0009** · nak Ashvini · TRI p13 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Light-hearted jovial humour; able to laugh at self and others.
- **TRI-0011** · nak Ashvini · TRI p13 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Helpful nature with an eye for others' needs, but only in short-term matters or quick bursts; transfers long-term responsibility to others.
- **TRI-0013** · nak Ashvini · TRI p13 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Independent, confident spirit; dislikes being told what to do; stubborn and unyielding, ignores good counsel unless handled with sensitivity mixed with firmness.
- **TRI-0014** · nak Ashvini · TRI p13 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Dresses well, spends considerable time on wardrobe; direct and straightforward, values the same in others.
- **TRI-0016** · nak Ashvini · TRI p14 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Feels unique and specially gifted; pioneering approach; first to catch new trends.
- **TRI-0017** · nak Ashvini · TRI p14 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Moderate eater, modest in any circumstance, not a show-off, does not gloat over accomplishments.
- **TRI-0022** · nak Ashvini · TRI p15 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ashvini — male nakshatra
  **THEN** Projects archetypal masculine drive, ambition, logic and initiative; connected mainly to Sun and Mars.
- **TRI-0026** · nak Ashvini, pada 1 · TRI p16 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet or lagna in Ashvini pada 1 (0°00'-3°20' Aries, Aries navamsa, Mars)
  **THEN** Most pioneering, courageous and physically active part; full of initiative, abundant energy and drive.
- **TRI-0027** · nak Ashvini, pada 2 · TRI p16 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet or lagna in Ashvini pada 2 (3°20'-6°40' Aries, Taurus navamsa, Venus)
  **THEN** Practical and resourceful; splendorous, graceful and indulgent; wants material manifestation of its ideas.
- **TRI-0029** · nak Ashvini, pada 4 · TRI p16 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet or lagna in Ashvini pada 4 (10°00'-13°20' Aries, Cancer navamsa, Moon)
  **THEN** Healing pada; eye for people's needs, in tune with collective consciousness; birthplace of empathy.
- **TRI-0037** · nak Ashvini · TRI p18 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini prominent
  **THEN** Most restless of all nakshatras; happy only in movement, mental and physical.
- **TRI-0045** · nak Ashvini · TRI p19 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashvini creative force uncontrolled
  **THEN** Downfall through attempting near-impossible feats without proper consideration; when it succeeds, the pioneer label follows.
- **TRI-0060** · nak Ashvini, planet Moon · TRI p24 · +/classical
  **IF** Moon in Ashvini (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Good appearance, manners and intelligence; fond of dressing up and ornaments; skilled in the chosen field.

## Bharani — nature (106)

- **ASH-0024** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · ASH p19 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani (birth nakshatra)
  **THEN** Inclined towards material success, with deep appreciation for beauty, music, art, creativity and money.
- **ASH-0220** · nak Bharani · ASH p91 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Ambitious, gossip-loving, impractical, radical, clever, constrained, fighting for justice, detached, with a sense of unfairness, lustful, indulgent, secretive, aspiring, determined, truthful, mature, transformative and courageous, through a struggleful and death-like life.
- **ASH-0224** · nak Bharani · ASH p95 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent (symbol the yoni, the divine feminine creative power)
  **THEN** Life marked by substantial transformation and personal development; takes obstacles head-on with passion and tenacity.
- **ASH-0226** · nak Bharani · ASH p95 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent (deity Yama, god of justice)
  **THEN** Responsible and just, strongly valuing ethics and justice; an initiator who does not hesitate to make the first move in work, romance or recreation.
- **ASH-0227** · nak Bharani · ASH p95 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Great hardship and barriers in life, present in order to force growth and transformation.
- **ASH-0229** · nak Bharani · ASH p96 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Struggle followed by success - the native must sacrifice to achieve anything big, paying the price directly or indirectly.
- **ASH-0230** · nak Bharani · ASH p96 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Associated with secrets and mysteries; extremism occurs after transition, and any balance created is temporary rather than permanent.
- **ASH-0235** · nak Bharani · ASH p97 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent, native possessing duty, strength, heroism, bluntness, gallantry and discipline
  **THEN** Bharani's energy functions fast and actively, giving extraordinary power to face adverse situations, defeat opponents and show bravery.
- **ASH-0236** · nak Bharani · ASH p97 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani energy without proper control and guidance
  **THEN** Becomes detrimental to others.
- **ASH-0240** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · ASH p98 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 1 (Leo navamsha)
  **THEN** Balance between wrong and right with authoritative tendencies and inventiveness; high ego, intelligence and a rigid stand on his opinion, overflowing with creative and imaginative traits.
- **ASH-0242** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · ASH p98 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 2 (Virgo navamsha, Mercury-ruled)
  **THEN** Knowledge, skill and communication turn into argument, chaos and pomposity with a need to win every time; overindulgence deteriorates energy, but a strong sense of duty is favourable.
- **ASH-0244** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · ASH p98 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 2
  **THEN** Exceptionally generous and selfless, putting others' needs and prosperity before their own, focused and lucid in whatever they do.
- **ASH-0251** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · ASH p99 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 4
  **THEN** Intensely energised, self-assured and productive, with a cunning temperament; great motivation for investigation and testing may lead to a discovery of remarkable value.
- **ASH-0252** · nak Bharani, house 1 · ASH p100 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Activities are suspicious or hard to understand because they are underground, psychic or political; very materialistic, romantic, luxury-seeking and sexual, with a mysterious, perplexed appearance.
- **ASH-0253** · nak Bharani, house 1 · ASH p100 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani ascendant
  **THEN** Experiences the better side of life only after a death-like situation or deep transformative event, after which life comes on track and materialistic tendencies become prominent.
- **ASH-0256** · nak Bharani, planet Sun · ASH p100 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Bharani, female native
  **THEN** Auspicious - she can express in a dominating, aggressive or overreactive way in public and private life.
- **ASH-0257** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · ASH p101 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani
  **THEN** Mentally strong and aware of his actions; feels emotions with great intensity, with strong attachments and desires, and faces unexpected events others cannot predict.
- **ASH-0263** · nak Bharani, planet Mars · ASH p102 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Bharani
  **THEN** Strong, healthy, determined, courageous and persistent, a natural leader - but more prone to rage and impulsiveness, requiring anger management.
- **ASH-0270** · nak Bharani, planet Jupiter · ASH p104 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Bharani
  **THEN** Tendency to be extremely dogmatic or rigid in opinion.
- **ASH-0272** · nak Bharani, planet Venus · ASH p104 · ~/stated
  **IF** Venus in Bharani, female native
  **THEN** Her behaviour may appear masculine in action and approach, while she remains very sensitive about style, look, beauty and attire.
- **ASH-0273** · nak Bharani, planet Venus · ASH p104 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Bharani
  **THEN** Keen indulgence in material and physical objects, with a higher chance of downgrading himself to obtain favours from others.
- **ASH-0276** · nak Bharani, planet Saturn · ASH p104 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Bharani
  **THEN** Addiction, intoxication, adultery or drug use may become a cause of self-destruction.
- **ASH-0278** · nak Bharani, planet Rahu · ASH p105 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Bharani
  **THEN** Amplifies Venus's significations - esoteric knowledge, sex, luxury and beauty become an obsession, with attraction to hidden things, secrets and occult practice.
- **AVA-2-0285** · nak Bharani · AVA-2 p39 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani placement anywhere
  **THEN** Apabharani shakti - the power to take things away and carry them to their appointed end; the native holds heavy loads without breaking, endures what others cannot and is drawn to the extremes of creation and destruction alike. The governing word is bearing.
- **AVA-2-0286** · nak Bharani · AVA-2 p39 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani at its best versus under strain
  **THEN** At its best the midwife and the moral force that sees a thing through from birth to death; under strain it grasps, indulges and refuses to let go long past the point of release.
- **AVA-2-0290** · nak Bharani · AVA-2 p40 · -/stated
  **IF** A planet in Bharani lacking dignity, aspect or conduct
  **THEN** The same intensity that creates can consume; the same magnetism that attracts can ruin. Bharani intensifies a placement; it never redeems a broken one.
- **AVA-2-0291** · nak Bharani · AVA-2 p40 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Bharani compared
  **THEN** The first asserts, the second serves, the third relates, the fourth transforms.
- **AVA-2-0292** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · AVA-2 p40 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 1, 13°20'-16°40', Leo navamsha, Sun
  **THEN** Creativity and self-expression burn at full strength; immersed in itself and can offend without intending to; inner drive and ambition enormous and where the chart supplies maturity all of it turns to constructive achievement.
- **AVA-2-0293** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · AVA-2 p41 · ~/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 1
  **THEN** Sacrificial yet short-tempered; fearless, broad-minded, persevering; discriminating and able to defeat opponents; pride and conceit grow from the strong self-focus and the temperament runs moody.
- **AVA-2-0300** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · AVA-2 p41 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 1 unrefined
  **THEN** Ego, self-centredness, temper and conflict with family tradition.
- **AVA-2-0301** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · AVA-2 p41 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 2, 16°40'-20°00', Virgo navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** Service, discipline and practical organisation take over; planets here work altruistically and the native becomes practical, useful, detail-conscious and genuinely capable of working for others.
- **AVA-2-0302** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · AVA-2 p41 · ~/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 2
  **THEN** Soft-natured, talkative, clever, intelligent; religious, honourable and charitable, often carrying scriptural knowledge; wealth and happiness indicated - yet laziness sits beside the intelligence, with strong attachment to pleasure and comfort.
- **AVA-2-0306** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · AVA-2 p42 · -/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 2 (karmic clue)
  **THEN** Fondness for liquor and women; harshness towards guests, monks or spiritual visitors is warned against.
- **AVA-2-0309** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · AVA-2 p42 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 2 unrefined
  **THEN** Laziness, sensual attachment, harsh conduct towards those owed hospitality, and indulgence.
- **AVA-2-0311** · nak Bharani, pada 3 · AVA-2 p42 · ~/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 3
  **THEN** Active, quick, prompt in response; wise yet conceited; cheerful with strong stamina; hard-hearted under affliction and capable of inflicting pain; composed on the surface, sharp when provoked; dissatisfaction with family lingers.
- **AVA-2-0318** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · AVA-2 p43 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 4, 23°20'-26°40', Scorpio navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** The most intense quarter: planets work in an uninhibited, primal, instinctive manner. Properly channelled it is wildly inventive and original; mishandled it turns self-destructive, aggressive or morally unstable.
- **AVA-2-0319** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · AVA-2 p43 · ~/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 4
  **THEN** Courageous, proud, obstinate, aggressive, short-tempered and physically strong, yet a good orator and a good friend; under affliction friendships turn to poor company, vices take hold and irreverent deeds follow.
- **AVA-2-0326** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · AVA-2 p43 · -/classical
  **IF** Bharani pada 4 afflicted
  **THEN** Association with wicked people brings downfall; cruelty, pride, vices, bad company, disease and self-destruction.
- **AVA-2-0329** · nak Bharani, planet Sun · AVA-2 p44 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Bharani aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Cruelty and aggression, with interest in martial arts or fighting skill; red eyes and a strong physique.
- **AVA-2-0358** · nak Bharani, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p46 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani pada 4 afflicted
  **THEN** Questionable character and wicked tendencies.
- **AVA-2-0379** · nak Bharani, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p47 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Bharani aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Cruelty, moral fall and quarrels.
- **AVA-2-0389** · nak Bharani, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p47 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Bharani aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Respect for elders, fame, acceptance of good advice, a peace-loving nature and good deeds.
- **AVA-2-0391** · nak Bharani, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p47 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Bharani aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Bereft of truth despite good speech; drawn to quarrels, loving several women at once, showy.
- **AVA-2-0405** · nak Bharani, planet Venus · AVA-2 p48 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Bharani aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Obstacle after obstacle - life cannot be enjoyed as desired.
- **AVA-2-0417** · nak Bharani, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p49 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Bharani aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Unkind even to those who feed him; obstacles at every step; moral weakness.
- **AVA-2-0418** · nak Bharani, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p49 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Bharani aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** A liar or thief who cannot enjoy good company.
- **AVA-2-0445** · nak Bharani, house 1 · AVA-2 p51 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Bharani
  **THEN** Intense, passionate, magnetic, courageous; identity forged through desire, struggle and repeated rebirth-like phases; expressive eyes, commanding aura, calm outside and storm within; strong-willed, blunt, refusing control, with a fierce survival instinct and a pull towards occult, psychology and transformation work.
- **AVA-2-0446** · nak Bharani, house 1 · AVA-2 p51 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord in Bharani, afflicted
  **THEN** Ego clashes, sexual excess, scandal and a sudden fall after a sudden rise; refined, it gives charisma, endurance and the ability to guide others through what the native has already survived.
- **AVA-2-0452** · nak Bharani, house 3 · AVA-2 p51 · -/stated
  **IF** Third lord in Bharani, afflicted
  **THEN** Courage turns to recklessness; speech blunt or offensive without intending it.
- **AVA-2-0455** · nak Bharani, house 4 · AVA-2 p52 · ~/stated
  **IF** Fourth lord in Bharani
  **THEN** The surface is calm and the depths volcanic; peace never comes from property or control - it comes when the maternal and ancestral patterns are healed.
- **AVA-2-0482** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · AVA-2 p56 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani
  **THEN** The mind becomes intense, instinctive and desire-driven, feeling everything deeply and swinging between extremes; the subconscious carries impressions of birth, family, sexuality and survival, with a deep need for emotional security and a hidden fear of loss.
- **AVA-2-0484** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · AVA-2 p56 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon afflicted in Bharani
  **THEN** Cruel speech, instability, dependency, fear of poison, fire or weapons and relationship complications.
- **AVA-2-0492** · nak Bharani, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p57 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury afflicted in Bharani
  **THEN** Quarrelsome speech, cruel thinking, manipulation, too many activities without result and a poor bond with the father.
- **AVA-2-0496** · nak Bharani, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p57 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter afflicted in Bharani
  **THEN** Wasted money, quarrels, show, multiple relationships, wrong advice and exploitation.
- **AVA-2-0509** · nak Bharani, house 1 · AVA-2 p60 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Bharani
  **THEN** Identity is carried rather than worn lightly - the native holds more than others and the presence is magnetic and slightly unreadable; personal chapters end and begin decisively and dashas touching this degree remake identity rather than adjust it. The lesson is restraint.
- **NAT-0078** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · NAT p94 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani (13°20'-26°40' Aries)
  **THEN** Placed at the threshold of birth and death; compelled to confront impermanence and the karmic cycle of action and consequence.
- **NAT-0079** · nak Bharani · NAT p94 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani ruled by Yama, god of death and dharma
  **THEN** Forces a reckoning with truth and endurance of hardship; severe (krura) in energy, yet every ending carries the seed of a new beginning.
- **NAT-0081** · nak Bharani · NAT p94 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani's process of creation
  **THEN** Requires surrender, endurance and a painful passage through thresholds; only through dissolution can something new take form.
- **NAT-0082** · nak Bharani · NAT p95 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani's Yama myth
  **THEN** Karmic accountability, resilience under early hardship and injustice, and the transformational power of holding to truth.
- **NAT-0083** · nak Bharani · NAT p96 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani's Savitri myth
  **THEN** Unwavering faith; ability to transcend even the finality of death through resilience, devotion and clever wisdom.
- **NAT-0084** · nak Bharani · NAT p97 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani's yoni as vessel and womb
  **THEN** Governs the period of containment and gestation before manifestation; what is brought into existence must be nurtured, sustained and eventually released.
- **NAT-0085** · nak Bharani · NAT p97 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent (archetype of the Enduring Bearer)
  **THEN** Formidable seen and unseen strength; carries karmic responsibility and emotional burdens others would avoid, with a deep calling to protect, hold and guide.
- **NAT-0086** · nak Bharani · NAT p98 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani native given burdens not of their own choosing
  **THEN** Internal tension between commitment and liberation; may abruptly cast off all burdens and choose autonomy over duty.
- **NAT-0087** · nak Bharani · NAT p98 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani's endurance carried past its purpose
  **THEN** Clings to a struggle long after its use, mistaking suffering for strength.
- **NAT-0088** · nak Bharani · NAT p98 · -/stated
  **IF** Something or someone resists a necessary transformation under Bharani
  **THEN** Bharani's protective force becomes uncompromising and forces the birth; can show as ruthless decisiveness, severing ties and relationships instantly with no second chances.
- **NAT-0089** · nak Bharani · NAT p99 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani's protective nature taken to extremes
  **THEN** Rigid, unyielding and intolerant of perceived weakness.
- **NAT-0090** · nak Bharani · NAT p99 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani facing release
  **THEN** Holds on to roles, struggles and relationships far past their time, fearing what comes after the letting go.
- **NAT-0106** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · NAT p104 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 1 (13°20'-16°40' Aries), Leo navamsa, Sun-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Bold creative leadership and decisive action with strong purpose; risk of unchecked pride and impulsiveness overshadowing integrity.
- **NAT-0107** · nak Bharani, pada 2 · NAT p105 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 2 (16°40'-20°00' Aries), Virgo navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Raw potential turned into disciplined action; determined builders and problem-solvers with practical growth and material success, prone to overextension and excessive criticism.
- **NAT-0108** · nak Bharani, pada 3 · NAT p106 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 3 (20°00'-23°20' Aries), Libra navamsa, Venus-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Creative expression, relational harmony and mediation; strong social presence and fulfilment through partnership, diplomacy or art, with risk of overindulgence and dependence on external validation.
- **NAT-0109** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · NAT p106 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani pada 4 (23°20'-26°40' Aries), Scorpio navamsa, Mars-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Intense karmic depth: emotional resilience, secrecy, courage to face trials, resolution of ancestral karmic burdens; internal struggle if transformation is resisted.
- **NAT-0112** · nak Bharani · NAT p108 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani's tamasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Emotional stagnation, denial and resistance to letting go.
- **NAT-0909** · nak Bharani, planet Mars · NAT p557 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Bharani
  **THEN** Transformational force and strategic action; intense drive merging material ambition with the necessity of inner evolution.
- **RBS-1-1130** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p165 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Bharani, which rules both birth and death
  **THEN** The native lives in extreme thoughts, sometimes behaving like a newborn insensitive to the world and at other times fearful and anxious; great effort is needed to centre thought and action.
- **RBS-1-1131** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p165 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Bharani
  **THEN** A unique capacity to analyse every extreme possibility of an action, so that it is very difficult to argue with them; highly secretive; with age they learn balance instead of extremes.
- **RBS-1-1135** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p165 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Bharani
  **THEN** He harms nobody and flatters nobody, faces much resistance and failure, spoils relations even with near ones, but forgets enmity the moment the opponent comes with folded hands; his arrogance and any subordination are like death to him and lead him into trouble.
- **RBS-1-1136** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p165 · +/stated
  **IF** Female born in Bharani
  **THEN** A clean, admirable and modest character, respects parents and elders, bold, impulsive and over-optimistic.
- **RBS-1-1147** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p166 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Bharani — positive side
  **THEN** Poise, calmness, multiplicity of interests, sincerity and dutifulness towards liabilities.
- **RBS-1-1148** · nak Bharani · RBS-1 p166 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Bharani — negative side
  **THEN** Indulgence in material and physical pleasure, vulnerability to luxury and extravagance, inability to bear domination or authority, and disregard for the sentiments of others.
- **RBS-2-0549** · nak Bharani, planet Venus · RBS-2 p141 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Bharani Nakshatra, ruled by Venus, symbol the yoni
  **THEN** Loves the good things of life and is sensation-driven; creative and artistic, admiring beauty and art; pleasant, fun-loving, warm, goal-oriented and an eternal seeker.
- **RBS-2-0962** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p261 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani, a Manushya star of dreadful nature symbolised by the womb
  **THEN** The star of restraint; it hides or eclipses things and carries the ideas of struggle and suffering, indicating one entangled in the material world.
- **RBS-2-0963** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p261 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani on the ascendant
  **THEN** A fickle-minded person addicted to the opposite sex, proud and courageous with good longevity, fond of kinsmen, helpful to friends, and usually having few children.
- **RBS-2-0964** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p261 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani native in company
  **THEN** A vital character easily led by others, with questionable moral behaviour, and troubled by the jealousy of others.
- **RBS-2-0966** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p262 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani ruling both the beginning and the end of life
  **THEN** The native lives in extreme thoughts, at times insensitive as a newborn and at times deeply fearful; analyses every extreme possibility so that argument with him is difficult, is highly secretive, and learns balance only with age.
- **RBS-2-0969** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p262 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Bharani Nakshatra
  **THEN** A stern disciplinarian, ruthless, truthful and law-abiding, intelligent, healthy, very courageous, long-lived and creative in painting and photography; dislikes being controlled or manipulated.
- **RBS-2-0972** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p263 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Bharani Nakshatra
  **THEN** Poise, calmness, multiplicity of interests, sincerity and dutifulness toward liabilities.
- **RBS-2-0973** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p263 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Bharani Nakshatra
  **THEN** Indulgence in material and physical pleasure, vulnerability to luxury and extravagance, inability to bear any domination or authority, and disregard for the sentiments of others.
- **RBS-2-0976** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p263 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Bharani
  **THEN** Does not harm or flatter anybody, faces much resistance and failure, spoils relations even with the nearest, but forgets enmity when the opponent comes with folded hands; his arrogance and insubordination lead him into trouble.
- **RBS-2-0977** · nak Bharani · RBS-2 p263 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Bharani
  **THEN** Clean, admirable and modest, respecting parents and elders; bold, impulsive and over-optimistic.
- **RBS-2-0994** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · RBS-2 p264 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani pada 1 (Aries 13°20' to 16°40')
  **THEN** Strong temper, reddish hair, able to defeat enemies, broad-minded, valorous and persevering; good education, administration and creativity, possibly connected with astrology, and suited to judge, magistrate, spiritual teacher or nursery teacher; elephant-like head and rough body hair.
- **RBS-2-0997** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · RBS-2 p265 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Bharani pada 4 (Aries 23°20' to 26°40')
  **THEN** Courageous, cruel, proud and obstinate, keeping bad company with few good qualities; explosive energy which if directed well makes a good explorer, researcher, gynaecologist, photographer, pathologist or detective; brown body hair and a monkey-like face, very talkative, cruel and untruthful.
- **TAM-RA-0058** · nak Bharani, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p66 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Bharani for natives born on Gemini, Aquarius or Scorpio ascendant
  **THEN** An excellent placement.
- **TRI-0062** · nak Bharani · TRI p26 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Carries restraint, caution, jealousy, secrecy, forbearance, struggle, sacrifice, catharsis, sexuality, nurturing and maternal love.
- **TRI-0063** · nak Bharani · TRI p26 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Unpredictable; matters brew on subtle planes and erupt suddenly; operates in hidden ways and revels in secrecy.
- **TRI-0065** · nak Bharani · TRI p27 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani ruled also by Kali
  **THEN** A battlefield between opposing qualities of nature; distinction between right and wrong is made here and the negative is uprooted; the most extreme of nakshatras.
- **TRI-0066** · nak Bharani · TRI p27 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Extreme nature; swings between polar opposites — puritanism and bohemia, naivete and wisdom, maturity and immaturity, life and death.
- **TRI-0072** · nak Bharani · TRI p28 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Desire to experience surroundings to the fullest through instinct rather than reason; feelings so overwhelming they cannot be restrained.
- **TRI-0073** · nak Bharani · TRI p28 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent, less evolved native
  **THEN** Moth-like hopping from one attraction to the next; jumps into anything without caution unless given guidance.
- **TRI-0074** · nak Bharani · TRI p28 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent, evolved native
  **THEN** Channels immense primeval energy into positive, wholesome universal pursuits.
- **TRI-0075** · nak Bharani · TRI p28 · +/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Strong character able to withstand life's turbulences; nothing holds the native down for long.
- **TRI-0081** · nak Bharani · TRI p29 · ~/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent
  **THEN** Able to hold a calm countenance while inwardly in raging torment.
- **TRI-0082** · nak Bharani · TRI p29 · -/stated
  **IF** Bharani prominent, negatively disposed
  **THEN** A childlike cruelty and morbidity arising from lack of responsibility for one's own actions; moves from act to act without regard for long-term implications.
- **TRI-0086** · nak Bharani · TRI p30 · -/classical
  **IF** Bharani — mleccha (outcaste) caste
  **THEN** Almost always breaks social norms or taboos; highly explosive energy that cannot be contained within societal structure.
- **TRI-0091** · nak Bharani, pada 1 · TRI p31 · ~/stated
  **IF** Planet in Bharani pada 1 (13°20'-16°40' Aries, Leo navamsa, Sun)
  **THEN** Creativity and self-immersion; extremely self-centric, offends others without meaning to; strong drive and will-power. Sun, Mars and Jupiter especially strong.
- **TRI-0094** · nak Bharani, pada 4 · TRI p31 · ~/stated
  **IF** Planet in Bharani pada 4 (23°20'-26°40' Aries, Aries navamsa, Mars)
  **THEN** Extreme in every sense; planets act in uninhibited primeval fashion; highly inventive if channelled. Sun, Mars and Ketu strong but not always beneficial; only Jupiter and Venus use it non-self-destructively.
- **TRI-0118** · nak Bharani, planet Moon · TRI p38 · +/classical
  **IF** Moon in Bharani (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Resolute, resourceful, honest, healthy and happy.

## Krittika — nature (111)

- **ASH-0289** · nak Krittika · ASH p108 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent
  **THEN** Pleasant, popular, intelligent, nurturing, enthusiastic, sincere, brave, a go-getter, motivator, initiator and leader who establishes justice - but also a cheater, miserly, untrustworthy, given to impersonation and illicit affairs, and worried.
- **ASH-0294** · nak Krittika · ASH p111 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent (symbol a sharp cutting tool, deity Agni)
  **THEN** Fiery, aggressive presentation and destructive energy requiring specific skill and precaution to be used productively; it eliminates the irrelevant to reveal the appropriate.
- **ASH-0295** · nak Krittika · ASH p111 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika energy where the intention and motive are destructive
  **THEN** The energy is used negatively and destroys.
- **ASH-0300** · nak Krittika, sign Taurus · ASH p112 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika falling in Taurus
  **THEN** Its energy is easy to handle - graceful, delightful, soft, refined and entertaining, adding creativity, beauty, elegance and luxury.
- **ASH-0301** · nak Krittika, sign Aries · ASH p112 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika falling in Aries
  **THEN** Needs proper guidance and restriction; uncontrolled it turns somewhat demonic through its rakshasa quality.
- **ASH-0302** · nak Krittika · ASH p112 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent
  **THEN** Very desirous of objectives requiring intense focus, but lacks the control and sense to enjoy the true satisfaction the goals bring.
- **ASH-0303** · nak Krittika, planet Venus · ASH p112 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Krittika
  **THEN** Creates suffocation, forcing the native to command and behave aggressively; Krittika's energy needs directed activism or it becomes destructive to the native.
- **ASH-0304** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · ASH p112 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika
  **THEN** Best suited to Krittika's active energy, providing a rhythmic foundation to act aggressively and attentively.
- **ASH-0309** · nak Krittika · ASH p113 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent
  **THEN** Powerful mind, self-assurance and tenacity bring great success and fortune; courageous in spiritual and social contribution and admired for a maternal, compassionate character.
- **ASH-0310** · nak Krittika · ASH p113 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent, when contradicted
  **THEN** Loud outbursts, easily upset and obstinate, challenging to keep wrath under control, lashing out at whoever disagrees.
- **ASH-0311** · nak Krittika · ASH p113 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent, after success
  **THEN** Must avoid becoming overly ambitious and greedy, or trouble follows.
- **ASH-0314** · nak Krittika · ASH p114 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent, female native
  **THEN** Delicate with a complex inner life; must control her emotions or others will exploit her; deeply caring but unable to bear the pressure of going beyond, and arrogant in personality.
- **ASH-0316** · nak Krittika, pada 1 · ASH p114 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 1
  **THEN** Extremely clever with great willpower, courage, endurance and strength; powerful within both family and social groups and hard to influence, but aggression is high and must be controlled.
- **ASH-0324** · nak Krittika, pada 3 · ASH p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 3
  **THEN** Liberal, affectionate and compassionate to all living things, naturally charitable and given to social service, but easily offended; may appear lazy through attachment to a luxurious lifestyle.
- **ASH-0325** · nak Krittika, pada 4 · ASH p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 4 (Taurus, Pisces pushkara navamsha)
  **THEN** Emotional and sensitive nature with an unstable but creative mind and a strong sense of freedom or liberation.
- **ASH-0329** · nak Krittika, house 1 · ASH p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Very critical and dominating personality with creative intelligence; fights for betterment by removing unnecessary drama, taking bold steps to remove impurity with full confidence.
- **ASH-0334** · nak Krittika, planet Sun · ASH p118 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Krittika
  **THEN** Vivacious, passionate and exuberant, brave and bold, honest, straightforward and principled, a trailblazer who inspires others and shapes trends.
- **ASH-0335** · nak Krittika, planet Moon, sign Aries · ASH p118 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika falling in Aries
  **THEN** Mentality inclined to courageous action and ambition, with passion and aggression.
- **ASH-0337** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · ASH p119 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika
  **THEN** Powerful emotions freely expressed, strong resolve and focus, courageous and fearless - but emotional ferocity brings irritability, impatience and impetuous decisions.
- **ASH-0340** · nak Krittika, planet Mars · ASH p120 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Krittika
  **THEN** Highly aggressive and competitive with strong physical and mental energy, proactive leadership and bravery - but easily irritated and impatient when obstructed.
- **ASH-0345** · nak Krittika, planet Mercury · ASH p121 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Krittika
  **THEN** Swift decision-making without overthinking, direct and intolerant of trickery or manipulation; a sceptic who challenges accepted wisdom.
- **ASH-0357** · nak Krittika, planet Rahu, sign Aries · ASH p124 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the Aries padas of Krittika
  **THEN** Very angry, quarrelsome, clever, egoistic and manipulative, getting things by hook or crook; if the energy is not used positively it creates havoc in life.
- **ASH-0359** · nak Krittika, planet Rahu · ASH p125 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Krittika
  **THEN** Rebellious streak defying authority, audacious risk-taking and fixation on particular objectives, with impatience and impulsivity to be managed.
- **AVA-2-0553** · nak Krittika · AVA-2 p66 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika placement anywhere
  **THEN** Dahana shakti, the power to burn - it separates the real from the false, cuts away what is rotten and then nourishes what survives the cut; honest to the point of sharpness, protective, ambitious, quick to criticise and slow to forgive a lie. The governing word is purification.
- **AVA-2-0554** · nak Krittika · AVA-2 p66 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika under affliction
  **THEN** It scorches - cruel with the tongue, impatient, burning the very things it means to protect.
- **AVA-2-0558** · nak Krittika · AVA-2 p67 · -/stated
  **IF** A planet in Krittika without dignity or good aspect
  **THEN** The same edge that discerns wounds and the same fire that warms scorches; Krittika sharpens a placement, it never repairs a broken one.
- **AVA-2-0559** · nak Krittika · AVA-2 p67 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Krittika compared
  **THEN** The first explores, the second provides, the third serves society, the fourth creates with others; the first blazes in Aries, the last three settle into Taurus and turn the fire into warmth.
- **AVA-2-0560** · nak Krittika, pada 1 · AVA-2 p67 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 1, 26°40'-30°00' Aries, Sagittarius navamsha, Jupiter
  **THEN** The explorer's quarter: highly moral, generous and altruistic, philosophical and principled, with a daredevil streak beside the principles - bold, adventurous, willing to take real risk. Planets here receive strong willpower, stamina and both physical and mental strength.
- **AVA-2-0567** · nak Krittika, pada 1 · AVA-2 p68 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 1 unrefined
  **THEN** Temper, moral rigidity and risk taken because it is righteous rather than because it is wise.
- **AVA-2-0568** · nak Krittika, pada 2 · AVA-2 p68 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 2, 00°00'-03°20' Taurus, Capricorn navamsha, Saturn
  **THEN** Ethics remain but expression turns material - spirituality lived through practical action; this quarter creates, organises and structures, nurtures, provides and sustains others and is at its best in a crisis. The maternal, caregiving face of Krittika is strongest here.
- **AVA-2-0569** · nak Krittika, pada 2 · AVA-2 p68 · ~/classical
  **IF** Krittika pada 2
  **THEN** Carries spiritual knowledge with weak self-control; short-tempered and reactive; may show irreverence towards scripture and spiritual authority, dislikes rigid religious systems and tends to provoke others into action.
- **AVA-2-0576** · nak Krittika, pada 3 · AVA-2 p69 · +/classical
  **IF** Krittika pada 3, 03°20'-06°40' Taurus, Aquarius navamsha, Saturn
  **THEN** The humanitarian quarter - altruistic and socially minded, joining ancient wisdom to futuristic thinking; collective duty and contribution weigh heavily and the tenth-house themes of karma, profession and public role run strong. The classics call it the university of the zodiac: it promotes every form of learning.
- **AVA-2-0577** · nak Krittika, pada 3 · AVA-2 p69 · -/classical
  **IF** Krittika pada 3
  **THEN** Brave yet proud and indiscreet; short-tempered and impulsive; grief, emotional instability and mood swings recur, with spells of dullness or unclear thinking and a turn towards disturbing thought patterns.
- **AVA-2-0583** · nak Krittika, pada 3 · AVA-2 p69 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 3 unrefined
  **THEN** Instability of mood, pride, indiscretion and ties with questionable company or livelihood.
- **AVA-2-0584** · nak Krittika, pada 4 · AVA-2 p70 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 4, 06°40'-10°00' Taurus, Pisces navamsha, Jupiter, Pushkara
  **THEN** Deep feeling turned into practical form through group work, collaboration and collective creativity; the approach to others is joyful, kind and compassionate and the native thrives in a team. As a Pushkara navamsha it is highly auspicious and grants creative ability alongside emotional intelligence.
- **AVA-2-0586** · nak Krittika, pada 4 · AVA-2 p70 · -/classical
  **IF** Krittika pada 4
  **THEN** Melancholy, introspective and sensitive, worried and mentally burdened at times, with a pensive, overthinking nature.
- **AVA-2-0612** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · AVA-2 p72 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Intelligent, kind and ready to help the needy.
- **AVA-2-0653** · nak Krittika, pada 3, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p74 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Krittika pada 3
  **THEN** Pragmatic and firm; enjoys power, with a beautiful appearance.
- **AVA-2-0659** · nak Krittika, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p74 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Krittika aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Truthful, respectful, kind-hearted and blessed with good luck.
- **AVA-2-0670** · nak Krittika, planet Venus · AVA-2 p75 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Krittika aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Well-behaved, with a magnificent personality.
- **AVA-2-0683** · nak Krittika, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p75 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Krittika aspected or conjunct the Sun
  **THEN** A tactful speaker - peace-loving, calm and dependent on others for food.
- **AVA-2-0685** · nak Krittika, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p75 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Krittika aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Talkative and generally happy in life.
- **AVA-2-0709** · nak Krittika, house 1 · AVA-2 p77 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Krittika
  **THEN** Self-awareness and strength of personality come early - the native looks mature, independent and dominant from childhood and is taken for the elder in any room; communication is blunt and the honesty costs as much as it earns. Identity is refined in stages, so life carries several visible transformations of the self.
- **AVA-2-0740** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · AVA-2 p82 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika
  **THEN** The mind is emotionally intense yet protective and affection takes the form of security, food, care and practical support rather than sentiment - this native feeds people instead of reassuring them; emotional maturity comes early, often too early.
- **AVA-2-0743** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · AVA-2 p83 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon afflicted in Krittika
  **THEN** Emotional rigidity, impatience, criticism levelled at those closest and real difficulty showing vulnerability.
- **AVA-2-0749** · nak Krittika, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p83 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury afflicted in Krittika
  **THEN** Sarcasm, criticism as a habit, argument for its own sake and a reputation for being right at other people's expense.
- **AVA-2-0758** · nak Krittika, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p84 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn afflicted in Krittika
  **THEN** Pessimism, rigidity, loneliness and a severity turned first on the self and then on everyone else.
- **AVA-2-0763** · nak Krittika, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p85 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu afflicted in Krittika
  **THEN** Isolation, chronic dissatisfaction, harsh detachment and alienation from people who would have helped.
- **AVA-2-0764** · nak Krittika, house 1 · AVA-2 p86 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Krittika
  **THEN** The native cuts a clear outline early - people know exactly who they are dealing with and quickly; the presence is bright and a little severe, the gaze direct. Identity is refined in stages and transits over this degree mark those cuts. The lesson is warmth.
- **NAT-0116** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · NAT p111 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Krittika (26°40' Aries - 10°00' Taurus)
  **THEN** Power of separation and refinement; cuts away the unnecessary to reveal the essential, forging raw potential into form.
- **NAT-0117** · nak Krittika · NAT p111 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika as the third of the Ashvini-Bharani-Krittika triad
  **THEN** The decisive act of separation, the cutting of the cord; naksatra of independence and self-realisation.
- **NAT-0119** · nak Krittika · NAT p113 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika native, per the myth of the six wives
  **THEN** Navigates profound challenges and false accusation that shape character and awaken inner strength; rises above limitation through perseverance.
- **NAT-0120** · nak Krittika · NAT p114 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika's dahana sakti (power to burn and purify)
  **THEN** Insatiable hunger — not for food alone but for experience, self-discovery and achievement; urges facing challenges head on.
- **NAT-0121** · nak Krittika · NAT p115 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika's fire and blade untempered by care and mindfulness
  **THEN** Its relentless drive to strip away illusion causes harm; intensity must be balanced with wisdom.
- **NAT-0122** · nak Krittika · NAT p115 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent (archetype of Keeper of the Sacred Flame)
  **THEN** Nurtures through trial rather than comfort; keen discernment and unshakable will that separates the essential from the excess.
- **NAT-0123** · nak Krittika · NAT p116 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika becoming too rigid and ruthless in its pursuit of truth
  **THEN** Cuts too deeply, leaving scorched earth; sharpness turns to cruelty, harsh judgement, isolation and alienation of those seeking guidance.
- **NAT-0124** · nak Krittika · NAT p116 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika native doubting their own strength and failing to act
  **THEN** Hesitation lets illusion and stagnation take over; complacency, loss of clarity, weakened resolve and a sense of purposelessness.
- **NAT-0125** · nak Krittika · NAT p116 · -/stated
  **IF** Krittika's fire misused for recognition, driven by ego or insecurity
  **THEN** Power used to dominate rather than guide; hollow authority built on control, demanding obedience instead of nurturing through discipline.
- **NAT-0128** · nak Krittika · NAT p118 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika's demand of self-reliance
  **THEN** Stands firm in its own truth without needing external approval; but self-sufficiency can harden into isolation and clarity into rigidity.
- **NAT-0139** · nak Krittika, pada 1 · NAT p123 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 1 (26°40' Aries - 0°00' Taurus), Sagittarius navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Bold principled action from moral vision; cuts through ambiguity toward higher truth and righteous leadership, with risk of unchecked zeal and burnout.
- **NAT-0140** · nak Krittika, pada 2 · NAT p124 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 2 (0°00'-3°20' Taurus), Capricorn navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Fiery energy disciplined into tangible achievement; patience, resilience, endurance and mastery, though excess focus on structure and material gain can eclipse spiritual purpose.
- **NAT-0141** · nak Krittika, pada 3 · NAT p124 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 3 (3°20'-6°40' Taurus), Aquarius navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Practical grounding joined to intellectual innovation; integrates tradition with progressive ideals and challenges existing structures, but scatters focus if not anchored.
- **NAT-0142** · nak Krittika, pada 4 · NAT p125 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika pada 4 (6°40'-10°00' Taurus), Pisces navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Moksa

- **RBS-1-1168** · nak Krittika · RBS-1 p168 · -/stated

- **RBS-1-1177** · nak Krittika · RBS-1 p169 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-1005** · nak Krittika, planet Saturn · RBS-2 p266 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn posited in Krittika, especially in its first part where it is debilitated

- **RBS-2-1009** · nak Krittika, planet Moon · RBS-2 p267 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-1015** · nak Krittika · RBS-2 p267 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Krittika Nakshatra

- **RBS-2-1016** · nak Krittika · RBS-2 p267 · -/stated

- **RBS-2-1019** · nak Krittika · RBS-2 p267 · ~/stated

- **TRI-0126** · nak Krittika · TRI p42 · ~/stated

- **TRI-0127** · nak Krittika · TRI p42 · ~/stated

- **TRI-0128** · nak Krittika · TRI p42 · ~/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent

- **TRI-0129** · nak Krittika · TRI p43 · +/stated
  **IF** Krittika prominent
  **THEN** Hides a caring, nurturing, maternal side beneath a hard stern exterior; becomes the life-sustaining element of its social circle through warmth, independence and will-power.

## Rohini — nature (82)

- **ASH-0366** · nak Rohini · ASH p127 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Abundance, good looks, creativity, sincerity, attractiveness, productivity, religiousness, sympathy, wealth, passion, magnetism, focus, love of family and good manners - but also jealousy.
- **ASH-0370** · nak Rohini · ASH p129 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent (Moon as lord, Venus as sign lord, both natural benefics)
  **THEN** Supports growth, harmony, creativity and overall wellness in life; works spontaneously and without much effort, enjoying continuous creation.
- **ASH-0371** · nak Rohini, planet Sun · ASH p130 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Rohini
  **THEN** Adds creativity to the nakshatra's motherly energy.
- **ASH-0372** · nak Rohini, planet Ketu · ASH p130 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Rohini
  **THEN** Restricts the creativity of the nakshatra.
- **ASH-0373** · nak Rohini · ASH p130 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent, female native
  **THEN** Moon's energy is more tuned to females - appealing appearance, exquisiteness, sexual orientation, delicacy, attraction and romance.
- **ASH-0374** · nak Rohini · ASH p130 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent, male native
  **THEN** Behaves differently, exhibiting dominance, aggression, courage, activism, logic and speed.
- **ASH-0375** · nak Rohini · ASH p130 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Does not favour fanaticism; supports a balanced life, doing one thing at a time with dedication and avoiding multitasking.
- **ASH-0378** · nak Rohini · ASH p130 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent, freedom or limits challenged
  **THEN** Feels suffocated and becomes inflexible; emotionally attached to the comfort zone and stressed when boundaries constrain.
- **ASH-0388** · nak Rohini · ASH p132 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent, male native
  **THEN** Lacks trust in others, has a short fuse, leads a difficult life and keeps things to himself; logical and reasoned worldview distinguishes him.
- **ASH-0393** · nak Rohini, pada 1 · ASH p133 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 1 (Aries navamsha, Martian energy prominent)
  **THEN** Aggressive, passionate and action-oriented toward material objectives, active, fiery, sporty and competitive, yet outwardly calm, gentle and respectful of others' feelings.
- **ASH-0397** · nak Rohini, pada 2 · ASH p133 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 2
  **THEN** Fond of food, emotional, connected to a large network, highly imaginative and satisfied by material acquisition; but materialistic and atheistic, coming across as unpleasant and cruel to people he dislikes.
- **ASH-0398** · nak Rohini, pada 3 · ASH p134 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 3 (Gemini navamsha, Mercury-ruled)
  **THEN** Childish behaviour maturing late, around age 33; good grasping power, learning capacity, artistic nature and a calculative mind, but impatient, procrastinating, avaricious, impulsive and indecisive.
- **ASH-0403** · nak Rohini, house 1 · ASH p135 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Deep constant desire to create something new; deals with life naturally, supporting every stage of growth, seeking attention, drawn to inner satisfaction and nurturing children.
- **ASH-0412** · nak Rohini, planet Mars · ASH p137 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Rohini
  **THEN** Adds fuel to Rohini's free will - acts aggressively without considering consequences and demands privileged treatment; takes no responsibility for failure.
- **ASH-0416** · nak Rohini, planet Mercury · ASH p138 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Rohini
  **THEN** Childish behaviour, possessive of luxury, comfort or beauty, with tendencies to travel, gossip, flirtation and insecurity.
- **ASH-0424** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · ASH p142 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Rohini
  **THEN** Fanatical about relationships, money or material possessions; in some cases the mind is diverted to intoxication, drug addiction or law-breaking, with high sexual desire and manipulation.
- **AVA-2-0799** · nak Rohini · AVA-2 p92 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini placement anywhere
  **THEN** Rohana shakti, the power to make things rise and grow: everything it touches swells - wealth, beauty, appetite, love, reputation - and that abundance is exactly the difficulty, because what grows here also binds. Magnetic, sensual, artistic and deeply loyal. The governing word is attachment.
- **AVA-2-0800** · nak Rohini · AVA-2 p92 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini under affliction
  **THEN** It grasps, envies and cannot bear to lose.
- **AVA-2-0804** · nak Rohini · AVA-2 p93 · -/stated
  **IF** Ego grows in a Rohini placement
  **THEN** The downfall begins and nature simply replaces the native; abundance here is lent rather than owned and the placement holds only as long as the channel stays open. Rohini enlarges a placement; it never corrects a broken one.
- **AVA-2-0805** · nak Rohini · AVA-2 p93 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Rohini compared
