  **THEN** The first wants, the second disciplines, the third thinks, the fourth understands - and because all four sit inside Taurus, the change between them is one of temperament rather than temperature.
- **AVA-2-0806** · nak Rohini, pada 1 · AVA-2 p93 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 1, 10°00'-13°20', Aries navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** Prosperous but restless: strong desires and passions with a marked pull towards sensuality, indulgence and material gain; material ambition dominates the early decades though a subtle spiritual streak surfaces later. Clever and tactful, quick to read a situation and quicker to turn it to advantage.
- **AVA-2-0812** · nak Rohini, pada 2 · AVA-2 p94 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 2, 13°20'-16°40', Taurus navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** Struggle refines this quarter - adversity comes early and real inner strength grows out of it; Venus in its own navamsha steadies Rohini's appetite into taste, so desire becomes discrimination. Truthful and religious, with genuine control over the senses, gentle, cultured and amicable, speech sweet and eloquent.
- **AVA-2-0818** · nak Rohini, pada 3 · AVA-2 p94 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 3, 16°40'-20°00', Gemini navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** Timid yet intelligent - a quarter that produces rather than accumulates; devotion and moral conduct run strong, with charity, righteous action and settled good habits, and the personality stays light-hearted and jovial. Timidity sits oddly beside the talent and holds it back.
- **AVA-2-0823** · nak Rohini, pada 4 · AVA-2 p95 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 4, 20°00'-23°20', Cancer navamsha, Moon
  **THEN** The Moon in its own navamsha in its own nakshatra - Rohini at its most characteristic: truthful, wealthy and intuitive, understanding people at depth, reading minds and anticipating events with real accuracy; life runs harmoniously and comfortably with access to luxury and pleasure.
- **AVA-2-0869** · nak Rohini, pada 3, planet Mars · AVA-2 p98 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars in Rohini pada 3
  **THEN** Courageous and respected by learned people.
- **AVA-2-0871** · nak Rohini, pada 4, planet Mars · AVA-2 p98 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Rohini pada 4
  **THEN** Wealthy but indulgent, drawn to wine and the opposite sex; the mindset stays unstable and pleasure-seeking. Under affliction, illegal activity or smuggling.
- **AVA-2-0884** · nak Rohini, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p99 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Rohini aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Truthful, respectful and charitable.
- **AVA-2-0907** · nak Rohini, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p100 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Rohini aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Talkative, restless and unstable.
- **AVA-2-0908** · nak Rohini, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p100 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Rohini aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Immoral tendencies and bad company.
- **AVA-2-0909** · nak Rohini, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p100 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Rohini aspected or conjunct Jupiter
  **THEN** Kind, service-minded and hardworking.
- **AVA-2-0926** · nak Rohini, house 1 · AVA-2 p102 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Rohini
  **THEN** Personality develops early and the shape it takes holds for life; appearance, dress and attractiveness matter greatly and identity is expressed emotionally rather than argued for. The presence is warm and creative and can influence large numbers without deliberate effort.
- **AVA-2-0958** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · AVA-2 p107 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Rohini
  **THEN** The strongest placement in the zodiac for the Moon: beauty, charm and genuine popularity; emotional, nurturing and attractive, with food, comfort and family running to the root of the personality; affection is expressed by feeding rather than by saying so.
- **AVA-2-0960** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · AVA-2 p107 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon afflicted in Rohini
  **THEN** Attachment, indulgence, emotional dependency and a mind that cannot put anything down.
- **AVA-2-0973** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p108 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu afflicted in Rohini
  **THEN** Addiction, illusion, overindulgence and appetite mistaken for purpose.
- **AVA-2-0975** · nak Rohini, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p109 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu afflicted in Rohini
  **THEN** Isolation, loss, confusion and dependency.
- **NAT-0149** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · NAT p130 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Rohini (10°00'-23°20' Taurus)
  **THEN** Rohana sakti — the power to make things rise and grow; magnetic allure, fertility, creative expansion, abundance, beauty and material comfort.
- **NAT-0150** · nak Rohini · NAT p130 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini governed by Prajapati the Creator
  **THEN** Nurturing spirit of civilisation, sustenance and emotional nourishment; procreative and generative force fostering creativity, continuity and refinement.
- **NAT-0152** · nak Rohini · NAT p131 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini's beauty and abundance, per the Prajapati myth
  **THEN** A sensitive point requiring safeguarding; must hold the balance between attraction and restraint, nurturing and overindulgence, or harmony breaks.
- **NAT-0155** · nak Rohini · NAT p132 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini, the birth naksatra of Sri Krsna
  **THEN** Divine beauty, charm, nurturing leadership and the power to inspire devotion; bridges material success and spiritual growth.
- **NAT-0157** · nak Rohini · NAT p133 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Magnetic and luxuriant energy radiating sensuality, artistic expression and prosperity; one of the most desirable naksatras, evoking admiration and envy.
- **NAT-0159** · nak Rohini · NAT p134 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent (archetype of the Sacred Preserver)
  **THEN** Innate ability to nourish, comfort and create harmony; magnetism, sensuality, emotional warmth, refinement and an eye for beauty, attracting admiration and devotion.
- **NAT-0161** · nak Rohini · NAT p135 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini's need for security outweighing willingness to evolve
  **THEN** Possessiveness, emotional attachment and resistance to change; charm turns to control, expectation and indulgence.
- **NAT-0163** · nak Rohini · NAT p136 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini's attachment to tradition and legacy
  **THEN** Romanticising of the past and nostalgia that breeds dissatisfaction with the present.
- **NAT-0164** · nak Rohini · NAT p136 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini's magnetism becoming a crutch
  **THEN** Dependence on admiration, self-worth tied to approval, neediness and inability to be alone; expectation of constant devotion.
- **NAT-0165** · nak Rohini · NAT p136 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini's love of pleasure and refinement unchecked
  **THEN** Excess and complacency; indulgence replaces purpose and luxury replaces self-reflection.
- **NAT-0166** · nak Rohini · NAT p136 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini's overwhelming sense of duty to others at its extreme
  **THEN** Burnout, resentment and unhealthy dependency, where giving becomes sacrifice and generosity is not reciprocated.
- **NAT-0177** · nak Rohini, pada 1 · NAT p141 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 1 (10°00'-13°20' Taurus), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Fertile energy charged with boldness, passion and pioneering spirit; leadership in nurturing and creative work, with impulsiveness and desire for instant gratification to be tempered.
- **NAT-0180** · nak Rohini, pada 4 · NAT p143 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 4 (20°00'-23°20' Taurus), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Deep emotional sensitivity, intuition and connection to the subconscious; strong tie to ancestral lineage and tradition, with risk of emotional dependency and nostalgia.
- **RBS-1-1186** · nak Rohini · RBS-1 p170 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Rohini
  **THEN** Attractive with beautiful eyes and sharp features; very sensitive to the environment and easily pleased or disturbed by petty things.
- **RBS-1-1189** · nak Rohini · RBS-1 p170 · -/stated
  **IF** Male born in Rohini
  **THEN** A knack for finding fault in others, ruled by his brain, spends everything on present comfort, lacks patience and forgiveness, fails to follow due procedure and falls, and believes others blindly.
- **RBS-1-1190** · nak Rohini · RBS-1 p170 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Rohini
  **THEN** Well behaved and well dressed with pomp and show, but with a weak heart, short tempered and inviting trouble.
- **RBS-1-1198** · nak Rohini · RBS-1 p171 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Rohini — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Temperamental sweetness and gentleness, rarely losing temper, affectionate and persevering; but lacking purpose, stability, imagination, endurance and patience, with struggle, indecision and fickle-mindedness.
- **RBS-2-0554** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · RBS-2 p142 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Rohini Nakshatra, ruled by the Moon, symbol the chariot
  **THEN** Extremely attractive, good-looking and magnetic; deeply emotional, charming and sensitive; truthful, pious, soft-spoken and gentle, endearing through commitment and dedication.
- **RBS-2-1043** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · RBS-2 p270 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Rohini Nakshatra
  **THEN** Truthful, pure and sweet-tongued, of a settled mind and lovely appearance.
- **RBS-2-1046** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p271 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini native pursuing interests
  **THEN** Highly imaginative and fond of the fictional world, restless of mind like a deer and unable to stick to one thing, a lover of art, music, dance, sculpture and photography.
- **RBS-2-1049** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p271 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini native using or neglecting his imaginative faculty
  **THEN** Very successful in life if he uses his imaginative skill, otherwise the talent is wasted; life lacks stability because of his indecisive and variable nature.
- **RBS-2-1053** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p272 · -/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Rohini
  **THEN** A knack of finding fault in others and ruled by his brain, spends everything on present comfort, lacks patience and forgiveness, fails to follow due procedure and so falls, and believes others blindly.
- **RBS-2-1054** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p272 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Rohini
  **THEN** Well behaved and well dressed with much show, but of a very weak heart, short-tempered, and invites trouble.
- **RBS-2-1062** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p272 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Rohini Nakshatra
  **THEN** Temperamental sweetness and gentleness so fine-tuned that fits of temper are rare; a softly affectionate nature incapable of offence or revenge, blended with perseverance.
- **RBS-2-1063** · nak Rohini · RBS-2 p272 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Rohini Nakshatra
  **THEN** Lack of purpose, stability and imagination, lack of endurance and patience, struggle, indecision and fickle-mindedness.
- **RBS-2-1070** · nak Rohini, pada 3 · RBS-2 p273 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Rohini pada 3 (Taurus 16°40' to 20°00')
  **THEN** Strong devotion and praiseworthy habits, charitable, happy and talented at mathematics; resourceful and enjoying the lighter side of life, relaxing with friends especially of the opposite sex; slim, humorous, soft-spoken with beautiful eyes.
- **TAM-RA-0093** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p88 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Rohini, the star of stardom
  **THEN** Native aspires to glamour, attraction and attention directed towards himself.
- **TAM-RA-0096** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p88 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Rohini nakshatra
  **THEN** The placement brings a shallowness into the being.
- **TAM-RA-0098** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p90 · +/inferred
  **IF** Rahu in Rohini, where the Moon is exalted
  **THEN** Rahu too is exalted there, being the north node of the Moon, so Rahu in Rohini often gives positive results.
- **TAM-RA-0103** · nak Rohini · TAM-RA p90 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** Native is too emotional by nature, and therefore has no real power but is weak and submissive.
- **TAM-RA-0107** · nak Rohini · TAM-RA p92 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** Native lacks the direct authority of Anuradha or Pushya but wields great manipulative skill, exercising power indirectly.
- **TAM-RA-0112** · nak Rohini, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p95 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Rohini for natives born on Cancer, Pisces or Sagittarius ascendant
  **THEN** More beneficial than for other ascendants.
- **TRI-0166** · nak Rohini · TRI p54 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Steadiness, fixity and the ability to romp over whatever comes in its way, after the ox.
- **TRI-0167** · nak Rohini · TRI p54 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Source of the Taurean traits — earthiness, stability, creativity, productivity, material security, acquisitiveness, refinement, luxury, indulgence, idealism in love and beauty.
- **TRI-0168** · nak Rohini · TRI p55 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** The most alluring of all nakshatras; makes people and even gods chase after it.
- **TRI-0170** · nak Rohini · TRI p55 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Very productive; supports all types of growth — the planting of seed into earth is a Rohini process.
- **TRI-0174** · nak Rohini · TRI p55 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Unresponsive to external stimuli like a cow; opens up only in extremely intimate situations; unflinching quality reads as stubbornness.
- **TRI-0177** · nak Rohini · TRI p56 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent, personal ends not aligned with universal ends
  **THEN** Uses charm and social ease to manipulate situations; becomes the most self-serving of people, exploiting trust for material benefit.
- **TRI-0179** · nak Rohini · TRI p56 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Simplistic personality that appears dull to natives of more complex nakshatras; in Kali-Yuga often plain materialists and sensualists.
- **TRI-0181** · nak Rohini · TRI p56 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Facilitates materialisation of one's thoughts and desires; the resulting expressiveness makes the native artistic and a patron of the arts.
- **TRI-0182** · nak Rohini · TRI p56 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** Tremendous fixity of purpose; perseveres until its will is done.
- **TRI-0188** · nak Rohini · TRI p57 · +/stated
  **IF** Rohini prominent
  **THEN** One of the most enjoyment-oriented nakshatras; believes in making the most of the good things in life.
- **TRI-0191** · nak Rohini · TRI p57 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini in its negative aspect
  **THEN** Life passed with the least possible adventure; ultra-conservative and reluctant to try new things that would add wisdom and experience.
- **TRI-0199** · nak Rohini, pada 2 · TRI p59 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 2 badly used
  **THEN** Native suffers from extreme materialism.
- **TRI-0202** · nak Rohini, pada 4 · TRI p59 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini pada 4 badly disposed
  **THEN** Excessive possessiveness, narrow-mindedness and unnecessary hoarding — Rohini's worst qualities relay through this pada.
- **TRI-0218** · nak Rohini · TRI p64 · -/stated
  **IF** Rohini as the devouring nakshatra (Sage Atri lineage)
  **THEN** Consumes souls through excessive materialism.
- **TRI-0221** · nak Rohini, planet Moon · TRI p66 · +/classical
  **IF** Moon in Rohini (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Honest, pure, beautiful, steady of mind and pleasant of speech.

## Mrigashira — nature (87)

- **ASH-0433** · nak Mrigashira · ASH p145 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** Strategist, attractive, civilised, witty, respected, joyful, polite, emotional, smart, compassionate, gentle, truthful, intelligent, enthusiastic and ingenious - but fickle-minded, contradictory and suspicious.
- **ASH-0439** · nak Mrigashira · ASH p147 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent (symbol the deer's head)
  **THEN** Searching, fearful, fickle, frisky, lustful, tender and wandering, always seeking something new whether physical or material.
- **ASH-0440** · nak Mrigashira, sign Taurus · ASH p147 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira falling in Taurus (Venus influence)
  **THEN** Creativity, materialistic approach, artistic nature, monetary attachment, fondness for beauty, physical pleasure, muscle power, action orientation and sociability.
- **ASH-0442** · nak Mrigashira, sign Gemini · ASH p147 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira in Gemini, the cutting tendency dominant
  **THEN** Narrow-mindedness - the native cuts everything down and remains in his own world, overthinking.
- **ASH-0444** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · ASH p148 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira (contrasted with Moon in Rohini)
  **THEN** Movability, instability of mind and a pleasure-seeking, enjoyment-driven tendency - rather than Rohini's charm, benevolence and prosperity.
- **ASH-0451** · nak Mrigashira · ASH p149 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent, female native
  **THEN** Crazy for cosmetics, appearance, fashion and new electronic gadgets, with a competitive sense.
- **ASH-0456** · nak Mrigashira · ASH p149 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent, when provoked or threatened
  **THEN** Normally gentle and curious, avoiding confrontation by staying in corners, but can be deadly if provoked - though usually escapes before trouble starts through high intuition.
- **ASH-0459** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1 · ASH p150 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 1 (Leo navamsha, Sun-ruled)
  **THEN** Sharp intelligence, creative thinking, sweet eloquent voice, fondness for beauty and fashion, artistic mind, love of music and singing, originality, attention-seeking, food-loving, believing in authority and self-empowerment.
- **ASH-0467** · nak Mrigashira, pada 2 · ASH p151 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 2 with positive placement of planets
  **THEN** Polite, social, calculative and scholarly, organised, stable, persistent and composed under pressure - though sometimes acting from selfish and devious motives.
- **ASH-0468** · nak Mrigashira, pada 3 · ASH p151 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 3 (Libra navamsha, Venus-ruled)
  **THEN** Social, diplomatic public speaker, peace-making, attractive, balanced and harmonious in dealing with people, a good researcher, explorer and caretaker - but blistering, impulsive and suspicious.
- **ASH-0478** · nak Mrigashira, house 1 · ASH p153 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira ascendant (the golden deer)
  **THEN** Fantasy, imagination and the search for the ethereal and the greatest joy - fascinating others but never held; must learn the difference between the dream and the work needed to realise it.
- **ASH-0482** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · ASH p154 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Sensitive and hyperreactive to surrounding events, with a fickle, curious mind that anticipates coming events; timid and shy yet a good speaker, restless for what is next.
- **ASH-0483** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · ASH p154 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Negative thoughts create mental trouble; unexpected events occur in life.
- **ASH-0484** · nak Mrigashira, planet Mars · ASH p154 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Extra courage, strength and confronting ability; quick action and immediate response, high anticipation of the enemy's moves, and intense desire to control people or situations.
- **ASH-0489** · nak Mrigashira, planet Jupiter · ASH p155 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Difficulty in decision-making and lack of clarity of thought; establishes peace through knowledge but aggressively.
- **ASH-0493** · nak Mrigashira, planet Venus · ASH p155 · ~/stated
  **IF** Venus in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Loves travel, drink, food and physical pleasure; highly imaginative and romantic, seeking enjoyment at beaches, spas, bars, water resorts and nightclubs, and looking sexy, energetic and younger.
- **ASH-0495** · nak Mrigashira, planet Saturn · ASH p157 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Dilemma in expressing thoughts, rise of controversies, delay in action and laziness; good at planning but poor at execution.
- **AVA-2-1010** · nak Mrigashira · AVA-2 p115 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira placement anywhere
  **THEN** Prinana shakti, the power to give fulfilment: it compares, questions, samples and moves on, giving the finest discrimination in the zodiac and the least peace - the native holds something good in hand and still looks past it. The governing word is searching.
- **AVA-2-1011** · nak Mrigashira · AVA-2 p115 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira under affliction
  **THEN** Scattered, suspicious and never satisfied; at its best it is the explorer and fine perceiver who finds what others miss.
- **AVA-2-1012** · nak Mrigashira · AVA-2 p116 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira falls on any house
  **THEN** The native keeps looking even when the object or person is already present - losing things inside the house, window shopping without buying, hunting for what is in front of the eyes, putting valuables away safely and then forgetting where. Read the same pattern at full scale in the house involved.
- **AVA-2-1018** · nak Mrigashira · AVA-2 p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Any placement in Mrigashira (Mars lord, Soma deity)
  **THEN** The nakshatra runs a divided agenda - the lord drives towards action while the deity draws towards reflection; which side wins is decided by the planet's own dignity and aspects. Mrigashira unsettles a placement; it never strengthens a weak one.
- **AVA-2-1019** · nak Mrigashira · AVA-2 p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Mrigashira compared
  **THEN** The first expresses, the second analyses, the third socialises, the fourth argues; the first two sit in Taurus and stay grounded, the last two move into Gemini and turn airy.
- **AVA-2-1020** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1 · AVA-2 p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 1, 23°20'-26°40' Taurus, Leo navamsha, Sun
  **THEN** Creative and artistic with a strong urge to express experience outwardly - this quarter searches by showing rather than by asking; the nature is fixed and unyielding once set, the one quarter of this nakshatra that does not easily change its mind.
- **AVA-2-1024** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1 · AVA-2 p117 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 1 unrefined
  **THEN** Temper, obstinacy once a position is taken and a carelessness about religious or traditional duty.
- **AVA-2-1026** · nak Mrigashira, pada 2 · AVA-2 p117 · ~/classical
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 2
  **THEN** Good-natured, truthful and full of knowledge, wealthy, calculative, humorous and productive; yet timid and mild, the respectable exterior carrying less self-confidence than it appears. Thievish tendencies, anger and jealousy are recorded alongside the virtues, with attraction towards the opposite sex.
- **AVA-2-1030** · nak Mrigashira, pada 3 · AVA-2 p118 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 3, 00°00'-03°20' Gemini, Libra navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** The sociable quarter oriented towards relationship and keen to explore it - interaction is mental and emotional at once, noticeably airier and less grounded; Venus turns the search outward, towards people.
- **AVA-2-1035** · nak Mrigashira, pada 4 · AVA-2 p118 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 4, 03°20'-06°40' Gemini, Scorpio navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** Intellectual and argumentative, with a suspicious mindset and a habit of over-intellectualising; Mars in Scorpio navamsha gives the search depth and teeth - nothing here is superficial and this quarter is more intense than the three before it.
- **AVA-2-1036** · nak Mrigashira, pada 4 · AVA-2 p118 · ~/classical
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 4
  **THEN** Intelligent, clever and charitable, fond of debate and competition, with wealth and grains indicated; passionate and lustful and clever in lovemaking; timid beneath the argument, short-tempered, with cheating tendencies and a trickster streak; success comes only after struggle.
- **AVA-2-1039** · nak Mrigashira, pada 4 · AVA-2 p118 · -/classical
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 4 unrefined
  **THEN** Suspicion, cheating, over-argument and the whimsical instability the classics attach to this quarter.
- **AVA-2-1062** · nak Mrigashira, planet Mars · AVA-2 p120 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Mrigashira aspected or conjunct the Sun
  **THEN** Lives in forests, dislikes the opposite sex and is courageous.
- **AVA-2-1090** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p122 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Mrigashira pada 1
  **THEN** An unstable mind, with a strong interest in reading and writing; fame and wealth. Gout and shoulder trouble; disability is possible.
- **AVA-2-1092** · nak Mrigashira, pada 3, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p122 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Mrigashira pada 3
  **THEN** Selfish and unwilling to share. Blood and heart problems.
- **AVA-2-1099** · nak Mrigashira, planet Venus · AVA-2 p122 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Mrigashira aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Misery and harassment.
- **AVA-2-1125** · nak Mrigashira, house 1 · AVA-2 p124 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Mrigashira
  **THEN** The personality itself is restless - always searching for identity and purpose, trying on styles, roles, circles and beliefs, each fitting for a while; the body is light and quick, the gaze alert, health following the nerves rather than the constitution. The gift is genuine adaptability and fine perception; the lifelong task is choosing.
- **AVA-2-1152** · nak Mrigashira, planet Mars · AVA-2 p129 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Mars loses its own nature here - despite being the star lord it makes the native fearful of conflict: fights, property decisions and any decisive action get overthought first and aggression is avoided out of fear of consequences, producing action paralysis from over-analysis.
- **AVA-2-1165** · nak Mrigashira, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p130 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu afflicted in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Anxiety, dependence on others for support, jealousy directed at the native and a mind that questions everything including its own questioning.
- **AVA-2-1166** · nak Mrigashira, house 1 · AVA-2 p131 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Mrigashira
  **THEN** The identity itself keeps turning - the native tries on versions of himself and each fits for a while before the eye moves on; the body is light and quick, the gaze alert, health following the nerves. The lifelong lesson is choosing.
- **NAT-0193** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · NAT p152 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira (23°20' Taurus - 6°40' Gemini)
  **THEN** Restless quest for meaning and deeper understanding; graceful curiosity of the perpetual seeker, drawn to both external exploration and inner self-awareness.
- **NAT-0194** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p152 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira ruled by Soma, the Moon, as both planetary ruler and deity
  **THEN** Nourishing, healing and ever-seeking essence; the act of seeking becomes as vital as what is found.
- **NAT-0196** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p153 · ~/stated
  **IF** The karma of Mrigashira
  **THEN** A cycle of steep rise and steep fall: propelled to great heights, then humbled by setbacks that force re-evaluation, humility and return to the spiritual core.
- **NAT-0198** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p154 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira, per Brahma's fifth head
  **THEN** The fine line between inspired pursuit and attachment that disrupts dharma; desire redirected toward possession distorts purpose.
- **NAT-0199** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p154 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira symbolised by the deer's head
  **THEN** Curiosity, instinctual awareness and the unyielding quest to uncover the unknown in knowledge, truth and spirit.
- **NAT-0201** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p155 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira's boundless curiosity and drive to explore
  **THEN** Difficulty committing to a single path or finding stillness; restlessness is both its strength and its vulnerability.
- **NAT-0202** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p155 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent (archetype of the Eternal Seeker)
  **THEN** Insatiable thirst for knowledge; drawn to new experience, fresh ideas and deeper perception, with each discovery breeding further questions.
- **NAT-0204** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p156 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira native after the fall
  **THEN** Transformation comes only by confronting excess, seeking counsel from elders and surrendering ego.
- **NAT-0219** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1 · NAT p162 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 1 (23°20'-26°40' Taurus), Leo navamsa, Sun-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Courage, creativity and a strong sense of identity; seeks security through leadership and resilience, with pride and inflexibility as the hazard.
- **NAT-0220** · nak Mrigashira, pada 2 · NAT p162 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 2 (26°40' Taurus - 0°00' Gemini), Virgo navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Practicality, discipline and meticulous method; restless curiosity grounded into tangible achievement, but perfectionism and overthinking cause stagnation.
- **NAT-0221** · nak Mrigashira, pada 3 · NAT p163 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 3 (0°00'-3°20' Gemini), Libra navamsa, Venus-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Social adaptability, artistic expression and diplomacy; skilled at connection and negotiation, though prone to scattered energy, indecisiveness and superficiality.
- **NAT-0222** · nak Mrigashira, pada 4 · NAT p163 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira pada 4 (3°20'-6°40' Gemini), Scorpio navamsa, Mars-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Introspective and transformative; inner exploration, shadow work, psychological insight and spiritual refinement, demanding mastery over fluctuating emotion.
- **NAT-0225** · nak Mrigashira · NAT p165 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira's rajasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Restlessness, overextension and inability to integrate one experience before moving to the next.
- **RBS-1-1206** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-1 p172 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mrigashira, the deer's head, with the Moon as its deity
  **THEN** Good physical features and thin body parts; sensitive, anxious and suspicious like a deer, fickle and quick-witted like the Moon, yet sincere and honest in dealings, believing in simple living and high thinking.
- **RBS-1-1207** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-1 p172 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mrigashira though it is ruled by Mars
  **THEN** The native is not very bold or courageous because of his suspicious character, and has a restless mind very sensitive to the environment.
- **RBS-1-1212** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-1 p172 · -/stated
  **IF** Male born in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Very sincere in dealings but cheated in partnership business; trusts blindly and reaps frustration and repentance; an inborn coward without peace of mind, irritated over small matters.
- **RBS-1-1214** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-1 p172 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Keenly interested in social work, quick-witted and selfish, with a poisonous tongue; educated, fond of fine arts, acquires considerable wealth, enjoys good food, is lucky in ornaments and fine clothing, and is greedy for wealth.
- **RBS-1-1221** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-1 p173 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mrigashira — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Truthful, clean-hearted, intelligent, good at administration, obedient and respectful to teachers and keen to learn; but restless, nervous and impatient, taking wrong decisions and committing mistakes.
- **RBS-1-1228** · nak Mrigashira, pada 2 · RBS-1 p174 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mrigashira pada 2
  **THEN** Practical, social, calculative and humorous, keen on singing and music and making good soldiers; if the nakshatra is afflicted the native becomes adulterous; minor family disputes and proneness to being cheated; recognisable by a fearful, cowardly nature.
- **RBS-2-0556** · nak Mrigashira, planet Mars · RBS-2 p142 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Mrigashira Nakshatra, ruled by Mars, symbol the deer's head
  **THEN** Quick-witted, witty, a good orator, very optimistic, wealthy and fond of luxury.
- **RBS-2-0557** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p142 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in the Taurus part of Mrigashira versus its last two padas in Gemini
  **THEN** The first part gives stability and dedication; the last two quarters give creativity, versatility and changeability.
- **RBS-2-1074** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p274 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Mrigashira Nakshatra, the deer's head, with the Moon as deity
  **THEN** Good physical features and thin limbs; sensitive, anxious and suspicious like the deer, fickle and quick-witted like the Moon, yet sincere and honest in dealing with people.
- **RBS-2-1075** · nak Mrigashira, planet Mars · RBS-2 p274 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira native although the Nakshatra is ruled by Mars
  **THEN** Believes in simple living and high thinking, but is not very bold or courageous because of his suspicious character, with a restless mind very sensitive to the external environment.
- **RBS-2-1078** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p275 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira, a soft Deva star ruled by the Moon with the serpent yoni
  **THEN** Creates a gentle and peaceful person able to do research, poetic and creative, with a thin body, satirical nature and a sensitive, perceptive temperament.
- **RBS-2-1079** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p275 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira on the ascendant
  **THEN** Attractive and inquisitive, a seeker of truth and beauty, of good intelligence and feminine appearance, much enthusiasm but a vacillating mind, sickness in childhood and many enemies; thin body, talkative and whimsical, devoted to the mother, and possibly an orator or singer.
- **RBS-2-1080** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · RBS-2 p275 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira Nakshatra
  **THEN** Fickle, sharp-witted, timid, eloquent in speech, afraid of confrontation, industrious in work, wealthy and indulgent in sensual pleasures.
- **RBS-2-1085** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p276 · -/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Very sincere in dealings but cheated in partnership business through blind trust, ending in frustration and repentance; an inborn coward without peace of mind, irritated by small matters.
- **RBS-2-1087** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Mrigashira
  **THEN** Keen on social work, quick-witted but selfish with a poisonous tongue; educated and fond of fine arts, acquires considerable wealth, enjoys good food, ornaments and fine clothing, and is greedy for wealth.
- **RBS-2-1095** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p276 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Mrigashira Nakshatra
  **THEN** Truthful and clean at heart, intelligent with good administrative power, obedient, respectful to teachers and always keen to learn and observe.
- **RBS-2-1096** · nak Mrigashira · RBS-2 p276 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Mrigashira Nakshatra
  **THEN** Restless and nervous, impatient, takes wrong decisions and commits mistakes.
- **RBS-2-1105** · nak Mrigashira, pada 2 · RBS-2 p277 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira pada 2 (Taurus 26°40' to Gemini 0°00')
  **THEN** Good-natured, handsome, truthful and knowledgeable but inwardly angry and envious; practical, social, calculative and humorous, keen on singing and music and a good soldier; if afflicted becomes adulterous, with minor family disputes and liability to be cheated; of fearful, cowardly bearing.
- **TAM-RA-0120** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p98 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira nakshatra
  **THEN** Often gives birth to talented, intelligent beings who by default happen to be more sexual than others.
- **TAM-RA-0126** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p100 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira for natives born on Pisces, Sagittarius, Cancer, Taurus, Virgo or Capricorn ascendant
  **THEN** More beneficial than for other ascendants.
- **TAM-RA-0127** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p101 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira nakshatra
  **THEN** Unlike others, these natives fall quickly to their temptations.
- **TAM-RA-0128** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p103 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira with Jupiter and Saturn strong in the birth chart
  **THEN** Though Rahu pushes the native into mistakes and misdoings, strong Jupiter and Saturn enable him to correct them.
- **TAM-RA-0129** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p103 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira nakshatra
  **THEN** Either makes the native wise by rising and correcting his mistakes, or makes him stupid if he repeats the same mistake again and again.
- **TAM-RA-0131** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p104 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira nakshatra
  **THEN** Provides the native with a sixth sense - a natural ability to know things before other people, or to know what others do not.
- **TAM-RA-0133** · nak Mrigashira, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p106 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Mrigashira nakshatra
  **THEN** Fires up the Taurean quality of making effort; these natives are always working on themselves.
- **TRI-0222** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p69 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** Deer-like qualities — timidity, lightness, fragility, fickleness, wandering.
- **TRI-0225** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · TRI p69 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira ruled by Moon as deity
  **THEN** Carries Moon's inconstancy, fickleness, changeability, tenderness, persuasiveness, gentleness, sensuality and perceptivity.
- **TRI-0227** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p70 · +/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira ruled by Parvati
  **THEN** Benevolence, charm, changeability, compassion, playfulness and joyousness; its timidity is not cowardice — it can be strong, unyielding and wrathful when necessary.
- **TRI-0228** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p71 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** 'Searching' is its essence; the most curious of all nakshatras, always seeking new areas of experience, akin to Rahu.
- **TRI-0231** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p71 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira on its lower level
  **THEN** Prances from one transitory experience to the next like a man chasing mirages; fickleness, inconstancy and superficiality; must learn to stay with a thing until completion.
- **TRI-0236** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p72 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** Naturally shy behind the sociability; dislikes confrontation and is cautious about whom it mingles with; mind roams, often as daydreaming.
- **TRI-0237** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p72 · -/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** Highly suspicious nature arising from high alertness and the ability to sense danger.
- **TRI-0241** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p73 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira prominent
  **THEN** The place where discrimination is born; the first nakshatra where conclusions are drawn and fixed opinions formed; never backs down in a civilised argument.
- **TRI-0247** · nak Mrigashira, pada 1 · TRI p74 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet in Mrigashira pada 1 (23°20'-26°40' Taurus, Leo navamsa, Sun)
  **THEN** The fixed and unyielding side of Mrigashira, able to express its experiences through creative and artistic activity.
- **TRI-0250** · nak Mrigashira, pada 4 · TRI p75 · -/stated
  **IF** Planet in Mrigashira pada 4 (3°20'-6°40' Gemini, Scorpio navamsa, Mars)
  **THEN** Intellectual, argumentative, suspicious, flaky and whimsical; over-intellectualises rather than reaching the root; most of Mrigashira's negative qualities express here and it works wisely only in evolved souls under guidance.
- **TRI-0266** · nak Mrigashira · TRI p80 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mrigashira seen as deer's head or arched bow
  **THEN** Dual functioning as both the hunter and the hunted.
- **TRI-0269** · nak Mrigashira, planet Moon · TRI p81 · ~/classical
  **IF** Moon in Mrigashira (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Fickle, clever, timid, shrewd, energetic, wealthy and delighting in enjoyment.

## Ardra — nature (101)

- **ASH-0508** · nak Ardra · ASH p160 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, mentally active and observant, but emotional, lavish, aggressive, unstable, angry, moody, painstaking, piercing, dishonest, ungrateful, addicted and impulsive; sacrifices easily.
- **ASH-0512** · nak Ardra · ASH p163 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent (deity Rudra, lord Rahu)
  **THEN** Destructive energy that tears things down and clears hindrances to growth, bringing fundamental change or transformation.
- **ASH-0513** · nak Ardra · ASH p163 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Seeks pleasure through an expanding friend circle, social network and associations; remains filled with desires and strives to create balance in all spheres.
- **ASH-0517** · nak Ardra · ASH p163 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Drawn to social taboos and inclined to challenge established rules, traditions and systems; seeks enjoyment at extremes through drugs, sex or illicit activity.
- **ASH-0519** · nak Ardra · ASH p163 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent, where patience, listening and sincerity are required
  **THEN** Very impulsive and hyperreactive; causes trouble, short-tempered, torn by dilemmas, ungrateful and obsessive.
- **ASH-0520** · nak Ardra · ASH p164 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent, in a restricted environment
  **THEN** Irritated by lack of freedom; animalistic instincts seek group and tribal association, and the native remains isolated from society as an outcast, foreigner or troublemaker.
- **ASH-0530** · nak Ardra · ASH p165 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent (Rahu's nakshatra falling in the desire triangle)
  **THEN** Deep desires fulfilled through affiliation with people and society; enough courage, will, stamina and confidence to connect socially, and must prove himself through bold action to reach transformation and success.
- **ASH-0533** · nak Ardra · ASH p166 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Likely an unpopular personality through unrestrained expression of ideas and refusal to stay silent at what he sees as wrong - but on closer acquaintance the smartest and most endearing person, devoted to family and humorous.
- **ASH-0541** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · ASH p167 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 1
  **THEN** Unstable mind whose thoughts flow like a storm, creating difficulty in concluding things; meditation is required to calm this storm.
- **ASH-0546** · nak Ardra, pada 2 · ASH p168 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 2
  **THEN** The most unfavourable characteristics of Ardra manifest here - tragedy and misfortune - and the native must stay alert and control his emotions; slow in working, rough in talk.
- **ASH-0552** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · ASH p169 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 4 (Jupiter influencing, expressed through Mercury)
  **THEN** Wisdom, intelligence, spirituality, long-term vision and benevolence; charitable, sympathetic and sensitive, supporting others beyond his own interest.
- **ASH-0555** · nak Ardra, house 1 · ASH p170 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra ascendant
  **THEN** Good understanding of the dark side of anything, determined to achieve what he wants, faces adversity boldly, seeks detailed information, holds a global vision and believes in jungle rule; able to transform his life completely despite setbacks.
- **ASH-0560** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · ASH p171 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra
  **THEN** Shadowy mentality feeling suffocated in taking right decisions; chaotic, restless mind, illusion prominent so things are not perceived correctly.
- **ASH-0564** · nak Ardra, planet Mars · ASH p172 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ardra (an inimical placement)
  **THEN** Very aggressive and violent, out of focus with no clear direction in action; may damage others, with an inclination toward crime.
- **ASH-0566** · nak Ardra, planet Mars · ASH p172 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ardra (Ardra's humidity resisting Mars's heat)
  **THEN** Thoughts get smoky; impulsive snap judgments, useful in emergencies.
- **ASH-0580** · nak Ardra, planet Ketu · ASH p176 · +/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Ardra
  **THEN** Ability to handle chaotic situations patiently without hyperreaction; learns from past mistakes and corrects the present; accepts the world as a jungle where sudden threats are always expected.
- **AVA-2-1195** · nak Ardra · AVA-2 p137 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra placement anywhere
  **THEN** Yatna shakti, the power of effort and of gains won only through struggle: Ardra breaks what is settled, shakes what is comfortable, transforms what survives and finally sets the native free - working through fear first, then exposure, then fearlessness. The governing word is transformation.
- **AVA-2-1196** · nak Ardra · AVA-2 p138 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra under affliction
  **THEN** Turbulent, resentful and destructive, consuming itself in the storm it cannot calm; at its best it is the mind that breaks an old truth open and finds a better one.
- **AVA-2-1201** · nak Ardra · AVA-2 p139 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Ardra compared
  **THEN** The first is protected, the second endures, the third breaks with convention, the fourth goes deepest; the storm grows heavier with each one.
- **AVA-2-1202** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p139 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 1, 06°40'-10°00' Gemini, Sagittarius navamsha, Jupiter
  **THEN** The mildest quarter of Ardra by a clear margin: principled and expansive, dignified in bearing, expressing experience outwardly through speech, teaching or creative work, with natural charm, attraction and presence.
- **AVA-2-1203** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p139 · +/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 1
  **THEN** Good looks, a strong personality and real oratory ability; noble and proud and openly seeking recognition; pure-hearted, intelligent, yet extravagant; a controller of the senses drawn to companionship and the opposite gender; composure holds in adversity.
- **AVA-2-1208** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p139 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 1 unrefined
  **THEN** Extravagance, pride and the open hunger for recognition.
- **AVA-2-1210** · nak Ardra, pada 2 · AVA-2 p140 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 2
  **THEN** Witty, humorous and satirical; straightforward, outspoken and frank, argumentative and critical; crafty, tactful and mentally sharp; a jack of all trades, curious about everything and a capable speaker; proud, with a sickly tendency recorded alongside.
- **AVA-2-1214** · nak Ardra, pada 2 · AVA-2 p140 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 2 unrefined
  **THEN** Criticism that hardens into cruelty, pride and restlessness that scatters the ability.
- **AVA-2-1215** · nak Ardra, pada 3 · AVA-2 p140 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 3, 13°20'-16°40' Gemini, Aquarius navamsha, Saturn
  **THEN** The quarter that breaks with convention on principle - socially engaged, humanitarian in reach and genuinely interested in people as a subject rather than as company; the classics say plainly that this quarter opposes order.
- **AVA-2-1216** · nak Ardra, pada 3 · AVA-2 p140 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 3
  **THEN** Sociable, charming and engaging, gentle outside and sharp underneath; investigative and observant, with actions arriving suddenly and responses coming fast; short-tempered and impulsive, unstable in behaviour and a carrier of tales.
- **AVA-2-1221** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · AVA-2 p141 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 4, 16°40'-20°00' Gemini, Pisces navamsha, Jupiter
  **THEN** The heaviest quarter of Ardra: the intellect is powerful and the emotional life complicated, the search running past the surface answer to the root cause; compassion and turbulence occupy the same nature.
- **AVA-2-1222** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · AVA-2 p141 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 4
  **THEN** Argumentative, suspicious and investigative, given to over-intellectualising, with strong analytical depth; restless and unpredictable, emotionally intense and probing; generous, with a real connection to animals; short-tempered and inclined to receive bad advice; needs guidance to find direction and turns genuinely wise only in the evolved state.
- **AVA-2-1226** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · AVA-2 p141 · -/classical
  **IF** Ardra pada 4 unrefined
  **THEN** Suspicion, scheming, unclean habits, hostility towards relatives and emotional upheaval.
- **AVA-2-1241** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · AVA-2 p143 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Courageous and fearless.
- **AVA-2-1246** · nak Ardra, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p143 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra pada 1
  **THEN** Irritable and short-tempered; with Jupiter's influence, high status and wealth follow instead.
- **AVA-2-1266** · nak Ardra, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p144 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ardra aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Disciplined and respected.
- **AVA-2-1303** · nak Ardra, pada 2, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p146 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra pada 2
  **THEN** Over-talkative, with immoral tendencies; accident-prone; ego brings alienation from others.
- **AVA-2-1315** · nak Ardra, house 1 · AVA-2 p148 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Ardra
  **THEN** A highly transformative life marked by phases of identity crisis: fearless, rebellious and emotionally intense, thinking without boundaries, the lifestyle unconventional by choice; self-reinvention comes easily and extreme situations are attracted rather than avoided. The presence is magnetic and widely misunderstood in equal measure.
- **AVA-2-1340** · nak Ardra, planet Sun · AVA-2 p152 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Ardra
  **THEN** The ego breaks down and an identity crisis follows - the self must be rebuilt rather than developed; conflict with authority and with the father and rebellion against what society expects, so the career path turns non-traditional. Confidence here covers a real inner insecurity.
- **AVA-2-1342** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · AVA-2 p153 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra
  **THEN** Emotional fluctuation runs to extremes: the mind is highly sensitive and reactive, prone to overthinking and turbulence, with sudden mood swings; the cycle repeats through the life - fear, then confrontation, then fearlessness - and after enough repeated pain a genuine emotional detachment sets in.
- **AVA-2-1347** · nak Ardra, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p153 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ardra, unrefined
  **THEN** Communication turns sarcastic or blunt and knowledge spreads across many subjects rather than settling into one; mental instability comes first and clarity comes later.
- **AVA-2-1354** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p154 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra, its own nakshatra
  **THEN** Extreme desires and ambitions, magnified; illusion and confusion cloud judgement and obsession with success takes hold, so rise and fall both arrive suddenly and often within the same decade; the pull runs towards taboo areas and psychological instability is a genuine risk.
- **AVA-2-1357** · nak Ardra, house 1 · AVA-2 p156 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Ardra
  **THEN** The native is rebuilt several times over a lifetime, each rebuild following a collapse of the version before it; the presence is intense and hard to ignore and the nerves and breathing carry the strain. Dashas touching this degree arrive as crises and leave as clarity.
- **NAT-0229** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · NAT p169 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra (6°40'-20°00' Gemini)
  **THEN** Intensity, emotional depth and the dual force of destruction and renewal; old energies cleared through emotional storms and catharsis.
- **NAT-0230** · nak Ardra · NAT p169 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra symbolised by the teardrop and ruled by Rudra
  **THEN** Grief, loss and cathartic release that paves the way for healing; transformation comes from navigating emotional extremes with courage and humility.
- **NAT-0231** · nak Ardra · NAT p171 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra's severing of illusion, per Rudra and Brahma's fifth head
  **THEN** Truth cuts through illusion, but the act leaves an imprint: consequences of wielded power must still be carried and transformed.
- **NAT-0232** · nak Ardra · NAT p172 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra's destructive force, per Daksa's yajna
  **THEN** Never senseless — it clears what veils truth and tears down arrogance, and restores balance once the storm passes.
- **NAT-0233** · nak Ardra · NAT p172 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra native's inner life
  **THEN** Storms of rage and sorrow that end in renewal; emotional intensity is cleansing when expressed, destructive when repressed.
- **NAT-0234** · nak Ardra · NAT p173 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent (archetype of the Fierce Transformer)
  **THEN** A catalyst for change, placed where they must challenge, judge or execute power with precision; uncompromising pursuit of truth intolerant of deception or mediocrity.
- **NAT-0235** · nak Ardra · NAT p174 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Sharp intellect, unfiltered speech and the ability to see through facades; powerful thinker, strategist and leader.
- **NAT-0236** · nak Ardra · NAT p174 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra's clarity wielded without wisdom or compassion
  **THEN** Excessive criticism, detachment and a tendency to destroy without rebuilding; isolation, and truth that alienates rather than liberates.
- **NAT-0238** · nak Ardra · NAT p174 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra native's karmic cycle
  **THEN** Cycles of power and downfall in which strength is tested and missteps demand correction; hardship serves as necessary refinement.
- **NAT-0251** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · NAT p181 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 1 (6°40'-10°00' Gemini), Sagittarius navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Philosophical depth and expansive vision; boldness and thirst for knowledge turned to purposeful intellectual pursuit, though focus scatters if unchecked.
- **NAT-0252** · nak Ardra, pada 2 · NAT p181 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 2 (10°00'-13°20' Gemini), Capricorn navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Discipline, practicality and perseverance grounding the stormy energy; excels at problem-solving and methodical planning, risking rigidity and over-caution.
- **NAT-0253** · nak Ardra, pada 3 · NAT p181 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 3 (13°20'-16°40' Gemini), Aquarius navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Visionary, collaborative and innovative; social adaptability and collective endeavour, with detachment and scattered energy as the risk.
- **NAT-0254** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · NAT p182 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra pada 4 (16°40'-20°00' Gemini), Pisces navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Spiritual depth, emotional healing, introspection and intuitive understanding; a path to inner peace through self-reflection.
- **NAT-0257** · nak Ardra · NAT p183 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra's rajasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Restlessness, impulsivity and emotional intensity requiring grounding and cooling.
- **NAT-0937** · nak Ardra · NAT p574 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra natives
  **THEN** Experience life as a series of destruction and renewal cycles, emerging stronger after every storm.
- **NAT-0938** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · NAT p574 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra
  **THEN** A restless, emotionally complex and deeply expressive mind whose expression is raw, transformative and unapologetically honest.
- **RBS-1-1232** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p174 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra
  **THEN** Very responsible, polite and cordial, fascinates crowds by public speaking, has a sharp mind that reads body language, responds quickly and acquires good knowledge of his field.
- **RBS-1-1233** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p174 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra
  **THEN** Brave in facing hardship without complaint; outwardly relaxed but with a storm within; undertakes multiple tasks at once which becomes a cause of mental agony.
- **RBS-1-1236** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p175 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra not well supported by other benefic planets, or afflicted
  **THEN** The native becomes selfish and sarcastic, a confusing personality whose energies go into unyielding activity; he may become a cheater, thief, fraud or criminal, and if he enters astrology becomes a tantric; these vices may be prominent until about 32 years of age.
- **RBS-1-1237** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p175 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra, whose deity Rudra relates to death
  **THEN** The native is hard enough to face the unpleasant facts of life and believes in removing and throwing away anything that has lost its importance and utility.
- **RBS-1-1239** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p175 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Ardra
  **THEN** A good psychologist, very cordial with friends and relatives, but without constancy in behaviour.
- **RBS-1-1247** · nak Ardra · RBS-1 p175 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Hard working and intelligent, innovating old things into new with fertile thinking, strong and mentally stable and empathetic; but always sad, impulsive, clever and calculating, stubborn and of violent temperament, unable to make lasting social bonds.
- **RBS-1-1251** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · RBS-1 p176 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra pada 1
  **THEN** Real turbulence of mind so that the native finds it hard to conclude anything; he must meditate to control this storm; recognisable by fair complexion, large face and red eyes.
- **RBS-1-1254** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · RBS-1 p176 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ardra pada 4
  **THEN** Emotional and caring, charitable and ready to give financial support to others; a comparatively stable mind devoted to fruitful activity; beautiful lips and honey-coloured eyes.
- **RBS-2-0559** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · RBS-2 p143 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Ardra Nakshatra, ruled by Rahu, symbol the head
  **THEN** Intelligent, versatile and immensely popular, drawn to all kinds of communication work; an intellectual warrior, but made secretive by Rahu so that nobody can read his mind.
- **RBS-2-1110** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p278 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Ardra Nakshatra
  **THEN** Very responsible, polite and cordial, fascinating crowds by public speaking, sharp-minded and able to read others by body language, quick to answer, acquiring good knowledge of his field, and brave enough to face hardship without complaint.
- **RBS-2-1111** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra native under pressure
  **THEN** Appears relaxed outwardly but a storm goes on within; undertakes several tasks at once, which becomes a cause of mental agony.
- **RBS-2-1112** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra native in society
  **THEN** Quickly associates with social organisations and enjoys social service, going to any length to help a sufferer, and gives little importance to finances.
- **RBS-2-1114** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra native not well supported by other benefic planets
  **THEN** Becomes selfish and sarcastic in nature.
- **RBS-2-1116** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra native, the deity Rudra relating to death
  **THEN** Hard enough to face the unpleasant facts of life, and believes in removing and throwing away whatever has lost its importance and utility.
- **RBS-2-1117** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra, a sharp earthly star meaning the teardrop
  **THEN** Indicates one who can cause others pain, of the hunter and animal-killer class; either one who torments others or one who feels for their suffering, but usually the former.
- **RBS-2-1118** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p279 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra on the ascendant
  **THEN** Proud and long-lived, receiving money from the government, with few children, a thief of others' goods, of crafty speech and fickle mind and robust body; usually an athlete or manual labourer unsuited to intellectual work, poor, deceitful and critical but with a good memory.
- **RBS-2-1119** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · RBS-2 p280 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra Nakshatra
  **THEN** Perfidious, haughty, ungrateful, mischievous and sinful.
- **RBS-2-1123** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p280 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Ardra
  **THEN** A good psychologist, very cordial with friends and relatives, but without constancy of behaviour.
- **RBS-2-1133** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p281 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Ardra Nakshatra
  **THEN** Hardworking and intelligent, renovating old things and producing new ones with fertile thinking, mentally strong and stable yet empathetic toward others.
- **RBS-2-1134** · nak Ardra · RBS-2 p281 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Ardra Nakshatra
  **THEN** Always sad and impulsive, clever and calculating, stubborn and liable to violent temper, which prevents close social bonds.
- **RBS-2-1139** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · RBS-2 p281 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra pada 1 (Gemini 6°40' to 10°00')
  **THEN** Noble and of praiseworthy habits, truthful, of good intelligence and sense control, but with real turbulence of mind that makes conclusions difficult, so meditation is needed; fair complexion, large face and red eyes.
- **RBS-2-1141** · nak Ardra, pada 3 · RBS-2 p282 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra pada 3 (Gemini 13°20' to 16°40')
  **THEN** An angry and cruel back-biter who opposes the godly, yet this is the pada that makes a social worker willing to give physical support to those in trouble; sharp-minded and consistent in work; thick limbs, large head and protruding veins.
- **RBS-2-1142** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · RBS-2 p282 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ardra pada 4 (Gemini 16°40' to 20°00')
  **THEN** Ugly, dirty and evil-minded, receiving bad advice, hating his brethren and of bad character; yet emotional and caring, charitable and free with financial help, with a comparatively stable mind devoted to fruitful activity; beautiful lips and honey-coloured eyes.
- **TAM-RA-0145** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p113 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra (Ardra means moist, wet)
  **THEN** Ardra-born natives cry a lot; with Rahu there the native does not merely cry but bursts into tears.
- **TAM-RA-0149** · nak Ardra · TAM-RA p116 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra, the Shiva nakshatra, Shaiva by essence
  **THEN** It is not soft but Rudra avatar - dashing, dynamic, aggressive, a fighter, a justice seeker and a risk taker.
- **TAM-RA-0153** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p117 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Native wants people around him - a social animal with many friends who tries hard to stay connected with every family member.
- **TAM-RA-0154** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p117 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Excellent memory - the native remembers every face and every distant relative, a walking directory of contacts who never forgets a birthday.
- **TAM-RA-0159** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p120 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Native finds it very difficult to accept his own mistake.
- **TAM-RA-0161** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p120 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Native loves the blame game and puts blame on others, and so misses the chance to learn, evolve and mature.
- **TAM-RA-0163** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p121 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings a great deal of intellectualism; the native lives in his head and is highly logical, his brain commanding his heart - the root of all his problems and miseries.
- **TAM-RA-0164** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p122 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** The mind becomes more cunning and treacherous, capable of acting a virtue it does not have.
- **TAM-RA-0165** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p122 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra, judged by Rahu's placement and the aspects it receives
  **THEN** The native goes one of two ways - a total fake and hypocrite, or a very intense realistic person.
- **TAM-RA-0168** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p122 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Native always believes his problem is caused by someone else, which makes him more miserable still.
- **TAM-RA-0176** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu, sign Gemini · TAM-RA p125 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra (6 deg 40 min to 20 deg Gemini), where Rahu is exalted
  **THEN** Rahu is totally empowered to deliver its results in totality - the peak point of Rahu, a sign that the desire itself is exalted.
- **TAM-RA-0182** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p126 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ardra nakshatra
  **THEN** Mind is always turned outward, so the native is attached to gossip, intrigue, plots and politics, preoccupied with who says what about him.
- **TRI-0273** · nak Ardra · TRI p83 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent, storms survived
  **THEN** Produces a glowing personality, as the diamond is formed under extreme heat and pressure; hard work extracts the jewel of knowledge.
- **TRI-0274** · nak Ardra · TRI p84 · +/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Unyielding and determined; sharp piercing quality that cuts through anything; depth of perception and insistence on getting to the root of the matter.
- **TRI-0275** · nak Ardra · TRI p84 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra ruled by Rudra
  **THEN** Brings chaos, disorder, confusion, anarchy and havoc; the essence of Ardra is change, and it is expert at producing the situations that force it.
- **TRI-0277** · nak Ardra · TRI p85 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Childlike — flits from intense joy to intense sorrow within minutes; constant churning and constant transformation, producing either gems of knowledge or poisonous thoughts and confusion.
- **TRI-0279** · nak Ardra · TRI p85 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Probing observation without regard for courtesy; upfront about voicing findings, even in children.
- **TRI-0281** · nak Ardra · TRI p86 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ardra prominent
  **THEN** Mental sensitivity and impressionability; susceptible to all kinds of hurt, and in some cases a strong desire to help those who suffer. Its emotional thirst is based on empathy.
- **TRI-0286** · nak Ardra · TRI p86 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra in a lesser evolved soul
  **THEN** Childlike pursuit of selfish ends through lying and unscrupulousness; though courageous like Rudra and unafraid of confrontation.
- **TRI-0289** · nak Ardra, planet Rahu · TRI p87 · -/stated
  **IF** Ardra under Rahu's illusory energy
  **THEN** Creates confusion and scatters intellectual energy into meaningless pursuits; makes the native stubborn, arrogant and reckless.
- **TRI-0296** · nak Ardra, pada 1 · TRI p89 · ~/stated
  **IF** Planet in Ardra pada 1 (6°40'-10°00' Gemini, Sagittarius navamsa, Jupiter)
  **THEN** Exploratory and curious; happy-go-lucky but prone to material excess. The beginning of the storm — conditions are not very unfavourable.
- **TRI-0299** · nak Ardra, pada 4 · TRI p89 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet in Ardra pada 4 (16°40'-20°00' Gemini, Pisces navamsa, Jupiter) — pushkara navamsa
  **THEN** Sensitive and compassionate, with a strong desire to help the less fortunate; the storm is ending, conditions mild, and planets usually give benefic expansive results.
- **TRI-0319** · nak Ardra, planet Moon · TRI p96 · -/classical
  **IF** Moon in Ardra (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Ungrateful, wicked, violent and proud.

## Punarvasu — nature (84)

- **ASH-0587** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p179 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Wisdom, compassion, wealth, smartness, benevolence, positivity, supportiveness, helpfulness, imagination, intuition, energy, talent and sympathy - with a repetitive, easily threatened streak.
- **ASH-0591** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent (deity Aditi)
  **THEN** Grounding, nurturing, protective and reincarnating energy; a natural philosophical mind that makes sense of what comes and goes, goal-oriented yet friendly to all.
- **ASH-0592** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Focuses on what he needs while appearing careless externally, and makes a fantastic comeback despite setbacks.
- **ASH-0597** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Helping nature that supports the underprivileged who seek help, and forgives them easily.
- **ASH-0602** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p184 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Excellent nurturing skill giving the habit of interrupting or advising others in every task, developing training and educating skill that lets life prosper, including animals and pets.
- **ASH-0606** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p184 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Deep learning through troublesome events that inspires the native to move ahead despite losing everything and live happily with a purpose.
- **ASH-0610** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p185 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Appears ordinary but carries a subtle ego not easily noticed; fails to accept faults easily but seeks forgiveness when found at fault, keeping balance in everything.
- **ASH-0612** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p185 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent (bamboo as its tree)
  **THEN** Unbelievable adaptability with great reach - strong, flexible, hollow, long, straight and fast-growing; gentle and giving, and eventually spiritually inclined.
- **ASH-0613** · nak Punarvasu · ASH p185 · -/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent, after success later in life
  **THEN** May become egotistical and cruel to younger peers; hard to understand because he sometimes acts against his own nature.
- **ASH-0622** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · ASH p186 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 1
  **THEN** Straightforward and confident in expression, works aggressively for the benefit of people, takes bold risks and faces opponents firmly; difficulties at the beginning but successful accomplishment of goals.
- **ASH-0632** · nak Punarvasu, pada 3 · ASH p187 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 3
  **THEN** Always goal-oriented and focused like Arjuna - nothing can distract him from his objective.
- **ASH-0634** · nak Punarvasu, pada 4 · ASH p187 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 4
  **THEN** Homesickness or controversial ideologies with an overly speculative and restless mind; a nature lover who follows the cycle of the seasons, with a distinct sense of humour.
- **ASH-0636** · nak Punarvasu, house 1 · ASH p188 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Cool, calm and easy-going, marked by reconciliation, redemption, optimism, reunion, remembrance, mentorship and regeneration; makes a magical comeback out of suffering and grave problems and ends up safe.
- **ASH-0639** · nak Punarvasu, planet Sun · ASH p188 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Disciplinary approach to life, authoritative and dominating, rigid in opinion and believing only he knows the truth; proud and egoistic with a juvenile but tactful way of expression.
- **ASH-0641** · nak Punarvasu, planet Moon · ASH p189 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Gives priority to a contented life rather than chasing material things; easy-going nature bringing success and prosperity, humble, with a sharp memory full of emotional experience.
- **ASH-0650** · nak Punarvasu, planet Mercury · ASH p191 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Restlessness and an urge to overthink; must learn to concentrate mental effort and keep from wandering.
- **ASH-0657** · nak Punarvasu, planet Venus · ASH p192 · ~/stated
  **IF** Venus in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Looks cool and calm and helps others recover from misery, with caring, nurturing, home-like feeling in dealing with people - but may become superficial or overindulgent in the pursuit of pleasure.
- **ASH-0662** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · ASH p193 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu
  **THEN** The native loses his originality and becomes hypocritical; intention to cheat or defraud while appearing very social and benevolent externally, with a deep desire to challenge established norms.
- **ASH-0667** · nak Punarvasu, planet Ketu · ASH p194 · +/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Mental satisfaction in helping, guiding and caring for others; the ability to establish normalcy secretly or in a hidden way.
- **AVA-2-1387** · nak Punarvasu · AVA-2 p162 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu placement anywhere
  **THEN** Vasutva prapana shakti, the power to regain wealth and substance once lost: optimistic, philosophical, adaptable and forgiving, able to begin again after loss without bitterness, at home wherever they go. The governing word is renewal.
- **AVA-2-1388** · nak Punarvasu · AVA-2 p162 · -/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu unrefined
  **THEN** The one who can always start over may never settle, scattering energy across too many fresh beginnings.
- **AVA-2-1392** · nak Punarvasu · AVA-2 p163 · -/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu unrefined and unactivated
  **THEN** Procrastination, over-idealism, a what-is-the-use mindset, cycles that repeat without learning and real difficulty letting go; morality is strong and the nature simple and the standing conflict is between idealism and action. Punarvasu protects a placement and lets it recover; it does not make a weak one strong overnight.
- **AVA-2-1393** · nak Punarvasu · AVA-2 p163 · +/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Punarvasu compared
  **THEN** They move from action to comfort to intellect to nurture and each is stronger than the one before, ending in one of the most auspicious padas in the zodiac.
- **AVA-2-1394** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · AVA-2 p163 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 1, 20°00'-23°20' Gemini, Aries navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** Movable, dynamic and adventurous - this quarter pioneers and acts, with a strong pull towards initiative and leadership; the focus falls on friendship, group work, social networks and shared goals rather than solitary effort.
- **AVA-2-1395** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · AVA-2 p163 · ~/classical
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 1
  **THEN** Educated, wise, humorous and intelligent; changeable, restless and mobile, active in social circles and seeking recognition through associations; wise but conceited, lustful, short-tempered and pleasure-seeking.
- **AVA-2-1399** · nak Punarvasu, pada 2 · AVA-2 p164 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 2, 23°20'-26°40' Gemini, Taurus navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** Materialistic and grounded, earthy and stable, with a real inclination towards comfort and luxury - the restlessness of the first quarter settles here into taste; a love of fineness and beauty, sweet-tongued, with a strong sense of self-respect.
- **AVA-2-1400** · nak Punarvasu, pada 2 · AVA-2 p164 · ~/classical
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 2
  **THEN** Learned, well-mannered and protective towards relatives and respectful towards religion; clever at work, yet bilious, lazy, melancholic and pensive by temperament; inclined to the material side of life, with a tendency to a heavy body.
- **AVA-2-1405** · nak Punarvasu, pada 3 · AVA-2 p164 · ~/classical
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 3
  **THEN** Creative and research-minded, poetic and long-lived - yet the classics also record greed and hypocrisy alongside the gifts, with a wandering tendency.
- **AVA-2-1409** · nak Punarvasu, pada 4 · AVA-2 p165 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 4, 00°00'-03°20' Cancer, Cancer navamsha, Moon, vargottama and Pushkara
  **THEN** The most beneficial and powerful pada in the nakshatra - nurturing and maternal, highly emotional and compassionate, expansive and protective; one of the most auspicious placements in the entire zodiac, and Jupiter reaches its maximum exaltation strength here.
- **AVA-2-1410** · nak Punarvasu, pada 4 · AVA-2 p165 · +/classical
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 4
  **THEN** Talkative, intelligent, fair-minded, pious, educated and generous, with a scholarly disposition; strongly nurturing, compassionate, charitable and protective; untruthful at times, yet dear to women and eloquent and given to meritorious deeds.
- **AVA-2-1426** · nak Punarvasu, pada 4, planet Sun · AVA-2 p167 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Punarvasu pada 4
  **THEN** Short in stature and beautiful; takes part in many auspicious ceremonies and has a talent for spotting defects and judging the true nature of things.
- **AVA-2-1451** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1, planet Mars · AVA-2 p168 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Punarvasu pada 1
  **THEN** Harsh in language, passionate and short-tempered, but practical with money. Prone to upper respiratory trouble.
- **AVA-2-1486** · nak Punarvasu, planet Venus · AVA-2 p170 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Punarvasu aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Sometimes cheats or deceives others, which leads to unhappiness and anxiety.
- **AVA-2-1488** · nak Punarvasu, pada 2, planet Venus · AVA-2 p170 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Punarvasu pada 2
  **THEN** Tends to lose motivation or else to stay overly fixed and rigid, which becomes the source of the trouble.
- **AVA-2-1494** · nak Punarvasu, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p170 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Punarvasu aspected or conjunct the Sun
  **THEN** Difficulty enjoying life's pleasures, with trouble caused by poor company.
- **AVA-2-1516** · nak Punarvasu, house 1 · AVA-2 p172 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Punarvasu
  **THEN** A life of repeated reinvention: identity moves through clarity, then confusion, then clarity again and finally settles into wisdom; the personality is calm, soft and wise on the surface and internally conflicted underneath, so this native looks simple and thinks deeply.
- **AVA-2-1546** · nak Punarvasu, planet Sun · AVA-2 p177 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Punarvasu
  **THEN** The personality is calm, wise and philosophical, avoiding domination and preferring the role of a guide to that of a commander; the shadow is a lack of direction and delayed self-confidence.
- **AVA-2-1547** · nak Punarvasu, planet Moon · AVA-2 p177 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu
  **THEN** The mind works in emotional cycles - the native feels loss deeply and then heals naturally, the recovery being constitutional rather than achieved; the emotional nature is nurturing, forgiving and adaptable. No emotional pain stays permanent under this Moon.
- **AVA-2-1550** · nak Punarvasu, planet Mars · AVA-2 p178 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Adventurous but inconsistent, courageous in bursts and passive between them; the energy fluctuates and needs deliberate discipline. The shadow is procrastination and a lack of sustained effort.
- **AVA-2-1554** · nak Punarvasu, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p178 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Punarvasu, shadow side
  **THEN** Excessive idealism and a lack of practicality, the assumption that grace will cover what effort should have.
- **AVA-2-1558** · nak Punarvasu, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p179 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Punarvasu, shadow side
  **THEN** Pessimism and inactivity - the risk that a placement built for waiting forgets to act; the native is otherwise responsible, simple and grounded, without the harshness Saturn shows in less protected stars.
- **AVA-2-1560** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p179 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu, shadow side
  **THEN** Confusion and scattered focus, the appetite for options outrunning the ability to choose among them.
- **AVA-2-1562** · nak Punarvasu, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p179 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Punarvasu, shadow side
  **THEN** Emotional withdrawal and isolation, the retreat that begins as discernment and hardens into distance.
- **AVA-2-1563** · nak Punarvasu, house 1 · AVA-2 p181 · +/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Punarvasu
  **THEN** The native is drawn and released many times over a life - setbacks land and the person returns each time a little truer; the presence is calm, kind and unforced, easy to underestimate and the recovery power remarkable.
