Health dips and restores rather than breaking. Dashas touching this degree begin something a later dasha will complete.
- **NAT-0260** · nak Punarvasu, planet Moon · NAT p187 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu (20°00' Gemini - 3°20' Cancer)
  **THEN** Rebirth, restoration and resilience; after every hardship the promise of beginning again, wiser and better prepared.
- **NAT-0261** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p187 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu governed by Aditi, mother of the gods
  **THEN** Boundless potential, expansion, protection and the power to restore balance after loss; a naksatra of second chances.
- **NAT-0263** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p188 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu native meeting setbacks
  **THEN** Setbacks are cycles of renewal, not endings; the seeds of resurgence already take root in apparent defeat.
- **NAT-0265** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p189 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu symbolised by the quiver of arrows
  **THEN** Preparation, potential and sustained effort; learns from past attempts so that each setback recalibrates the aim toward a higher purpose.
- **NAT-0266** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p190 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent (archetype of the Restorer)
  **THEN** A natural source of hope offering strength, guidance and unwavering support; innate ability to protect, nurture and restore balance after adversity.
- **NAT-0267** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p191 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Introspective yet action-oriented; balances reflection with decisive effort, knowing when to act and when to prepare.
- **NAT-0268** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p191 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu at its best
  **THEN** Patience, perseverance and adaptability; revival and hope always within reach after the greatest falls.
- **NAT-0279** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · NAT p196 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 1 (20°00'-23°20' Gemini), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Courage, decisiveness and pioneering spirit; leadership and fearless pursuit of higher ideals, needing care against impulsivity.
- **NAT-0280** · nak Punarvasu, pada 2 · NAT p196 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 2 (23°20'-26°40' Gemini), Taurus navamsa, Venus-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Intellect grounded in practicality and patience; creativity with tangible results and sustainable, resourceful achievement.
- **NAT-0281** · nak Punarvasu, pada 3 · NAT p197 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 3 (26°40'-30°00' Gemini), Gemini navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Intellectual dynamism, curiosity, communication skill and relational agility; vibrant collaboration, though prone to scattered energy and superficial engagement.
- **NAT-0282** · nak Punarvasu, pada 4 · NAT p197 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu pada 4 (0°00'-3°20' Cancer), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Deep emotional sensitivity and nurturing; intuitive wisdom, compassion and connection to higher truth, promoting spiritual alignment and emotional resilience.
- **NAT-0285** · nak Punarvasu · NAT p199 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu's sattvic guna cultivated
  **THEN** Clarity, balance and purity supporting healing, optimism and the ability to begin again after loss.
- **RBS-1-1255** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-1 p177 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Punarvasu, meaning to become prosperous again, ruled by Aditi the infinite
  **THEN** The native is very adjusting, accommodating himself to different circumstances and different people — a quality rare in other nakshatras.
- **RBS-1-1257** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-1 p177 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Punarvasu, one of the most contented of nakshatras
  **THEN** He prefers a steady life and takes no major risk to avoid unpleasant situations; sometimes he becomes so contented that he loses enthusiasm and, after achieving some status, stops further effort.
- **RBS-1-1260** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-1 p177 · +/stated
  **IF** Male born in Punarvasu
  **THEN** He does not like to cause trouble to others and leads a simple life.
- **RBS-1-1261** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-1 p177 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Punarvasu
  **THEN** An argumentative tongue leading to frequent friction with relatives and neighbours, but many servants and a comfortable life.
- **RBS-1-1269** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-1 p178 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Punarvasu — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Spiritual, sober, pleasant, social, with a tendency to discern spiritual meaning and a domestic nature; but lacking forethought and carefulness, susceptible, disregarding people, fickle-minded and unstable.
- **RBS-2-1144** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p282 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Punarvasu Nakshatra, meaning to become prosperous again, ruled by Aditi the goddess of infinity and abundance
  **THEN** Very adjusting, able to accommodate himself to different circumstances and different people — a quality rare among the other Nakshatra.
- **RBS-2-1146** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p283 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu native after attaining some status
  **THEN** Among the most contented of all Nakshatra and capable of achieving material and spiritual objectives, preferring a steady life without major risk; but sometimes so contented that enthusiasm is lost and further effort stops.
- **RBS-2-1150** · nak Punarvasu, planet Moon · RBS-2 p283 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu Nakshatra
  **THEN** Self-controlled, happy, friendly, dull and easily contented, but diseased and unable to bear thirst.
- **RBS-2-1155** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p284 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Punarvasu
  **THEN** Does not like to cause trouble to others and leads a simple life.
- **RBS-2-1156** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p284 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Punarvasu
  **THEN** An argumentative tongue leading to frequent friction with relatives and neighbours, but she has many servants and a comfortable life.
- **RBS-2-1164** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p284 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Punarvasu Nakshatra
  **THEN** Spiritual, sober, pleasant and social, with a tendency to discern the spiritual meaning of life and an essentially domestic nature.
- **RBS-2-1165** · nak Punarvasu · RBS-2 p284 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Punarvasu Nakshatra
  **THEN** Lack of forethought and carefulness, susceptibility, disregard for people, a fickle mind and instability.
- **RBS-2-1170** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · RBS-2 p285 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu pada 1 (Gemini 20°00' to 23°20')
  **THEN** Hairy and fat-bodied with imbalances of air, wise but arrogant, passionate and quick-tempered; skilled, talented and creative, hasty at first but settling to succeed, makes friends easily, blessed with children and a dedicated wife, and knows how to make money without harming anybody; copper-red complexion and humorous nature.
- **TAM-RA-0193** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p131 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu with Jupiter yogakaraka and unafflicted by Venus, Rahu or Ketu
  **THEN** Exaggerates the native's conscience (vivek) - doing what he believes right even when unpopular or difficult.
- **TAM-RA-0194** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p131 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu for natives born on Aries, Leo, Sagittarius, Cancer, Scorpio or Pisces ascendant
  **THEN** Works wonders, as Jupiter is yogakaraka for all six of these ascendants.
- **TAM-RA-0204** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p140 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu compared with Rahu in Ashlesha, both in Cancer
  **THEN** Punarvasu's caring is of the heart and centred on values, principles and ethics (siddhant); Ashlesha's caring is of the head.
- **TAM-RA-0211** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p144 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu with Jupiter and Moon in positive houses and not in the 6th, 8th or 12th
  **THEN** Only then is the placement beneficial.
- **TAM-RA-0212** · nak Punarvasu, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p144 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Punarvasu with Moon in the 6th, 8th or 12th but receiving the 5th, 7th or 9th aspect of Jupiter
  **THEN** Rahu can still give positive results.
- **TRI-0322** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p98 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Governs all motion, movement and searching; carries the Sagittarian qualities of the arrow symbol.
- **TRI-0323** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p99 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu ruled by Aditi, the unbounded
  **THEN** Maternal, caring, sensitive, reasonable and accommodating; accommodates all people, situations, circumstances and energies as space accommodates all.
- **TRI-0325** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p100 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Nurturing and harmony; contented natives easily satisfied, believing in a simple spiritual life and working to shed negative energies obstructing evolution.
- **TRI-0326** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p100 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Mental and emotional in equal strength, producing reasonability; the ability to live in the moment while being friendly and good-natured is retained lifelong.
- **TRI-0330** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p101 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Quiet, patient, devout, temperate, amiable, adaptable, noble, holding contrary views; generosity is conditional and works on give-and-take, which accounts for the native's safety.
- **TRI-0331** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p101 · ~/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Lacks Ardra's mental capacity and has a simpler approach; lacks foresight and falls into complications, but is always lucky enough to escape unscathed.
- **TRI-0333** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p101 · +/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu prominent
  **THEN** Comfortable life with intermittent periods of adventure and upheaval.
- **TRI-0338** · nak Punarvasu · TRI p102 · -/stated
  **IF** Punarvasu in its negative aspect
  **THEN** Cannot discard anything for fear it may serve some future purpose; the evolved native knows when to let go.
- **TRI-0343** · nak Punarvasu, pada 1 · TRI p104 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet in Punarvasu pada 1 (20°00'-23°20' Gemini, Aries navamsa, Mars)
  **THEN** Moveable, adventurous and pioneering; focus on friendships, goals and group work.
- **TRI-0365** · nak Punarvasu, planet Moon · TRI p110 · +/classical
  **IF** Moon in Punarvasu (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Easily contented, self-controlled, slow-witted, fortunate and of good character.

## Pushya — nature (68)

- **ASH-0001** · nak Pushya · ASH p10 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Orthodox, passive temperament committed to social norms, caste/class distinctions and community rules; accepts differences without seeking change.
- **ASH-0002** · nak Pushya · ASH p10 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Upholds the peace and punishes those who defy with severe penalties, yet acts from care; moral character and spiritual awareness.
- **ASH-0003** · nak Pushya, planet Sun · ASH p10 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Pushya
  **THEN** Marked self-satisfaction and divine pride.
- **ASH-0008** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Life forces the native to rise above his past — poverty to riches, or criminal to saint; he overlooks his history and seldom holds himself responsible for past conduct.
- **ASH-0009** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Held in high regard for obstinacy, determination and commitment to the system of laws; enjoys the spotlight and can be egotistical.
- **ASH-0010** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Very responsive to praise or blame, which makes him seem stiff; grows more social later in life out of a wish to fit in.
- **ASH-0012** · nak Pushya, planet Mars · ASH p11 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Pushya
  **THEN** The fierce force of Mars disturbs Pushya and turns everything into a competition; without Mars the native avoids the unanticipated and competitive sport.
- **ASH-0015** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Takes on several errands, develops workaholism and exhausts himself with difficult tasks and long hours.
- **ASH-0018** · nak Pushya · ASH p11 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent, female native
  **THEN** Active in civic and social life, enjoys entertaining at home, well-read and cultivated; spends heavily on club and religious events and is submissive to her husband, withholding her opinions.
- **ASH-0672** · nak Pushya · ASH p197 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Benevolent, trustful, nurturing, liberal, learned, respected, of good character, spiritual, religious, careful, gentle, dedicated, wealthy, focused, hard-working, caring and patient.
- **ASH-0677** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Very practical, respects others' opinions, follows a Sattvic lifestyle and contributes to nurturing and nourishing life, with an emotionally balanced approach.
- **ASH-0678** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent, of either sex
  **THEN** Exhibits feminine qualities - emotionally sensitive, caring, affectionate and nurturing like a mother; Pushya treats the native like a female or mother.
- **ASH-0680** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent, in matters of service or goodness
  **THEN** Gives no importance to individuality or person-specificity - the motive is to relieve a large group, community or society where big interest is involved.
- **ASH-0685** · nak Pushya · ASH p202 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent (symbolised also by the lotus flourishing in mud)
  **THEN** Struggles at the initial stage of life and in childhood, but shines like a lotus in the later part of life - the difficult past inspiring him to reach his goal.
- **ASH-0695** · nak Pushya, pada 1 · ASH p204 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 1
  **THEN** Generous, kind and renowned for charitable work, handsome and fond of being the centre of attention - but often lonely and depressed in private life, and primarily materialistic.
- **ASH-0707** · nak Pushya, house 1 · ASH p205 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Very practical, respects others' opinions, follows a Sattvic lifestyle, and lives with connectivity, benevolence, servitude, wisdom, peace and emotional balance.
- **ASH-0708** · nak Pushya, house 1 · ASH p206 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya ascendant
  **THEN** A super-curious child who pauses to understand how things work and becomes uncomfortable when no solution is found; gives selflessly without questioning his own motive.
- **ASH-0717** · nak Pushya, planet Moon · ASH p207 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Pushya
  **THEN** Emotional stability and resilience - keeps cool in stressful situations and reassures others; enhanced intuition, deciding by instinct.
- **ASH-0722** · nak Pushya, planet Mercury · ASH p208 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Pushya
  **THEN** Conflict especially in decision-making - emotional decisions, overthinking and slow action that damage his own health, though he advises others well.
- **ASH-0733** · nak Pushya, planet Rahu · ASH p211 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Pushya
  **THEN** The native becomes hypocritical with an inner inferiority complex; presents his knowledge in an exaggerated manner to manipulate others, and deals with people manipulatively though without intent to harm.
- **AVA-2-1592** · nak Pushya · AVA-2 p186 · +/classical
  **IF** Pushya placement anywhere
  **THEN** Brahmavarchasa shakti, the power to create spiritual energy and to feed the soul as well as the body: devoted, dependable, disciplined and quietly generous, the ones others lean on, gifted at teaching, guiding and holding a family or community together. Long held the most auspicious of all nakshatras. The governing word is nourishment.
- **AVA-2-1593** · nak Pushya · AVA-2 p186 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya under affliction
  **THEN** Controlling, narrow and unable to let those it protects grow up; it can smother what it feeds, cling to its charges and grow rigid, cautious and self-denying.
- **AVA-2-1598** · nak Pushya · AVA-2 p187 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Pushya compared
  **THEN** They move from dignity to service to social charm to the occult and the responsibility deepens with each one.
- **AVA-2-1599** · nak Pushya, pada 1 · AVA-2 p187 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 1, 03°20'-06°40' Cancer, Leo navamsha, Sun
  **THEN** Achievement-oriented, drawn to the limelight, recognition and status - this quarter provides from a position rather than from the background; wealth and material prosperity and real pride in family, lineage and ancestry.
- **AVA-2-1600** · nak Pushya, pada 1 · AVA-2 p187 · +/classical
  **IF** Pushya pada 1
  **THEN** Intelligent and intuitive, with a scholarly reputation and respect in society; kind, supportive and nurturing - a fatherly or authority figure to others; gains support from parents and from figures of authority.
- **AVA-2-1608** · nak Pushya, pada 2 · AVA-2 p188 · +/classical
  **IF** Pushya pada 2
  **THEN** Talkative and communicative, easy-going and adaptable and a steady, persistent worker; humorous and entertaining; wealth and material comfort, luxury and a stable lifestyle.
- **AVA-2-1613** · nak Pushya, pada 3 · AVA-2 p189 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 3, 10°00'-13°20' Cancer, Libra navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** Social and outwardly interactive with external charm - the nourishing instinct turns towards society rather than the household and this quarter provides in public and enjoys the credit; patient and enduring, with a desire for fame and recognition.
- **AVA-2-1614** · nak Pushya, pada 3 · AVA-2 p189 · ~/classical
  **IF** Pushya pada 3
  **THEN** Socially adaptable, with kindness and selflessness that can be superficial, covering an inner sense of superiority; less attachment to one's own community than to the wider circle and an inclination towards personal comfort.
- **AVA-2-1619** · nak Pushya, pada 4 · AVA-2 p189 · -/classical
  **IF** Pushya pada 4
  **THEN** Engaged at times in hidden or undercover activity; selfish tendencies; enjoys the present without planning for the future.
- **AVA-2-1699** · nak Pushya, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p195 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Pushya aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Does good deeds for society; hot-tempered and not very attractive.
- **AVA-2-1702** · nak Pushya, pada 1, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p195 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Pushya pada 1
  **THEN** Not a good position: the most selfish of natures; middle stature, languid eyes; poor rapport with relatives - yet courageous and earns wealth by self-effort.
- **AVA-2-1719** · nak Pushya, house 1 · AVA-2 p197 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Pushya
  **THEN** A strong sense of duty forms early and the personality is mature, serious and dependable - the caregiver who supports others emotionally and materially, often before he is old enough for the role; development is slow but steady and early struggle gives way to gradual rise.
- **AVA-2-1749** · nak Pushya, planet Moon · AVA-2 p202 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Pushya
  **THEN** A natural nurturer and caregiver, outwardly stable and inwardly burdened - the most characteristic combination in the whole nakshatra; the native gives care and does not feel fully cared for in return and seeks security through responsibility rather than through feeling.
- **AVA-2-1766** · nak Pushya, house 1 · AVA-2 p205 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Pushya
  **THEN** This native is the one others lean on from an early age and the role is carried with a steadiness that looks effortless outside and weighs heavily within; the presence is calm, mature and reassuring and the identity is built rather than given. Dashas touching this degree add responsibility before they add reward.
- **NAT-0288** · nak Pushya, planet Moon · NAT p203 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Pushya (3°20'-16°40' Cancer)
  **THEN** Nourishment, protection and spiritual wisdom; the sustaining power of care, devotion and continuity that makes what is nurtured flourish.
- **NAT-0290** · nak Pushya · NAT p203 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya's tie to Cancer's stickiness
  **THEN** Duality of attachment and detachment: unwavering support alongside a tendency to cling to the familiar; abundance without moderation leads to stagnation.
- **NAT-0293** · nak Pushya · NAT p205 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya's giving without boundaries, per the cow's udder
  **THEN** Depletion; the giver must be nourished too, so self-care must accompany the instinct to nurture.
- **NAT-0294** · nak Pushya · NAT p205 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent (archetype of the Sacred Provider)
  **THEN** Steps into roles of teacher, caregiver, leader and protector, offering material and emotional stability to family, community and institution.
- **NAT-0295** · nak Pushya · NAT p206 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya's role as provider unbalanced
  **THEN** Overextension, giving more than received, depletion and frustration; duty hardens into rigidity and resistance to change.
- **NAT-0296** · nak Pushya · NAT p206 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya without self-awareness
  **THEN** Paternalism, moral superiority and excessive attachment to tradition; becomes an enabler, mistaking control for care and imposing ideals on others.
- **NAT-0305** · nak Pushya, pada 1 · NAT p211 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 1 (3°20'-6°40' Cancer), Leo navamsa, Sun-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Leadership, confidence and purpose-driven protective care; compassionate leadership with clarity and warmth, tending to overprotectiveness.
- **NAT-0306** · nak Pushya, pada 2 · NAT p212 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 2 (6°40'-10°00' Cancer), Virgo navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Emotional sensitivity grounded into methodical caregiving; excels at creating stability and managing resources, but risks losing empathy to perfectionism.
- **NAT-0307** · nak Pushya, pada 3 · NAT p212 · ~/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 3 (10°00'-13°20' Cancer), Libra navamsa, Venus-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Relational harmony, artistic creativity and emotional adaptability; vibrant social connection, though authenticity can be sacrificed for surface harmony.
- **NAT-0308** · nak Pushya, pada 4 · NAT p212 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya pada 4 (13°20'-16°40' Cancer), Scorpio navamsa, Mars-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Fiery determination and emotional depth turned to profound healing, resilience and transformative leadership; nurture merged with spiritual fulfilment.
- **NAT-0311** · nak Pushya · NAT p213 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya's sattvic guna in excess
  **THEN** Self-neglect and emotional overextension.
- **NAT-0952** · nak Pushya, planet Ketu · NAT p585 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Pushya
  **THEN** A soul that already mastered emotional intelligence, nurturance and structured learning in past lifetimes; must now shift toward concrete structured achievement rather than rely on innate wisdom.
- **RBS-1-1278** · nak Pushya · RBS-1 p179 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Pushya
  **THEN** Caring, generous, soft spoken, helpful, ethical and kind; even males give motherly care to their children; reliable and good friends in the hour of need; emotional and caring through Cancer yet disciplined, philosophical and practical through Saturn.
- **RBS-1-1286** · nak Pushya · RBS-1 p179 · -/stated
  **IF** Male born in Pushya
  **THEN** Very weak-hearted and unable to reach a conclusion; his outward expression is hypocritically positive while his inner one is negative; fond of good dress.
- **RBS-1-1287** · nak Pushya · RBS-1 p179 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Pushya
  **THEN** Peace-minded, submissive to elders yet oppressed by all; sincere, affectionate but moody, religious, god-fearing, conservative and methodical.
- **RBS-1-1297** · nak Pushya · RBS-1 p180 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Pushya — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Benevolent, philanthropic and humanitarian, cool and well mannered, protective against destruction and violence, creative and expansive; but zealously protective of family and community, orthodox, narrow-minded, possessive, conservative and prejudiced.
- **RBS-2-0563** · nak Pushya, planet Saturn · RBS-2 p143 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Pushya Nakshatra, ruled by Saturn, symbol the flower
  **THEN** An intrinsic thirst for knowledge; plodding, sincere, dedicated, hard-working and extremely patient.
- **RBS-2-1176** · nak Pushya · RBS-2 p286 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Pushya Nakshatra
  **THEN** Caring, generous, soft-spoken, helpful, ethical and kind; even males give motherly care to their children, and they prove reliable friends in the hour of need.
- **RBS-2-1185** · nak Pushya, planet Moon · RBS-2 p287 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Pushya Nakshatra
  **THEN** A composed mind, learned, wealthy, virtuous and liked by all.
- **RBS-2-1192** · nak Pushya · RBS-2 p288 · -/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Pushya
  **THEN** Very weak of heart and unable to reach a conclusion; his outward expression is hypocritical, positive outside while negative within; fond of good dress.
- **RBS-2-1193** · nak Pushya · RBS-2 p288 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Pushya
  **THEN** Peace-minded and submissive to elders but oppressed by all; sincere and affectionate though moody, religious and god-fearing, respectful of elders, conservative and methodical.
- **RBS-2-1202** · nak Pushya · RBS-2 p289 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Pushya Nakshatra
  **THEN** Benevolent, philanthropic and humanitarian, cool and well mannered, protective against destruction and violence, with inherent qualities of creation and expansion.
- **RBS-2-1203** · nak Pushya · RBS-2 p289 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Pushya Nakshatra
  **THEN** Zealously protective of family, society or community, orthodox, narrow-minded, possessive, conservative and prejudiced.
- **TAM-RA-0225** · nak Pushya, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p154 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rebellious Rahu in the highly traditional nakshatra Pushya
  **THEN** Native questions age-old traditions and customs, and where he finds only superstition he rebels ferociously against practices that exploit society.
- **TAM-RA-0226** · nak Pushya, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p154 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Pushya nakshatra
  **THEN** Native loves the truth and states facts about himself with unusual honesty.
- **TAM-RA-0227** · nak Pushya, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p155 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Pushya nakshatra
  **THEN** Humility and honesty come naturally to the native.
- **TAM-RA-0235** · nak Pushya, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p158 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Pushya nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings the illusion 'I am the doer'; this doership breeds an egocentric person and ultimately proves fatal for the native.
- **TRI-0368** · nak Pushya · TRI p113 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Prosperous, courteous and refined; no place in it for brutality, savagery, vulgarity or animalistic behaviour; an obsessive streak for round things.
- **TRI-0370** · nak Pushya · TRI p114 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Maternal, kind, helpful, generous, protective and nourishing; the most nourishing of all nakshatras; natives usually end up in comfortable positions in life.
- **TRI-0376** · nak Pushya · TRI p114 · -/stated
  **IF** Pushya in its negative aspect
  **THEN** So comfortable in its own world that it becomes rigid, narrow-minded and orthodox toward anything outside it — especially in religion, culture and social convention; prejudice, over-protection and bigotry.
- **TRI-0377** · nak Pushya · TRI p115 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya prominent
  **THEN** Balanced and even approach with realism and sanity; reacts reasonably to most situations; the most helpful of the nakshatras, helpfulness being its primary concern.
- **TRI-0386** · nak Pushya · TRI p116 · -/stated
  **IF** Afflicted planets occupying Pushya
  **THEN** Promotes laziness, listlessness and paralysing idealism.
- **TRI-0409** · nak Pushya · TRI p121 · +/stated
  **IF** Pushya as Moon plus Saturn
  **THEN** Emotional stability — mind joined to matter; gives easy material expansion on the dictates of the mind.
- **TRI-0420** · nak Pushya, planet Moon · TRI p125 · +/classical
  **IF** Moon in Pushya (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Lucky, learned, wealthy, ethical and of a peaceful nature.

## Ashlesha — nature (94)

- **ASH-0064** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p54 · -/classical
  **IF** Born in Ashlesha (BPHS ch. 81)
  **THEN** Walks slowly, inactive, flat eyes, cruel, angry, jealous, a donor, a masseur, a sufferer, and full of habits and fondnesses.
- **ASH-0745** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p215 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, intuitive, wise, calculative, mentally active and observant - but tricky, dishonest, lavish, perilous, clingy, angry and ungrateful.
- **ASH-0752** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p218 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Difficulty aligning with the external world, so others perceive the native as mysterious; high intelligence able to analyse beyond a common person's understanding.
- **ASH-0753** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p218 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Restless from continuous thought, becoming irritated and aggressive because he assumes others must match his intelligence; feels untuned with others, giving a sense of superiority, argument and rigidity.
- **ASH-0754** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p218 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent (snake qualities manifested)
  **THEN** Poisonous, deceiving and enthralling; lazy and passive, appearing normal and patient but extremely alert once threatened, able to stay hidden long with little food and water, with a strong sense of smell and keen observation.
- **ASH-0755** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Extraordinary mental strength shown in intelligence, communication and an ambiguous way of self-expression; resists thoughts that do not suit people's norms, yet is contemporary, adaptable and progressive.
- **ASH-0756** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Secretive in grabbing opportunities without disclosing them even to the closest, reacting in surprising and unexpected ways.
- **ASH-0758** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Very diplomatic and hard to understand, presenting himself as polite and knowledgeable to create an illusion; seriously image-conscious and works to maintain that image even when his true nature is recognised.
- **ASH-0762** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p220 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Sometimes a vicious opportunist through high manipulative skill, emotional quotient and fascinating gestures.
- **ASH-0763** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p220 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent (Garuda qualities)
  **THEN** Special vibrant intense energy with excellent intuition and premonition; far-sighted, watching closely from a height, remaining alone or in isolation but attacking by surprise, fighting to the last with strong determination.
- **ASH-0765** · nak Ashlesha · ASH p220 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Quite rigid, aggressive and prejudiced, insisting on his own view and arguing to the last - which positions him as argumentative or rude; takes actions that shock others and pursues spirituality on unconventional paths.
- **ASH-0770** · nak Ashlesha, pada 2 · ASH p221 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 2
  **THEN** Childlike behaviour with boss and team, behaves emotionally to gain status through manipulative words or action, serves people like his own family and stays attached to society, showing confidence and power in action.
- **ASH-0773** · nak Ashlesha, pada 4 · ASH p222 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 4, the gandanta pada, where Mars is exalted at 28 degrees
  **THEN** The native is good at heart and pure with no cunning mentality, serving people with the right intention through knowledge and spirituality.
- **ASH-0775** · nak Ashlesha, house 1 · ASH p223 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Enigmatic or deceiving appearance, reticent and mysterious, not readily disclosing genuine intent; appears passive or careless while remaining fully alert mentally.
- **ASH-0781** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · ASH p225 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Deals with people cleverly and tactfully, using emotional ground to grow the seeds of his dreams, while a sense of insecurity follows him.
- **ASH-0784** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mars · ASH p225 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ashlesha (Cancer being Mars's debilitation sign)
  **THEN** Not a good placement - makes the native cowardly, insecure and psychologically stressed, behaving violently or aggressively to deal with insecurity and wasting energy on the wrong people.
- **ASH-0788** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mercury · ASH p226 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Clouded mentality that hinders decision-making and the expression of feeling; stressed about the future through insecurity and fear.
- **ASH-0801** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · ASH p229 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Deals with people tactfully and diplomatically, displaying emotion to get work done or to cheat; creates an illusory environment to make others feel loved, with manipulative conduct and hypnotic speech.
- **ASH-0802** · nak Ashlesha, planet Ketu · ASH p230 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Inconsistent thought and secretive behaviour with a nostalgic effect in his actions; expresses opinion lethargically, assuming others already know, and appears irritated or unresponsive though not jealous at heart.
- **AVA-2-1797** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p211 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha placement anywhere
  **THEN** Visasleshana shakti, the power to inflict poison and in the same motion to hold and to heal with it: the mind as a weapon - it watches, calculates, waits and strikes only when it chooses; the serpent moves silently, senses danger instantly and protects what it must. The governing word is entwining.
- **AVA-2-1798** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p211 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha under affliction
  **THEN** Deceptive, clinging, secretive and self-destructive, biting the very hand it holds; at its best it is the wise counsellor and the healer who understands poison because it understands the depths.
- **AVA-2-1800** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p212 · +/stated
  **IF** Any Ashlesha placement
  **THEN** The native cannot easily be trapped, cornered or blackmailed and always finds an unconventional route to survive and win; standard processes are ignored in favour of shortcuts and hidden routes, with smart work chosen over hard work, so results arrive by methods nobody was watching.
- **AVA-2-1801** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p212 · +/stated
  **IF** Any Ashlesha placement
  **THEN** The intuition is extreme - the native senses problems before they arise and reads people's true intentions early; alongside runs the instinct to guard, keeper of knowledge and resources, sharing nothing easily.
- **AVA-2-1802** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p212 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha power used unconsciously
  **THEN** Hunter and hunted are the same energy - the native can manipulate or be manipulated and the power backfires whenever it is used unconsciously; at its lowest this is fear and control, at its highest wisdom, intuition and spiritual awakening. Ashlesha sharpens a planet's mind; it does not soften a cruel one.
- **AVA-2-1803** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p212 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Ashlesha compared
  **THEN** They move from struggle to ambition to secrecy to illusion, the last ending at the gandanta, the most sensitive junction in the zodiac.
- **AVA-2-1805** · nak Ashlesha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p212 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 1, 16°40'-20°00' Cancer, Sagittarius navamsha, Jupiter
  **THEN** Extremely hardworking, capable of sustained effort; expansion through struggle, with a strong sixth-house flavour - enemies, disease, conflict and obstacles - together with the ability to face adversity and overcome it; inclines towards service, healing and problem-solving.
- **AVA-2-1809** · nak Ashlesha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p213 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 1 unrefined
  **THEN** Argument for its own sake and a life organised around opposition.
- **AVA-2-1811** · nak Ashlesha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p213 · -/classical
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 2
  **THEN** A strongly materialistic approach, with a tendency towards trickery, deceit and manipulation and a desire to achieve goals at any cost; a hoarding streak makes it hard to let go of possessions.
- **AVA-2-1816** · nak Ashlesha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p214 · -/classical
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 3
  **THEN** A highly inventive, strategic mind that goes to extreme lengths in planning and execution; a pull towards scheming and plotting; naturally secretive, revengeful and psychologically intense.
- **AVA-2-1821** · nak Ashlesha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p214 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 4, 26°40'-30°00' Cancer, Pisces navamsha, Jupiter, gandanta
  **THEN** The peak of illusion and confusion: maya and moral conflict dominate and the serpent energy dissolves - the native shifts from deceiver to deceived. This is the gandanta, the final and most sensitive junction of Cancer and the most delicate quarter in the chapter.
- **AVA-2-1822** · nak Ashlesha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p215 · -/classical
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 4
  **THEN** Constantly confused between right and wrong; lives behind multiple layers of personality, hiding the true nature and keeping up false appearances - and is easily deceived or misled by others in turn; the mind is imbalanced and confused, with a strong internal moral struggle and emotional instability.
- **AVA-2-1841** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p216 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha aspected or conjunct Jupiter
  **THEN** Well-behaved, good in conversation and debate and blessed with a happy life.
- **AVA-2-1925** · nak Ashlesha, house 1 · AVA-2 p223 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Ashlesha
  **THEN** The personality becomes serpent-like - strategic, observant and psychologically dominant; nothing is approached directly, everything through analysis, anticipation and mental positioning; very hard to deceive and rather tiring to be close to. Trust is given slowly and withdrawn quickly.
- **AVA-2-1926** · nak Ashlesha, house 1 · AVA-2 p223 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Ashlesha, at its higher versus lower level
  **THEN** At the higher level deep intuition and genuine self-mastery; at the lower, overthinking, paranoia and a manipulative streak the native rarely recognises in himself.
- **AVA-2-1949** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p228 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha
  **THEN** The mind becomes deeply sensitive, intuitive and psychologically complex, constantly analysing emotions - heightened awareness together with genuine inner unrest and a strong tendency towards suspicion, emotional guarding and overthinking; the danger-sensing ability is strongest in the mind here but tips readily into anxiety, mood swings and mental fatigue.
- **AVA-2-1951** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p228 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Energy becomes strategic rather than direct - the native rarely fights openly, preferring tactics, anticipation and at times manipulation; aggression is controlled and hidden, which makes it more intense than open anger and less predictable.
- **AVA-2-1958** · nak Ashlesha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p229 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Deep karmic burdens around fear, control and survival, with a constant need to stay cautious, anticipate problems and protect the self; the danger-sensing ability is highly active in career and long-term planning, making the native difficult to catch out - and breeding anxiety, rigidity and emotional isolation as its permanent cost.
- **AVA-2-1960** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p230 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu afflicted in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Getting trapped in illusion, overplaying the psychological games until they are visible and creating self-made complications; life may bring sudden ups and downs, karmic entanglement and unconventional paths.
- **AVA-2-1962** · nak Ashlesha, house 1 · AVA-2 p231 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Ashlesha
  **THEN** The native meets the world side-on, never head-on - watching first, revealing little and moving only when the ground is read; the presence is quiet and hard to place and people sense they are being assessed. Dashas touching this degree sharpen the strategic instinct rather than soften it.
- **AVA-2-23266** · nak Ashlesha · AVA-2 p696 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha influence in the chart
  **THEN** Cultivating gratitude and learning to face reality exactly as it comes, rather than as one would like it to be, brings purity and positivity.
- **NAT-0315** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · NAT p217 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha (16°40'-30°00' Cancer)
  **THEN** Intuition, hidden knowledge and self-mastery; quiet intensity where deep insight and profound personal growth unfold.
- **NAT-0316** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p217 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha presided over by Ahisarpa, the serpent deity
  **THEN** Holds the dualities of protection and destruction, wisdom and temptation; guardian of hidden treasure and sacred knowledge.
- **NAT-0317** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p218 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha, per Siva holding the Halahala poison
  **THEN** Capacity to hold and master destructive force through restraint, enduring life's toxicity without succumbing to it.
- **NAT-0318** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p218 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha, the birth naksatra of Laksmana
  **THEN** Unwavering loyalty and relentless protective instinct, sacrificing personal comfort for those guarded; but a fiery temper and impatience turn protection into impulsive anger and overreaction.
- **NAT-0319** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha symbolised by the coiled serpent
  **THEN** Latent power waiting to be harnessed; strikes decisively when needed and retreats inward for regeneration and self-reflection.
- **NAT-0320** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p219 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha's serpent shedding its skin
  **THEN** Confronts fear and vulnerability, releases outdated patterns, and uses adversity as the catalyst for transformation.
- **NAT-0323** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p220 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent (archetype of the Mystical Protector)
  **THEN** Deeply protective, driven to safeguard loved ones, spiritual ideals or hidden knowledge, even to the point of self-sacrifice.
- **NAT-0325** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mercury · NAT p221 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha's connection to Mercury
  **THEN** Sharp intellect and effective communication, but a tension between light and darkness; jealousy, betrayal and vengefulness can be transmuted into compassion, insight and healing.
- **NAT-0326** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p221 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha's intensity guided by wisdom
  **THEN** Navigates complexity with precision and strategy; senses danger and anticipates challenge before others.
- **NAT-0328** · nak Ashlesha · NAT p222 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha's protective force without healthy boundaries
  **THEN** Nurturing and safeguarding devolve into control and entanglement; true protection fosters freedom, not possession.
- **NAT-0336** · nak Ashlesha, pada 1 · NAT p226 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 1 (16°40'-20°00' Cancer), Sagittarius navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Wisdom, optimism and higher understanding; emotional intensity aligned to philosophical or spiritual ideals, with recklessness and overextension as the risk.
- **NAT-0337** · nak Ashlesha, pada 2 · NAT p227 · +/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 2 (20°00'-23°20' Cancer), Capricorn navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Intuitive and strategic energy channelled into material pursuit and long-term planning; builds solid foundations, but Saturn amplifies emotional restraint and detachment.
- **NAT-0338** · nak Ashlesha, pada 3 · NAT p227 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 3 (23°20'-26°40' Cancer), Aquarius navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Visionary humanitarian bent; analytical nature turned to collaborative and community work, though emotional detachment can result.
- **NAT-0339** · nak Ashlesha, pada 4 · NAT p227 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha pada 4 (26°40'-30°00' Cancer), Pisces navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Intense emotional sensitivity, spiritual introspection and healing power; liberation through empathy, but risks excessive introspection and detachment from practical reality.
- **NAT-0943** · nak Ashlesha, planet Ketu · NAT p577 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Adds an intuitive, deeply introspective and self-protective dimension to the psyche; highly attuned to emotional undercurrents beneath the surface.
- **RBS-1-1305** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-1 p181 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashlesha, ruled by Mercury with the serpent Shesha Naga as deity, symbolised by a coiled snake
  **THEN** Penetrating eyes, sharp insight, strong intuition, cunning and a secretive nature; the native clings to an idea or a person with a show of intense love, and uses a poisonous cunning intellect to defeat enemies.
- **RBS-1-1308** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-1 p182 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashlesha, the most mean and selfish nakshatra of the zodiac
  **THEN** He appears humorous but is cold-blooded and ruthless; no emotional relationship is to be expected from him, and he reacts opposite to what the situation demands.
- **RBS-1-1310** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-1 p182 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha afflicted in the chart
  **THEN** The native becomes a big crook, greedy, alcoholic and of loose character, follows an illicit profession with black money, or loses the track of life entirely; in some cases serious psychological disorders appear.
- **RBS-1-1323** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-1 p183 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashlesha — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Intuition, ability to overcome danger, planning, cool calculation, courage and leadership; but cold ruthlessness, deceit, suspicion, cunning and merciless guile, dependence on others, plotting, dishonesty, falsehood, selfishness, ingratitude, depression, anxiety, schizophrenia and total absence of benevolence.
- **RBS-1-1328** · nak Ashlesha, pada 2 · RBS-1 p183 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashlesha pada 2, where the negative traits are strongest
  **THEN** Highly shrewd and unhesitating in pulling anybody down to reach his ends; the most difficult pada to handle and such people cannot be trusted; boiled-egg-like body, protruding veins and a crow-like appearance; they mostly have no house of their own and live in rented flats.
- **RBS-1-1330** · nak Ashlesha, pada 4 · RBS-1 p184 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Ashlesha pada 4
  **THEN** Here the Ashlesha native is himself the victim rather than the victimiser and must work hard to maintain any relationship; if afflicted it gives serious psychological problems; fat body, broad chest, fair complexion, long lips and fish-like eyes.
- **RBS-2-0446** · nak Ashlesha, pada 1 · RBS-2 p127 · +/classical
  **IF** Birth in the 1st pada of Ashlesha
  **THEN** Highly auspicious effects.
- **RBS-2-0509** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p134 · -/classical
  **IF** Birth in ghatika 16-24 of Ashlesha, up to Cancer 22°00' (Patra, leaf)
  **THEN** Danger.
- **RBS-2-0566** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mercury · RBS-2 p144 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashlesha Nakshatra, ruled by Mercury, symbol the serpent
  **THEN** Youthful and charming in appearance, with penetrating insight into people's motives, and very tactful in winning over opponents.
- **RBS-2-1214** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mercury · RBS-2 p291 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashlesha Nakshatra, ruled by Mercury with the serpent Shesha as deity and the coiled snake as symbol
  **THEN** Penetrating eyes, sharp insight, strong intuition, cunning and a secretive nature.
- **RBS-2-1217** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p291 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha native in relationships
  **THEN** Over-sensitive to the environment and trusting no one, always finding something to doubt in everybody including close friends and relatives, and so suspicious that he may hire a detective to watch his spouse.
- **RBS-2-1220** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p291 · -/stated
  **IF** Natural benefic planets placed in Ashlesha Nakshatra
  **THEN** The native is himself victimised by others instead of victimising them.
- **RBS-2-1221** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p291 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha native in company and in reaction
  **THEN** May have a bad friend circle involved in illegal activities, and reacts exactly opposite to what the situation demands, showing no joy at auspicious news and smiling at a death.
- **RBS-2-1226** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p292 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashlesha Nakshatra
  **THEN** A born leader, daring, angry, wicked and harmful to people; suffers from sex starvation, pays fines for sinful acts, spends wealth on evil purposes and loses on investments; can hypnotise anyone with a glare and use it for black magic; cold-blooded, extremely sexual, always sad, highly stingy and at enmity with his own people.
- **RBS-2-1234** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p293 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Ashlesha
  **THEN** Shy with morals of a very high order, enjoying good respect and recognition from relatives, and conquering her enemies.
- **RBS-2-1241** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p293 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Ashlesha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Intuition, ability to overcome situations steeped in danger, planning, cool calculation, courage and a leadership disposition.
- **RBS-2-1242** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p293 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Ashlesha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Cold ruthlessness, deceit and an aura of suspicion, unattractive features, cunning and merciless guile, insidious harmfulness, dependence on others, plotting, dishonesty, miserliness, selfishness, ingratitude, depression, anxiety, schizophrenia and total absence of benevolence.
- **RBS-2-1245** · nak Ashlesha · RBS-2 p294 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha, a Rakshasa star of sharp nature symbolised by the coiled snake
  **THEN** Suggests an anti-social and tactless character, penetrating in knowledge and austere in habit; it can bring pain but also good transcendental aspirations.
- **RBS-2-1247** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · RBS-2 p294 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Insincere, unable to control eating, sinful, deceitful and ungrateful.
- **RBS-2-1252** · nak Ashlesha, pada 2 · RBS-2 p294 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha pada 2 (Cancer 20°00' to 23°20')
  **THEN** Ugly, dirty, uncharitable, foolish, sickly and unmotivated; the most difficult pada, highly shrewd and ready to pull anybody down, never to be trusted; boiled-egg-like body, protruding veins and crow-like appearance, and usually owning no house of his own.
- **TAM-RA-0205** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p141 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native goes on worrying - a worry of the head which he mistakes for caring, and which only makes him worry more.
- **TAM-RA-0239** · nak Ashlesha · TAM-RA p163 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha, the clinging star
  **THEN** Clinging is the nature of the native, and one only loses what one clings to.
- **TAM-RA-0241** · nak Ashlesha · TAM-RA p164 · -/classical
  **IF** Ashlesha nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** The ancient seers held Ashlesha to be a not-so-good nakshatra, since its clinging nature attracts more misery into life.
- **TAM-RA-0243** · nak Ashlesha · TAM-RA p165 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha, an emotional-fool nakshatra
  **THEN** Native confuses love with attachment and passes through many agonies and sufferings, primarily on the mental rather than the physical level.
- **TAM-RA-0247** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p166 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha in the chart of a meditative being
  **THEN** The intense emotional nature it brings is good, because daily meditation cleanses the dirt accumulated on the mind.
- **TAM-RA-0249** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p167 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha in the chart of a less evolved being
  **THEN** Leads to sustained emotional turmoil in life, because the native has never made effort to purify his mind.
- **TAM-RA-0251** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p168 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings wild imagination and unique ideas to the brain (Mercury).
- **TAM-RA-0258** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p171 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is obsessed with security and rechecks locked doors before bed; Rahu brings excessive fear of being attacked by outsiders.
- **TAM-RA-0259** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p171 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native has the capacity to transform emotional vulnerability into emotional intelligence.
- **TAM-RA-0269** · nak Ashlesha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p175 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Ashlesha nakshatra
  **THEN** Overall a highly sensitive and emotional person, a man of letters, with the potential to connect with the supernatural world.
- **TRI-0422** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p127 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Secretive, creepy, wily, cold-blooded, deceptive, insincere, hypnotic and poisonous.
- **TRI-0425** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p129 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** The most difficult of all nakshatra energies to handle and channel correctly; even the ancient sages had few kind words for it.
- **TRI-0428** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p129 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Puts up socially acceptable fronts and uses courtesy to flatter, turning cold and ruthless once the objective is achieved; always scheming to climb the social ladder.
- **TRI-0429** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p129 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** Constant suspicion and mistrust in the background; worry and caution follow the native relentlessly.
- **TRI-0438** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p130 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** The hoarders of the zodiac; dislikes throwing anything away and goes to real extremes in this.
- **TRI-0445** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p131 · ~/classical
  **IF** Ashlesha — female nakshatra
  **THEN** Even male natives strongly under Ashlesha have a feminine manner; its intuitive, perceptive and deceptive qualities are of the feminine type.
- **TRI-0461** · nak Ashlesha, planet Mercury · TRI p135 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha ruled by Mercury with Moon
  **THEN** Highlights Mercury's negative traits — evasiveness, deception, deviousness; its alacrity and calculation serve selfish ends, though its true function is for intellect to control and direct the mind.
- **TRI-0462** · nak Ashlesha · TRI p135 · -/stated
  **IF** Ashlesha prominent
  **THEN** The battleground where intellect wars with emotion, conscious with subconscious, reasonability with primevality; its turbulent energies make it susceptible to decadence and the wrong path.
- **TRI-0474** · nak Ashlesha, planet Moon · TRI p138 · -/classical
  **IF** Moon in Ashlesha (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** False, wicked, ungrateful, crafty and gluttonous, eating everything in sight.

## Magha — nature (66)

- **ASH-0808** · nak Magha · ASH p232 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent
  **THEN** Attractive, smart, affectionate, energetic, dutiful, optimistic, honest, motivated, honoured, success-oriented, independent, reputation-conscious, alert, religious and ethical, with connections to authority - but angry, reserved and egoistic.
- **ASH-0815** · nak Magha · ASH p234 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent (Sun's moolatrikona in Leo aligned with Mars and Ketu)
  **THEN** Quite aggressive, creative, forceful, dominating, courageous, egoistic and spiritual, with strong energy to react.
- **ASH-0818** · nak Magha · ASH p234 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent, being a Gandmula nakshatra ruled by Ketu
  **THEN** Strong psychological powers and intense energy; sharp observation and mystical nature, or else headless, numb, eccentric and surrendered.
- **ASH-0819** · nak Magha · ASH p234 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Isolated, disengaged, indecisive or wandering; appears inactive, useless or cloudy in thinking, inclined to spirituality, saints, occult, ghosts, pilgrimage or black magic.
- **ASH-0821** · nak Magha · ASH p235 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent
  **THEN** Clear vision to uplift the needy and expects adherence to the system; avoids hypocrisy through a strong sense of authenticity and love of truth, with strong confidence and vitality.
- **ASH-0824** · nak Magha · ASH p236 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha native not receiving appropriate honour, recognition, status or entitlement
  **THEN** Becomes demoralised, though he is capable of initiating action to protect and benefit others selflessly.
- **ASH-0826** · nak Magha · ASH p236 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent
  **THEN** Very ambitious, authoritative and loyal; the soul concretises to manifest its full potential and accomplish its mission.
- **ASH-0827** · nak Magha · ASH p236 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent, despite material success
  **THEN** Discontentment in life; fails to resist others' opinions openly or accept them easily, and fails to meet the targets he has set for himself.
- **ASH-0829** · nak Magha · ASH p236 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha native persisting in hatred deep in his heart
  **THEN** Troubles in life; he can enjoy life easily only by giving up the revengeful attitude against those who disrespect tradition and rules.
- **ASH-0834** · nak Magha, pada 2 · ASH p237 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 2 (Taurus navamsha, Moon exalted)
  **THEN** The ego subsides and politeness appears; the native becomes wealthy, good-looking and attractive, though he remains isolated or unfocused - and after twenty years life turns dramatically positive.
- **ASH-0841** · nak Magha, pada 4 · ASH p238 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 4 (Cancer navamsha)
  **THEN** Quite sensitive, protective and supportive at heart though appearing rude or rigid externally; respects intellectuals, brahmins, rituals and ancestral tradition.
- **ASH-0846** · nak Magha, house 1 · ASH p239 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha ascendant
  **THEN** Strong moral principles and traditional, sometimes conservative views; generous and ready to support the needy despite a high sense of self-importance.
- **ASH-0848** · nak Magha, planet Sun · ASH p239 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Magha
  **THEN** Egoistic and power-hungry; big ambitions kept hidden while he works secretly, feeling discomfort and irritation when others are not in tune with him.
- **ASH-0849** · nak Magha, planet Moon · ASH p240 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Magha
  **THEN** The mind inclines to power and royalty; the native invests his life in searching for truth and holds great respect for his ancestors, feeling content only with authority to direct and empower people.
- **ASH-0865** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · ASH p244 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha
  **THEN** The native becomes hypocritical with a false ego and the intention of cheating others; pretends to be powerful and a central figure, and does not learn from past mistakes, repeating them without remorse.
- **AVA-2-1994** · nak Magha · AVA-2 p237 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha placement anywhere
  **THEN** Tyage kshepani shakti, the power to leave the body - the soul's passage linking each life to the ancestral line behind it; natives carry natural authority, pride, loyalty to tradition and a strong sense of duty to the family name. The governing word is ancestry.
- **AVA-2-1995** · nak Magha · AVA-2 p237 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha under affliction
  **THEN** Arrogant, status-hungry and contemptuous, demanding respect it has not earned; at its best it is the noble leader who upholds a lineage and lifts those beneath it.
- **AVA-2-1999** · nak Magha · AVA-2 p238 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha placement, humility versus ego
  **THEN** Power is the promise and stability depends on humility: where the native honours the throne the results hold, where the crown swells the head Magha takes back what it gave. Magha crowns a planet; it does not reform a corrupt one.
- **AVA-2-2000** · nak Magha · AVA-2 p238 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Magha compared
  **THEN** They trace the life of a king in order: the throne is won, the power is consolidated, the mind is cultivated and the lineage is honoured.
- **AVA-2-2001** · nak Magha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p238 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 1, 00°00'-03°20' Leo, Aries navamsha, Mars, gandanta
  **THEN** The will-power quarter - the throne achieved after defeating powerful opposition; self-assertion, leadership, courage and idealism express strongly and the nature is action-oriented, aggressive and authoritative with a natural pull towards taking charge.
- **AVA-2-2003** · nak Magha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p238 · +/classical
  **IF** Magha pada 1
  **THEN** Strong will and an authority-driven personality that dominates situations rather than negotiating them; a business-oriented mindset and a generous, charitable disposition that shows respect to the respectable.
- **AVA-2-2014** · nak Magha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p240 · ~/classical
  **IF** Magha pada 3
  **THEN** Prompt in action, with an artistic disposition and a good ear as a listener, though sometimes with hidden, self-serving motives; dictatorial tendencies sit alongside the culture and in some expressions there is less intellect than the polish suggests.
- **AVA-2-2029** · nak Magha, planet Sun · AVA-2 p242 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Magha aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Hot-tempered and invites a bad name through indecent behaviour.
- **AVA-2-2094** · nak Magha, pada 2, planet Venus · AVA-2 p245 · ~/stated
  **IF** Venus in Magha pada 2
  **THEN** Wealth and happiness, but less virility; even in comfort, imaginary and illusion-based problems make the native miserable for no real reason.
- **AVA-2-2124** · nak Magha, house 1 · AVA-2 p248 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Magha
  **THEN** The personality becomes royal, authoritative and proud: a rise through the native's own karma joined to past-life merit, with the ancestral identity carried visibly; leadership roles and recognition come without being pursued, because people concede authority before he asks for it.
- **AVA-2-2125** · nak Magha, house 1 · AVA-2 p248 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord in Magha, unrefined
  **THEN** Ego dominance, an inability to occupy a subordinate position gracefully and a genuine identity crisis if the status ever falls, since the self and the standing have grown into one thing.
- **AVA-2-2148** · nak Magha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p253 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Magha
  **THEN** The mind is pride-driven and emotionally attached to status and respect - the native needs validation and feels secure only when recognised, so the inner life is unusually dependent on external circumstances; the public image is strong and the native is seen as dignified and respectable.
- **AVA-2-2149** · nak Magha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p253 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon afflicted in Magha
  **THEN** Emotional ego, a sharp sensitivity to disrespect that others find disproportionate and mood swings that follow standing rather than circumstance; emotional stability depends on detachment from validation.
- **AVA-2-2155** · nak Magha, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p254 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Magha, shadow side
  **THEN** Ego in knowledge and a superiority complex that grows precisely because the wisdom is real; ego is the one thing that can destroy the dharma it rests on.
- **AVA-2-2163** · nak Magha, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p255 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu weak in Magha
  **THEN** Isolation, confusion and loss of direction - detachment without the power.
- **AVA-2-2164** · nak Magha, house 1 · AVA-2 p256 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Magha
  **THEN** The native carries a natural weight - people defer without knowing why and rooms organise themselves around him; the bearing is dignified, the self-respect non-negotiable and the ancestral stamp visible. The danger is exactly the gift: pride hardens into ego and a fall in status wounds deeply. Dashas touching this degree raise the station or test the humility.
- **NAT-0340** · nak Magha, planet Moon · NAT p234 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Magha (0°00'-13°20' Leo)
  **THEN** Themes of legacy, ancestral wisdom and karmic duty; leadership, strength and the responsibility of rulership, poised between power and humility.
- **NAT-0347** · nak Magha · NAT p237 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent (archetype of the Regal Steward)
  **THEN** Benevolent leadership marked by justice, compassion and values that transcend personal ambition; inspires trust and creates legacies of moral integrity.
- **NAT-0348** · nak Magha · NAT p238 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha's ancestral connection turned to entitlement
  **THEN** Over-identification with power and status; overemphasis on inheritance and tradition breeds rigidity that sacrifices innovation and alienates others.
- **NAT-0349** · nak Magha · NAT p238 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha's ambition driven by ego, as with Duryodhana
  **THEN** Unchecked pride and over-identification with material power blind the native to the responsibilities their influence entails, leading to division and destruction.
- **NAT-0350** · nak Magha · NAT p238 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha's karmic obligations from the Pitrs
  **THEN** Inner conflict as the demands of lineage collide with personal aspiration.
- **NAT-0351** · nak Magha · NAT p239 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha's loyalty to tradition become resistance to change
  **THEN** Clings too tightly to ancestral ways and misses opportunities to evolve.
- **NAT-0361** · nak Magha, pada 1 · NAT p244 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 1 (0°00'-3°20' Leo), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Courage, initiative and commanding presence; double fire gives decisive impactful action but risks impulsive, domineering behaviour.
- **NAT-0362** · nak Magha, pada 2 · NAT p244 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 2 (3°20'-6°40' Leo), Taurus navamsa, Venus-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Authority grounded into sustainable growth and practical leadership; creativity and material success, with the risk that material gain eclipses spiritual focus.
- **NAT-0363** · nak Magha, pada 3 · NAT p245 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 3 (6°40'-10°00' Leo), Gemini navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Communication skill, curiosity and relational dynamism; creative vitality in collaborative and artistic work, though focus scatters without Leo's steady drive.
- **NAT-0364** · nak Magha, pada 4 · NAT p245 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha pada 4 (10°00'-13°20' Leo), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Compassion, nurturing and spiritual depth; leadership turned to service, aligning material pursuit with spiritual aspiration.
- **NAT-0367** · nak Magha · NAT p247 · -/stated
  **IF** Magha's rajasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Restlessness, competitiveness and attachment to recognition.
- **RBS-1-1333** · nak Magha · RBS-1 p184 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Magha
  **THEN** More inclination to the material world, authority and position than to spirituality.
- **RBS-1-1337** · nak Magha · RBS-1 p185 · +/stated
  **IF** Male born in Magha
  **THEN** God-fearing, soft spoken and leading a noiseless life, honoured by learned persons, but hot tempered and unable to tolerate anything outside the purview of truth.
- **RBS-1-1338** · nak Magha · RBS-1 p185 · +/stated
  **IF** Female born in Magha
  **THEN** Charitable and god-fearing, enjoying royal comforts, mastering both household and official activity, helping without selfish motive.
- **RBS-1-1345** · nak Magha · RBS-1 p185 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Magha — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Religious and traditional, god-fearing, respectful of elders, strictly following forefathers' teaching, empathetic and careful; but attracted to material pleasure, proud through high position, over-confident and imposing decisions on others, causing resentment.
- **RBS-2-1260** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p296 · ~/stated
  **IF** Native under the influence of Magha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Inclines toward the material world, authority and position rather than toward spirituality.
- **RBS-2-1267** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p297 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Magha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Selfless and sincere in all spheres, peace-loving and soft-spoken, expert in many subjects and receiving accolades from the learned, and well versed in the scriptures.
- **RBS-2-1268** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p297 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha native in society
  **THEN** A champion of his culture who devotes maximum time to the betterment of society and of his community, and most such natives achieve a distinguished identity within their community in their lifetime.
- **RBS-2-1275** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p297 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Magha
  **THEN** God-fearing and soft-spoken, leading a quiet life and receiving honour and recognition from the learned; hot-tempered and unable to tolerate any action outside the bounds of truth.
- **RBS-2-1276** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p298 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Magha
  **THEN** Charitable and God-fearing, enjoying royal comforts, mastering both household and official activity, and helping without selfish motive.
- **RBS-2-1284** · nak Magha · RBS-2 p298 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Magha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Attracted to material pleasures and proud of his high position, often overconfident in power and imposing his decisions on others without consideration, causing resentment.
- **TAM-RA-0020** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p47 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha, the Rakshas-gan nakshatra of Ketu
  **THEN** Brings forth a Rajbhogi - one who enjoys royal pleasures.
- **TAM-RA-0272** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p181 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native likes to show off a great deal, displaying his strength and majesty even where it is out of place.
- **TAM-RA-0279** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p183 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is obsessed with pomp and show and with being the centre of attention, craves applause and admiration, and often becomes a victim of flattery - while himself knowing how to flatter, and therefore how to slander.
- **TAM-RA-0288** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p190 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Attracts many tragedies in life, many of them sudden, pushing the native into deep shock.
- **TAM-RA-0290** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p192 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native generally avoids being too serious and keeps his humour.
- **TAM-RA-0291** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p192 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Life feels like a roller coaster of sudden high and low phases; if the chart is overall positive the native never loses enthusiasm even in the low phases.
- **TAM-RA-0296** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p193 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is large-hearted and may do a favour without asking or expecting anything in return - the Leo nature of simply giving.
- **TAM-RA-0302** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p195 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Tamasic by nature - the craving for power, knowledge and position is excessive, and the subtle desire is to gain the seat of power by hook or by crook.
- **TAM-RA-0305** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p197 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is very ambitious, and when ambition takes over, joy is not found - ambition is itself the barrier.
- **TAM-RA-0306** · nak Magha, planet Rahu, sign Leo · TAM-RA p198 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha, especially conjunct Mars or Saturn in Leo
  **THEN** Rahu is more damaging here than elsewhere.
- **TAM-RA-0311** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p199 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native finds letting go very difficult and will never forgive.
- **TAM-RA-0316** · nak Magha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p206 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Magha nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings creativity - the native loves whatever he does, dances, sings, and celebrates life rather than analysing it.
- **TRI-0478** · nak Magha · TRI p140 · ~/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent
  **THEN** The first nakshatra of the individualisation process; represents all that is meant by 'I' — most closely related to the native's sense of identity and origin.
- **TRI-0486** · nak Magha · TRI p142 · +/stated
  **IF** Magha prominent
  **THEN** Power lies in covering weakness through reservedness; aligns with ancestral lineage and derives pride from belonging; magnanimous to those below its station.

## Purvaphalguni — nature (95)

- **ASH-0874** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p247 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent
  **THEN** Polite, outspoken, relaxed, fond of luxury, brilliant, gentle, generous, theatrical, skilled, cool, an occultist, a workaholic, tolerant, sincere and rational - but restless, angry and obsessive about sex.
- **ASH-0878** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p249 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent (deity Bhaga)
  **THEN** Enjoyment, prosperity, relaxation, glory, marital happiness, wealth, physical pleasure and honour; sensuality and a youthful disposition.
- **ASH-0883** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p250 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent
  **THEN** Clarity of mind about what to do rather than dualism; positive and optimistic toward life, seeking truth through beauty, relaxation, enjoyment, rhythm, luxury, dance and music.
- **ASH-0885** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p250 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent
  **THEN** Very ego-centric, seeking power and dominance over others with no intention to harm but to impress; strong enough to face adversity confidently and save people from injustice.
- **ASH-0888** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p251 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent
  **THEN** Appears very rude externally but remains calm and soft at heart; sensual pleasure becomes prominent through the nature of the union of two opposites.
- **ASH-0890** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p251 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent, with excess comfort
  **THEN** Laziness, lack of punctuality, a casual approach to important dealings and high tendency to procrastinate.
- **ASH-0891** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p251 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent
  **THEN** Good adaptability and comfort with the existing environment; believes in the status quo rather than big transformation and follows established rules, but takes bold steps without hesitation when circumstances are adverse.
- **ASH-0892** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p251 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni following Magha in the zodiac
  **THEN** After the exhaustion of Magha's responsibilities, the native seeks luxury, comfort, enjoyment and sensual pleasure to revitalise, and so takes wise decisions from a relaxed mind.
- **ASH-0896** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p252 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent (Rajasic quality)
  **THEN** Looks nice, grateful, knowledgeable, cool and relaxed, but remains internally active to achieve his goals by hook or by crook.
- **ASH-0904** · nak Purvaphalguni · ASH p253 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni prominent, pleasure being the major motivation
  **THEN** Lethargic and unmotivated toward anything not delightful, leaving practical duties unfinished and life unstable; vanity is a chief flaw, and he can come off as entitled or pretentious.
- **ASH-0908** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 1 · ASH p253 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 1
  **THEN** Very creative but mostly aggressive, dominating and power-seeking to enjoy life with royalty; also procrastinating, lazy, compassionate and loyal to his mate.
- **ASH-0910** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 2 · ASH p254 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 2
  **THEN** Fond of food, drink and travel, inclined to money, luxury, comfort and sensual pleasure; appears lazy externally but remains active internally, with strong confidence and stamina.
- **ASH-0914** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 3 · ASH p254 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 3
  **THEN** Knowledgeable, cool, calm and pleasant while desiring authority; happy, confident and relaxed at heart, incredibly inventive and prospering through that talent, valuing peace and an extravagant comfortable lifestyle.
- **ASH-0916** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 4 · ASH p255 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 4 (Scorpio navamsha, Mars-ruled)
  **THEN** Purvaphalguni does not feel good here - the Scorpio navamsha gives a secretive nature while the Sun demands truthfulness and Venus wants outward display, so the native is trapped in difficulty and feels suffocated.
- **ASH-0927** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Moon · ASH p257 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Focused on fulfilling his own needs first rather than chivalry; ego remains high, he takes things personally and seeks attention, while loving travel, food, luxury, beauty and fine attire.
- **AVA-2-2193** · nak Purvaphalguni · AVA-2 p261 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni placement anywhere
  **THEN** Prajanana shakti, the power of procreation and of creative and physical union: natives are charming, warm, sociable and artistic, drawn to romance, celebration, comfort and the arts, generous hosts who make life sweeter for everyone around them. The governing word is pleasure.
- **AVA-2-2194** · nak Purvaphalguni · AVA-2 p262 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni unrefined
  **THEN** The couch invites indulgence and the star slides into laziness, vanity and a taste for pleasure that outruns its discipline.
- **AVA-2-2198** · nak Purvaphalguni · AVA-2 p262 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni strong and well-placed versus afflicted
  **THEN** Strong: creativity, wealth through luxury and art, happy relationships, charisma and attraction. Afflicted: addiction, lust, financial loss, relationship drama, laziness and ego. Purvaphalguni sweetens a planet; it does not strengthen a weak one and it softens the discipline of a strong one.
- **AVA-2-2199** · nak Purvaphalguni · AVA-2 p263 · ~/stated
  **IF** The four quarters of Purvaphalguni compared
  **THEN** They move from regal self-display to practical enterprise to Venusian refinement to emotional intensity; the first is vargottama and the third is Pushkara, so two of the four carry an additional classical strength.
- **AVA-2-2200** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 1 · AVA-2 p263 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 1, 13°20'-16°40' Leo, Leo navamsha, Sun, vargottama
  **THEN** The most self-oriented, regal and dignity-conscious quarter, amplified by vargottama: the emphasis falls on identity, self-image, self-importance and personal radiance and the native wants to be seen, recognised and acknowledged. In evolved charts nobility, leadership, soul-warmth and the power to inspire; in less evolved ones ego, superiority, aggression, moodiness and self-centredness.
- **AVA-2-2201** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 1 · AVA-2 p263 · ~/classical
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 1
  **THEN** Skilful and pleasant in speech; brave, yet moody and lazy; religious, merchant-like in business dealing, courageous and talkative - with a superiority complex and aggression underneath; strongly self-focused, protective of dignity and sensitive to insult, aristocratic and commanding in bearing.
- **AVA-2-2208** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 2 · AVA-2 p264 · ~/classical
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 2
