  **THEN** Righteous, enthusiastic and well-known, with clean habits and hard work; a worrying nature and a fondness for drink; boastful, clever and enterprising and soft towards women; more grounded and capable of sustained work than any other quarter of this star.
- **AVA-2-2214** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 3 · AVA-2 p264 · ~/classical
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 3
  **THEN** Pleasant, courteous, honoured, artistic, devoted to spouse and family, fond of frequent travel, with a strong aesthetic sense and counselling ability - yet the classics are blunt about cruel, dishonest and lustful tendencies, evil thoughts, harsh speech, higher aggression and being less liked among women: polished outside, carrying strong passions and conflicting desires inside.
- **AVA-2-2220** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 4 · AVA-2 p265 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 4, 23°20'-26°40' Leo, Scorpio navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** The most emotionally intense, inwardly complex and psychologically heavy quarter: pleasure-seeking turns deeper and more charged - towards home, family, intense sentiment, self-reflection and personal valour, with passions strong, emotions stronger and unnecessary emotional conflict created where the energy is not handled wisely.
- **AVA-2-2221** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 4 · AVA-2 p265 · -/classical
  **IF** Purvaphalguni pada 4
  **THEN** Deeply emotional, passionate, serious and given to brooding melancholy; clever and shrewd with intense sentiments; sound health but a lack of ready cash; swings between passion and despair, turns suspicious and forceful and carries powerful subconscious drives. The harsher classical lines record sinful and careless tendencies, hostility towards religion and the troubling of pious people.
- **AVA-2-2241** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Moon · AVA-2 p267 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Emotional, social and responsible - it gains through public work and carries emotional dissatisfaction underneath.
- **AVA-2-2242** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Moon · AVA-2 p267 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvaphalguni aspected or conjunct the Sun
  **THEN** Skilled in handling people; helpful to all, yet unsupported himself.
- **AVA-2-2252** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Mars · AVA-2 p268 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Aggressive, technical and action-oriented, with strong physical and sexual energy and conflict or litigation possible.
- **AVA-2-2276** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Venus · AVA-2 p269 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Purvaphalguni with Jupiter's influence
  **THEN** Courage and fortune.
- **AVA-2-2283** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p270 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Saturn here carries karma, delay and burden - hard work and endurance.
- **AVA-2-2286** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p270 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purvaphalguni with Mars's influence
  **THEN** Intelligent and scientific.
- **AVA-2-2303** · nak Purvaphalguni, pada 4, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p270 · +/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Purvaphalguni pada 4 with Jupiter or Mars
  **THEN** Intelligent and balanced.
- **AVA-2-2307** · nak Purvaphalguni, house 1 · AVA-2 p271 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** The personality turns attractive, charming, magnetic, pleasure-seeking and comfort-loving; wants and receives attention and admiration; doors open easily.
- **AVA-2-2308** · nak Purvaphalguni, house 1 · AVA-2 p271 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord afflicted in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Self-obsession, a taste for drama, laziness, vanity and addiction; the charm outlives the substance behind it.
- **AVA-2-2333** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Sun · AVA-2 p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** The ego turns pleasure-driven and authority seeks recognition, admiration and validation rather than obedience; leadership becomes charismatic instead of authoritative.
- **AVA-2-2335** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Sun · AVA-2 p276 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined
  **THEN** Vanity, arrogance and attention-seeking.
- **AVA-2-2336** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Sun · AVA-2 p276 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun placed in Purvaphalguni, matured
  **THEN** An inspiring and genuinely radiant personality.
- **AVA-2-2337** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Moon · AVA-2 p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** The mind becomes comfort-oriented, finding its emotional security in pleasure and relationships and needing constant attention and affection to feel settled.
- **AVA-2-2339** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Moon · AVA-2 p277 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon placed in Purvaphalguni, matured
  **THEN** A genuinely warm and nurturing nature that people love for good reason.
- **AVA-2-2340** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Mars · AVA-2 p277 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Energy turns sensual and pleasure-driven and action directs itself towards desires and relationships rather than goals; sexual energy runs very high throughout the life.
- **AVA-2-2341** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Mars · AVA-2 p277 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined
  **THEN** Lust, impulsiveness and the scandals that follow both.
- **AVA-2-2342** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Mars · AVA-2 p277 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars placed in Purvaphalguni, matured
  **THEN** The passionate creator and the bold artist, where the same heat produces work instead of trouble.
- **AVA-2-2345** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p277 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined
  **THEN** Manipulation and knowledge that is broad and shallow.
- **AVA-2-2352** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Venus · AVA-2 p277 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined
  **THEN** Overindulgence and promiscuity and the reputational cost that follows both.
- **AVA-2-2353** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Venus · AVA-2 p277 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus placed in Purvaphalguni, matured
  **THEN** Becomes the artist, the lover and the creator of beauty.
- **AVA-2-2354** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p278 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Discipline clashes directly with pleasure; a standing tension between duty and desire, with delays falling on the enjoyment itself rather than on the work.
- **AVA-2-2355** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p278 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined
  **THEN** Frustration and suppression that break out eventually into sudden indulgence.
- **AVA-2-2356** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p278 · +/stated
  **IF** Saturn placed in Purvaphalguni, matured
  **THEN** Discipline balanced with enjoyment, the rarest and most useful result of this nakshatra.
- **AVA-2-2357** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p278 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in Purvaphalguni
  **THEN** Desire becomes extreme - obsession with pleasure, fame and relationships, magnified past the point where satisfaction is possible.
- **AVA-2-2358** · nak Purvaphalguni, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p278 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in Purvaphalguni, unrefined

## Uttaraphalguni — nature (78)

- **ASH-0950** · nak Uttaraphalguni · ASH p265 · +/stated

  **THEN** Intelligent, active, steady, elastic, sincere, contented, studious, rich, confident, competitive, industrious, independent, polite, prolific, gutsy, observant, recognised, honoured, optimistic and truthful.
- **ASH-0955** · nak Uttaraphalguni · ASH p267 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Sun's dominating, power-seeking, belligerent traits remain prominent but softer than in Krittika, because Aryaman is a much gentler deity than Agni.

  **IF** Uttaraphalguni prominent

- **RBS-2-1369** · nak Hasta · RBS-2 p311 · ~/stated

  **THEN** A likeable character skilled with the hands, sociable and entertaining but fixed in purpose; handsome, orthodox, sensitive and artistic, inclined to public service, able in contests, fond of foreign lands, eloquent in speech but of a thievish mind.
- **RBS-2-1370** · nak Hasta, planet Moon · RBS-2 p311 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Hasta Nakshatra
  **THEN** Thievish, merciless, impudent, industrious in work and inclined toward intoxication.
- **RBS-2-1373** · nak Hasta · RBS-2 p311 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Hasta Nakshatra
  **THEN** Ability to win people and situations over, exceptional sharpness, self-control, faithfulness, strength of intellect, and an innate ability to inspire and motivate.
- **RBS-2-1374** · nak Hasta · RBS-2 p312 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Hasta Nakshatra
  **THEN** Sometimes dominating and cruel, prone to rude callousness and lacking sensitivity.
- **RBS-2-1378** · nak Hasta · RBS-2 p312 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Hasta
  **THEN** Magnetic power to attract others, receiving respect and honour from the public, helping the needy and deceiving no one, simple in habit, but his life is full of frequent ups and downs due to some hidden curse upon him.
- **RBS-2-1384** · nak Hasta · RBS-2 p313 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Hasta
  **THEN** Has feminine shyness and respects elders, yet does not hesitate to express her views openly even though it earns her the enmity of relatives.
- **RBS-2-1394** · nak Hasta, pada 4 · RBS-2 p314 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Hasta pada 4 (Virgo 20°00' to 23°20')
  **THEN** Of right conduct, strong and of high status, a heavy eater helped by the mother's family; but of flickering mind and ordinary mental level and education, more inclined to family affairs and compromising on career; under affliction manipulative and easily addicted to alcohol; large belly, small face and elephant-like feet, with an unknown fear of flowing rivers in which he will never bathe.
- **TAM-RA-0365** · nak Hasta · TAM-RA p235 · ~/stated
  **IF** Hasta's creative energy in a positive or a negative being
  **THEN** The energy is the same - a positive being creates something beautiful with it, a negative one creates problems in others' lives; the white magician heals, the black magician destroys.
- **TAM-RA-0368** · nak Hasta, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p237 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Hasta nakshatra
  **THEN** Observing others, analysing every aspect of life and work, and serving mankind are the native's traits; he likes servitude and has the heart of a servant.
- **TAM-RA-0379** · nak Hasta, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p241 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Hasta for natives born on Cancer, Scorpio or Aries ascendant
  **THEN** More beneficial than for other ascendants.
- **TRI-0607** · nak Hasta · TRI p181 · ~/stated
  **IF** Hasta — clenched fist, positive aspect
  **THEN** Strength derived through unity and the harmonious working of parts; the nakshatra most closely associated with cooperation to achieve a goal — though a cooperation marked by a distinct lack of trust.
- **TRI-0610** · nak Hasta · TRI p182 · +/stated
  **IF** Hasta prominent
  **THEN** Activity and alertness; the proverbial wake-up call on both material and spiritual levels.
- **TRI-0611** · nak Hasta · TRI p182 · +/stated
  **IF** Hasta ruled by the jovial Savitar
  **THEN** Playful and into tricks, amusements and games; interested in both mind games and physical sport.
- **TRI-0614** · nak Hasta · TRI p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Hasta prominent
  **THEN** One of the most optimistic and creative nakshatras — a bright sunny day in which everyone feels good and is creatively occupied.
- **TRI-0616** · nak Hasta · TRI p183 · +/stated
  **IF** Hasta prominent
  **THEN** Takes work seriously with an internal lightheartedness underneath; very good at carrying plain faces and stern exteriors — its comedians work deadpan.
- **TRI-0628** · nak Hasta, pada 2 · TRI p185 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet in Hasta pada 2 (13°20'-16°40' Virgo, Taurus navamsa, Venus)
  **THEN** Down-to-earth practicality seeking material arrangement and perfection as its dharma; honesty and morality more developed than in other padas, and artistic activity supported. Mercury, Venus and Saturn give good results.
- **TRI-0643** · nak Hasta · TRI p188 · -/stated
  **IF** Hasta working through its lower functioning
  **THEN** The negative lunar traits take centre stage — ultra-conservatism, bigotry, fickleness, excessive attachment and inability to let go.
- **TRI-0653** · nak Hasta, planet Moon · TRI p193 · -/classical
  **IF** Moon in Hasta (per Varahamihira)
  **THEN** Energetic, daring, merciless, a drunkard and a thief.

## Chitra — nature (85)

- **ASH-1092** · nak Chitra · ASH p298 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent
  **THEN** Technical, creative, lovable, attracted to beauty, insightful, intuitive, practical, brave, energetic, confident, a workaholic, abstruse, brilliant, debating and a good decision-maker - though restless.
- **ASH-1096** · nak Chitra · ASH p300 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent (deity Twashta, Mars as lord)
  **THEN** Confident, intellectual, a leader and initiator, strong enough to fight for rights; externalises inherent power without fear, restriction or obstruction to achieve his goals.
- **ASH-1101** · nak Chitra · ASH p301 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent
  **THEN** Perceived as rude, obstinate and selfish but very soft and pure at heart; the tongue sometimes loses control over words and invites unsolicited trouble.
- **ASH-1108** · nak Chitra · ASH p302 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra female native
  **THEN** Overly enamoured with her freedom, direct in personality and hating to be told what to do; she experiences the freedom she craves only after turning twenty-five.
- **ASH-1111** · nak Chitra · ASH p302 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent, procrastinating
  **THEN** Failure and trouble in routine life, with duality also causing disturbance; Tamas energy restricts quick action and determination.
- **ASH-1121** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · ASH p303 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 1
  **THEN** Physically appealing and desirous of power and authority, skilled in cruelty, with an obvious need for acceptance; a thirst for power and dominance can cause his collapse.
- **ASH-1122** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · ASH p304 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 1
  **THEN** Should learn to control resentment to keep peace and harmony, suppress a narrow-minded streak and take up kind activities to raise the inspirational tone of life.
- **ASH-1129** · nak Chitra, pada 4 · ASH p305 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 4 (Scorpio navamsha, Mars-ruled)
  **THEN** Martian energy has difficulty expressing itself when needed; the mental set-up is very disturbed, making the native sensitive and emotionally imbalanced.
- **ASH-1134** · nak Chitra, house 1 · ASH p306 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra ascendant
  **THEN** Pleasant and appealing, with a tendency toward independence and a longing for personal liberty; may choose to work independently, with a strong interest in clothing, grooming and fashion.
- **ASH-1140** · nak Chitra, planet Moon · ASH p307 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Chitra
  **THEN** The mind feels suffocated in balancing life through duality of thought inherited from the mother or parents, with relationship conflicts and challenges in association.
- **ASH-1144** · nak Chitra, planet Mars, sign Virgo · ASH p307 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in the Virgo portion of Chitra
  **THEN** Duality of mind makes the native feel dilemma in his actions and efforts.
- **ASH-1158** · nak Chitra, planet Rahu · ASH p311 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Chitra
  **THEN** Amplifies the drive for success and perfection into compulsive behaviour, and increases materialism and the desire for material success.
- **AVA-2-5304** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p335 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra placement generally
  **THEN** Gives a crafted self focused on image, glamour, design and the art of being seen; natives are creative, charismatic, meticulous and striking, with a natural eye for design, architecture, dress and effect.
- **AVA-2-5305** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p335 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra under affliction or imbalance
  **THEN** The flaw is glitter for its own sake - it can prize appearance over substance and polish the surface while the core stays hollow.
- **AVA-2-5308** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p335 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra under Tvashtar and Mars
  **THEN** Works through craft, design and technical skill, building and refining in engineering and machinery as much as in art and glamour; the shadow is a polish with nothing underneath.
- **AVA-2-5309** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p336 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra turns each area of life into a performance
  **THEN** Aligned, it gives fame, beauty and success; misaligned, it gives illusion, ego and downfall, with the planet's own dignity deciding whether the image rests on substance or on show.
- **AVA-2-5310** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p336 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra's four quarters compared
  **THEN** They move from proud self-display to disciplined order to social charm to hidden intensity, with the second and third quarters, both vargottama, the strongest of the four.
- **AVA-2-5311** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p336 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 1
  **THEN** A strong focus on personal identity, image and self-expression, with a desire for glamour, recognition and visibility; ego-conscious and self-aware, driven towards self-perfection through discipline and sacrifice.
- **AVA-2-5312** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p336 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 1

- **AVA-2-5318** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · AVA-2 p336 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 1 imbalanced

- **AVA-2-5320** · nak Chitra, pada 2 · AVA-2 p337 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 2

  **IF** Chitra pada 2 imbalanced

  **IF** Chitra pada 3, vargottama

- **AVA-2-5333** · nak Chitra, pada 3 · AVA-2 p338 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra pada 3 imbalanced

- **AVA-2-5334** · nak Chitra, pada 4 · AVA-2 p338 · ~/stated

- **AVA-2-5373** · nak Chitra, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p341 · -/stated

  **IF** Moon in Chitra (karakatwa)

- **AVA-2-5557** · nak Chitra · AVA-2 p358 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0448** · nak Chitra, planet Moon · NAT p294 · +/stated

- **NAT-0449** · nak Chitra · NAT p294 · +/stated

- **NAT-0452** · nak Chitra · NAT p296 · +/stated

- **NAT-0453** · nak Chitra · NAT p297 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra at its highest expression

- **NAT-0454** · nak Chitra · NAT p297 · -/stated

- **NAT-0465** · nak Chitra, pada 2 · NAT p302 · +/stated

- **NAT-0466** · nak Chitra, pada 3 · NAT p303 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0467** · nak Chitra, pada 4 · NAT p303 · +/stated

- **NAT-0470** · nak Chitra · NAT p304 · -/stated

- **RBS-1-1423** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p193 · +/stated

- **RBS-1-1428** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p193 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Chitra

- **RBS-1-1429** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p193 · -/stated
  **IF** Chitra afflicted in the chart

- **RBS-1-1432** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p193 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Chitra
  **THEN** Peace loving but goes to any extreme for self benefit; great intuition and very cordial with others; confronted by enemies at every step yet capable of escaping any conspiracy; a soft corner for the downtrodden to whose uplift he devotes time and energy.
- **RBS-1-1440** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p194 · -/stated
  **IF** Female born in Chitra
  **THEN** Proud, veracious, sinful and lazy, committing sinful deeds.
- **RBS-1-1443** · nak Chitra · RBS-1 p194 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Chitra — positive and negative sides

- **RBS-1-1445** · nak Chitra, pada 1 · RBS-1 p195 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Chitra pada 1
  **THEN** Very intelligent and creative; he creates new things but hides the secret of their creation all his life; fair complexion, soft body, long thick arms and large unique eyes; behaves very aggressively and is egoistic.
- **RBS-2-1396** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p315 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Chitra Nakshatra
  **THEN** Brilliant and intelligent with exceptional creativity and intuition, able to create new things out of nothing, to foresee future developments and design for them; a pioneer and not a follower.
- **RBS-2-1401** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p315 · +/stated
  **IF** Chitra native in manner

- **RBS-2-1402** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p315 · -/stated

- **RBS-2-1405** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p316 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra on the ascendant
  **THEN** Wears colourful clothes and jewels, is liked by the opposite sex, engaged in a variety of pursuits, has a wicked or critical streak, and lives away from the birthplace.
- **RBS-2-1410** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p316 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Chitra
  **THEN** Peace-loving yet ready to go to any extreme for his own benefit; of great intuition and cordial with others, confronted by enemies at every step but able to escape any conspiracy, and devoting time and energy to uplifting the downtrodden.
- **RBS-2-1418** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p317 · -/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Chitra

- **RBS-2-1421** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p317 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Chitra Nakshatra

- **RBS-2-1422** · nak Chitra · RBS-2 p317 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Chitra Nakshatra

- **TRI-0658** · nak Chitra · TRI p195 · ~/stated

- **TRI-0659** · nak Chitra · TRI p195 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent
  **THEN** 'Create, create and create' — dynamic and energetic with little ability to sit still; when its creativity is out of harmony with universal functioning the native becomes unbalanced, selfish and indulgent.
- **TRI-0660** · nak Chitra · TRI p196 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent
  **THEN** Concerned with appearance, complexity, structure and form; approaches everything from an architectural, aesthetic point of view, and is tied to maya, illusion and glamour.
- **TRI-0663** · nak Chitra · TRI p196 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent, especially the Libra portion
  **THEN** Loves admiration and is motivated by it in whatever it does; carries an element of doubt and uncertainty from the Sun's weakness in Libra, which drives its focus on structure and form.
- **TRI-0668** · nak Chitra · TRI p197 · ~/stated
  **IF** Chitra prominent
  **THEN** Holds itself with a dignity that covers its weaknesses; makes a special effort to appear completely together, much of which is pretension.

## Swati — nature (112)

- **ASH-1166** · nak Swati · ASH p313 · +/stated
  **IF** Swati prominent
  **THEN** Restless, powerful, social, dynamic, smart, a workaholic, diplomatic, wandering, adaptive, attractive, fast, optimistic, self-governing, compassionate, brave, independent, clever, impulsive, religious, polite and sensitive.
- **ASH-1173** · nak Swati · ASH p316 · +/stated
  **IF** Swati prominent (symbol a young flower blown by the wind)

- **ASH-1175** · nak Swati · ASH p317 · -/stated

- **ASH-1209** · nak Swati, house 1 · ASH p322 · +/stated

  **THEN** Keeps an impartial position in public and makes the other person feel equal, so he is likeable; polite, with a calming aura and sophisticated social etiquette, comfortable in any social situation.
- **ASH-1210** · nak Swati, house 1 · ASH p322 · ~/stated
  **IF** Swati ascendant

- **ASH-1213** · nak Swati, planet Sun · ASH p322 · +/stated

- **ASH-1216** · nak Swati, planet Moon · ASH p323 · -/stated

- **ASH-1225** · nak Swati, planet Venus · ASH p325 · ~/stated

- **TRI-0702** · nak Swati · TRI p208 · ~/stated

- **AVA-2-5773** · nak Svati, house 1 · AVA-2 p375 · ~/stated

- **AVA-2-5787** · nak Svati, house 4 · AVA-2 p376 · ~/stated

- **AVA-2-5834** · nak Svati · AVA-2 p380 · ~/stated

## Vishakha — nature (91)

- **ASH-1237** · nak Vishakha · ASH p329 · +/stated

  **THEN** Fixation is its one-word summary; its single-mindedness is unmatched, but its Achilles heel is which goal it chooses to pursue.
- **TRI-0755** · nak Vishakha · TRI p222 · -/stated
  **IF** Vishakha prominent, sensory path chosen
  **THEN** Love of pomp and ceremony that ends in the native becoming a mad party type.
- **TRI-0756** · nak Vishakha · TRI p222 · -/stated
  **IF** Vishakha prominent
  **THEN** A continuous sense of emptiness after every accomplishment; fire is never satiated. Always concerned with what it lacks rather than what it has — the grass-is-greener mentality breeding unwholesome emotion.
- **TRI-0761** · nak Vishakha · TRI p222 · -/stated
  **IF** Vishakha prominent

- **TRI-0764** · nak Vishakha · TRI p223 · -/stated

## Anuradha — nature (77)

- **ASH-1314** · nak Anuradha · ASH p344 · +/stated

- **ASH-1417** · nak Jyeshta, house 1 · ASH p367 · -/stated
  **IF** Jyeshta ascendant
  **THEN** Strong desire for control and authority, finding it hard to give power to others; conceals his genuine emotions, which makes him seem enigmatic.

- **ASH-1422** · nak Jyeshta, planet Moon · ASH p369 · ~/stated

- **ASH-1424** · nak Jyeshta, planet Mars · ASH p369 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Jyeshta

- **ASH-1426** · nak Jyeshta, planet Mercury · ASH p370 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Jyeshta
  **THEN** Some deceptive and immature tendencies, and a slightly discreet way of communicating with a propensity to conceal particular facts.
- **ASH-1428** · nak Jyeshta, planet Jupiter · ASH p371 · -/stated

  **IF** Rahu in Jyeshta

- **NAT-0561** · nak Jyeshta, planet Moon · NAT p359 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0562** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p359 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0563** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p359 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jyeshta ruled by Indra
  **THEN** Strength paired with vulnerability; authority crowned in glory yet shadowed by insecurity, tempered only by self-realisation.
- **NAT-0564** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p360 · -/stated

- **NAT-0565** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p360 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0567** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p361 · -/stated

- **NAT-0568** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p361 · -/stated

- **NAT-0570** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p363 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jyeshta's umbrella symbol

- **NAT-0571** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p363 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jyeshta's earring symbol, per Karna's surrendered earrings

- **NAT-0572** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p364 · +/stated

- **NAT-0573** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p364 · -/stated

- **NAT-0574** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p364 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0576** · nak Jyeshta · NAT p366 · ~/stated

- **RBS-1-1550** · nak Jyeshta · RBS-1 p208 · -/stated

  **IF** Male born in Jyeshta
  **THEN** Very clean and sober but unable to keep a secret even about his own life; hot tempered and obstinate, which walls off his progress; he takes spot decisions without seeing opportunity or circumstance and lands in a precarious state; he will trouble even those who helped him, and is prone to drugs and alcohol which quickly damage family life.
- **RBS-1-1558** · nak Jyeshta · RBS-1 p209 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-1568** · nak Jyeshta · RBS-2 p338 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-1582** · nak Jyeshta · RBS-2 p339 · ~/stated

  **IF** Negative side of Jyeshta Nakshatra

  **IF** Jyeshta prominent
  **THEN** Keeping up an image matters more than anything else; acts always with regard to how others perceive it, and does whatever is needed to meet prevailing standards for respect.
- **TRI-0853** · nak Jyeshta · TRI p250 · -/stated

- **TRI-0860** · nak Jyeshta · TRI p251 · ~/classical

- **AVA-2-8489** · nak Jyeshtha · AVA-2 p428 · ~/stated
  **IF** Any Jyeshtha placement

- **AVA-2-8491** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p429 · ~/stated

  **THEN** The open quarter: more frank and carefree than typical Jyeshtha, with a fierce yet truthful nature, meritorious deeds, a capable lawyer-like intellect, high status and an honest personality, though inclined to act on poor advice; risk-taking, generous, chivalrous and protective of family.

- **AVA-2-8502** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p430 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jyeshtha pada 3, 23°20'-26°40' Scorpio, Aquarius navamsha, Saturn

- **AVA-2-8504** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p431 · -/classical
  **IF** Jyeshtha pada 3 (karmic clue)

- **AVA-2-8509** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p431 · -/classical
  **IF** Jyeshtha pada 4 (karmic clue)
  **THEN** Cruelty towards animals and birds is warned against, along with anger and a revenge-driven disposition.
- **AVA-2-8512** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Sun · AVA-2 p432 · -/stated

  **THEN** May become harsh, with reddish eyes.
- **AVA-2-8522** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p433 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Jyeshtha aspected or conjunct the Sun
  **THEN** Kind to those who seek help, though the underlying temper may be hard, and enjoys power and authority from government.
- **AVA-2-8531** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p434 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Jyeshtha pada 4

- **AVA-2-8541** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 4, planet Mars · AVA-2 p434 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Jyeshtha pada 4

- **AVA-2-8542** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p434 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-8571** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p436 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Constant travel, plain living and dealings in the concealed.
- **AVA-2-8575** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 4, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p436 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Jyeshtha pada 4

- **AVA-2-8579** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 4, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p436 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Jyeshtha pada 4

- **AVA-2-8581** · nak Jyeshtha, pada 2, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p436 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Jyeshtha pada 2

- **AVA-2-8590** · nak Jyeshtha, house 1 · AVA-2 p438 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord placed in Jyeshtha

- **AVA-2-8603** · nak Jyeshtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p443 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon placed anywhere in Jyeshtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** The Moon becomes restless, intense and psychologically deep, disturbing emotional stability; well placed this gives deep emotional intelligence and occult interest, while afflicted it gives anxiety, jealousy, emotional insecurity and disturbed sleep.
- **AVA-2-8611** · nak Jyeshtha, house 1 · AVA-2 p445 · ~/stated

  **THEN** The self holds the umbrella: this native is the eldest in effect whatever his actual position, commanding and early-maturing, with vitality that carries strain in the shoulders, neck and digestion and a fear of losing face beneath the competence; dashas here add authority and its accompanying anxiety.

## Mula — nature (103)

- **ASH-0091** · nak Mula, pada 4 · ASH p55 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mula pada 4
  **THEN** Average, or not bad.
- **ASH-1445** · nak Mula · ASH p376 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Cordial, sunny, caring, generous, honest, broad-minded, respectable, investigative, calm, responsible, practical, law-abiding, proud, well-mannered and smart - but mentally unstable, aggressive and prone to destructive thinking and sudden transformation.
- **ASH-1452** · nak Mula · ASH p379 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** Both spiritual and material success, but the dual sign makes the native confused in decision-making; he values peace, lives by a predetermined premise and can withstand any negative tidal wave.
- **ASH-1460** · nak Mula · ASH p380 · -/stated
  **IF** Mula native provoked
  **THEN** Usually peaceful but occasionally reveals a darker side of rage.
- **ASH-1461** · nak Mula · ASH p380 · -/stated

- **ASH-1464** · nak Mula · ASH p381 · ~/stated

- **ASH-1471** · nak Mula · ASH p382 · +/stated

- **ASH-1472** · nak Mula · ASH p383 · -/stated

- **ASH-1483** · nak Mula, pada 4 · ASH p384 · +/stated

- **ASH-1487** · nak Mula, house 1 · ASH p385 · ~/stated

- **ASH-1492** · nak Mula, planet Moon · ASH p387 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Significant changes in mental and psychological well-being, with the ability to delve deeply into his own psyche and uncover secrets; powerful and occasionally turbulent emotions.
- **ASH-1494** · nak Mula, planet Moon · ASH p387 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mula
  **THEN** Strong tenacity and willpower with a yearning for independence and freedom; values personal freedom and struggles to accept limitation, obstinate in his quest for truth.
- **ASH-1495** · nak Mula, planet Mars · ASH p387 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars in Mula
  **THEN** Extremely energising with a strong desire to act and make things happen; tenacious and concentrated, unafraid to face inner demons or challenging circumstances.
- **ASH-1505** · nak Mula, planet Saturn · ASH p390 · ~/stated

- **ASH-1506** · nak Mula, planet Saturn · ASH p390 · -/stated
