- **NAT-0597** · nak Mula, planet Moon · NAT p378 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mula (0°00'-13°20' Sagittarius)
  **THEN** Grounding, uprooting and renewal; intensity and the ability to penetrate to the core of things, dismantling the old to make way for the new.
- **NAT-0598** · nak Mula · NAT p378 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula presided over by Nirrti, goddess of dissolution
  **THEN** Unravels structures and severs attachments that no longer serve; purifying like a storm that clears the path for new growth.
- **NAT-0600** · nak Mula · NAT p379 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula, per Prthuyasas set adrift and raised abroad
  **THEN** Severance and displacement give rise to extraordinary intelligence and insight; renewal follows uprooting.
- **NAT-0601** · nak Mula · NAT p380 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula as anti-ahankara (anti-ego)
  **THEN** Dissolves attachments and dismantles inherited structures; painful, but yields raw clarity, and true growth comes only through letting go of false identity, comfort and illusion.
- **NAT-0603** · nak Mula · NAT p381 · +/stated
  **IF** Mula's Kali energy, per the slaying of Raktabija
  **THEN** Capacity to cleanse and transform by uprooting negativity at its very source, before it can regenerate.
- **NAT-0604** · nak Mula · NAT p381 · -/stated
  **IF** Mula's destructive force unchecked, per Kali's dance
  **THEN** Threatens to consume everything; destruction must serve the higher purpose of renewal and preservation.
- **NAT-0605** · nak Mula · NAT p382 · +/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent, symbolised by tied roots
  **THEN** Inherent groundedness and a raw unfiltered view of life rooted in truth rather than illusion; close to the soil literally and spiritually.
- **NAT-0606** · nak Mula · NAT p382 · -/stated
  **IF** Mula's roots bound too tightly by old patterns, beliefs or attachments
  **THEN** Growth is restricted; the native must unearth what lies beneath in order to thrive.
- **NAT-0607** · nak Mula · NAT p383 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent (archetype of the Destroyer and Seeker of Truth)
  **THEN** Dismantles falsehood and stagnation with purpose while relentlessly questioning, challenging and exposing what has been buried.
- **NAT-0618** · nak Mula, pada 1 · NAT p388 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula pada 1 (0°00'-3°20' Sagittarius), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Boldness, decisiveness and instinct to break through limitation; fearless pursuit of hidden truth, with impulsive or destructive tendencies if unguided.
- **NAT-0619** · nak Mula, pada 2 · NAT p389 · +/stated
  **IF** Mula pada 2 (3°20'-6°40' Sagittarius), Taurus navamsa, Venus-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Disruptive energy stabilised into methodical pursuit of practical goals; creativity and sustainable growth, guarding against fixation on material gain.
- **NAT-0620** · nak Mula, pada 3 · NAT p389 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula pada 3 (6°40'-10°00' Sagittarius), Gemini navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Sharp intellect, versatility and relational adaptability; intellectual exploration and collaboration, though focus scatters without clarity of purpose.
- **NAT-0621** · nak Mula, pada 4 · NAT p390 · +/stated
  **IF** Mula pada 4 (10°00'-13°20' Sagittarius), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Emotional depth and spiritual introspection; uprooting energy turned toward emotional renewal, compassion and the healing of others.
- **NAT-0624** · nak Mula · NAT p391 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula's dominant tamasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Inertia, avoidance and suppression; balanced it gives deep rest, introspection and the dismantling of harmful attachment.
- **RBS-1-0648** · nak Mula, planet Mars · RBS-1 p96 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Mula
  **THEN** Wounds, harsh speech, dependence on others, results only through hard work, impatience, squabbles, disappointment and regret.
- **RBS-1-1580** · nak Mula, planet Venus · RBS-1 p212 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus influence or affliction on a Mula native
  **THEN** His knowledge is used for personal gain and he may misuse his powers for personal benefit.
- **RBS-1-1585** · nak Mula · RBS-1 p213 · +/stated
  **IF** Male born in Mula
  **THEN** A very sweet, peace-loving nature with set principles, able to penetrate any adverse tide and reach his destination; unconcerned about tomorrow and leaving everything in God's hands.
- **RBS-1-1591** · nak Mula · RBS-1 p213 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Mula
  **THEN** Mostly pure hearted but very adamant, and lacking the knack of dealing with people she often lands in problems.
- **RBS-1-1597** · nak Mula · RBS-1 p213 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mula — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Hard working, committed and intelligent in innovating a road to success, highly optimistic and able to come out of the toughest situation; but provocative, stubborn and adamant, and lacking the knowledge to deal with things, which lands him in problems.
- **RBS-1-1601** · nak Mula, pada 1 · RBS-1 p214 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mula pada 1
  **THEN** It turns the native toward the material world: arrogant and egoistic, concerned with material growth and self respect in society; fair complexion, shining teeth, piercing eye, aggressive talk, a big beautiful nose and generally small testicles.
- **RBS-1-1604** · nak Mula, pada 4 · RBS-1 p214 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Mula pada 4
  **THEN** It makes the native emotional and so unable to take firm decisions; the most imbalanced people of this nakshatra, though intelligent, knowledgeable, skilled and joyful; above average height, reddish-white complexion and beautiful hair.
- **RBS-2-0426** · nak Mula, pada 2 · RBS-2 p126 · +/classical
  **IF** Girl born in the 2nd pada of Mula
  **THEN** Blessed with all kinds of happiness.
- **RBS-2-0445** · nak Mula, pada 4 · RBS-2 p127 · +/classical
  **IF** Birth in the 4th pada of Mula
  **THEN** Proves brightly auspicious.
- **RBS-2-0454** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p128 · -/classical
  **IF** Birth in Mula Nakshatra during morning twilight
  **THEN** Inauspicious for friends.
- **RBS-2-0586** · nak Mula, planet Ketu · RBS-2 p147 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Mula Nakshatra, ruled by Ketu, symbol the lion's tail
  **THEN** Gains wisdom and knowledge through hard work under the joint influence of Jupiter and Ketu; lives by his own principles, adventurous, brave and an excellent leader.
- **RBS-2-0747** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p200 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula Nakshatra, ruled by Nirriti, whose shakti is to ruin and destroy
  **THEN** Brings affliction but also the power to destroy the root of affliction, clearing the way for new creation and connecting with primordial creative powers.
- **RBS-2-1605** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p343 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula, meaning the root or the base
  **THEN** Signifies root vegetables and, because roots are unseen, the hidden or buried secrets of one's life and the search for the root cause of everything.
- **RBS-2-1612** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p344 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula, a neutral, Tamasic and demonic Nakshatra ruling knives, blades, scissors and swords
  **THEN** Its natives do not harm others in achieving their goals and their work benefits society, but anyone who plays politics or undercuts them cannot escape their anger.
- **RBS-2-1613** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p344 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula, a sharp Rakshasa star of the butcher class meaning the root
  **THEN** Suggests one searching after the origin of life and also one tied to a position of relative misfortune; clever and capable and possibly spiritually devoted, but with an air of suspiciousness.
- **RBS-2-1615** · nak Mula, planet Moon · RBS-2 p344 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mula Nakshatra
  **THEN** Proud, fairly well-to-do, of soft disposition, luxurious habits and fixed mind.
- **RBS-2-1623** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p344 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Mula
  **THEN** Very sweet-natured, peace-loving and principled, able to stand against and penetrate any adverse tide to reach his destination, unworried about tomorrow and leaving all happenings in the hands of God.
- **RBS-2-1629** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p345 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Mula
  **THEN** Mostly pure-hearted but very adamant, and lacking the knack of dealing with people she often lands in trouble.
- **RBS-2-1633** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p345 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Mula Nakshatra
  **THEN** Hardworking, committed and intelligent enough to innovate ideas that make the road to success, highly optimistic and able to come out of the toughest situations.
- **RBS-2-1634** · nak Mula · RBS-2 p345 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Mula Nakshatra
  **THEN** Provocative, stubborn and adamant, lacking the knowledge to deal with things, which often lands him in various problems.
- **RBS-2-1644** · nak Mula, pada 4 · RBS-2 p347 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Mula pada 4 (Sagittarius 10°00' to 13°20')
  **THEN** Strong, clever, gentle, wise and sense-controlled, able to defeat opponents but suffering throat disease; emotional, so that firm decisions are difficult and he is the most imbalanced of this Nakshatra, unable to direct his goals; intelligent, knowledgeable, skilled and joyful, above average height with reddish-white complexion and beautiful hair.
- **TAM-RA-0021** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p47 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola, the Rakshas-gan nakshatra of Ketu
  **THEN** Brings forth a Rajdrohi - a rebel against the throne; not to be judged bad, since the rule opposed may itself be corrupt.
- **TAM-RA-0525** · nak Mula · TAM-RA p329 · +/stated
  **IF** Moola nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** Native is no perfectionist - he loves to be natural, and it is his naturalness with all its imperfection that makes him appealing.
- **TAM-RA-0529** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p329 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is very hot-tempered and fired up with ideals and principles; a fighter by birth who can become a great revolutionary for justice and equal rights.
- **TAM-RA-0530** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p330 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** 'No nonsense' is the native's mantra - he sticks to the point, demands the bottom line, and moves fast.
- **TAM-RA-0538** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p335 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola, a nakshatra of extremes
  **THEN** The native is either a Hitler or a Buddha - there is no middle ground and mediocrity does not exist in his dictionary.
- **TAM-RA-0541** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p335 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola, itself a haughty nakshatra
  **THEN** Makes the native more haughty still.
- **TAM-RA-0542** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p335 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola with the chart negatively placed
  **THEN** Gives a very quarrelsome native little liked by his people or family, whose haughtiness becomes the primary reason for his fall from grace.
- **TAM-RA-0546** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p339 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** Natives are deeply faithful and never give up - thrown out of what they built, they return with something still greater.
- **TAM-RA-0550** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p341 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings great interest in suspense, thrill and the mysteries of life.
- **TAM-RA-0552** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p341 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola, both being tamasic
  **THEN** Native emphasises the negative aspects of life more than the positive.
- **TAM-RA-0554** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p342 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** Natives are hardworking; passing through many difficulties they grow strong, more faithful and more industrious, and keep smiling with a never-give-up attitude.
- **TAM-RA-0557** · nak Mula, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p344 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola nakshatra
  **THEN** Brings great courage; the native takes risks and gives the world fresh perspectives, innovations, new ideas and new establishments.
- **TRI-0894** · nak Mula · TRI p261 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** The tied roots impose constraint and limitation — Mula does not allow much freedom or scattering of energy and makes the native delve deeply within a limited sphere; the most pinpointed of all nakshatras.
- **TRI-0895** · nak Mula · TRI p262 · -/classical
  **IF** Mula ruled by Nritti, goddess of dissolution
  **THEN** Daughter of unrighteousness and violence, mother of death and fear; not a pleasant nakshatra, though it lies in the luckiest sign, Sagittarius, ruled by the great benefic Jupiter.
- **TRI-0898** · nak Mula · TRI p262 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** Relentless — will stop at nothing until it has exercised its will, and fully utilises whatever willpower is at its command.
- **TRI-0899** · nak Mula · TRI p263 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** Quickly cuts out and destroys what has lost its value; very impulsive and more often than not regrets its actions in hindsight. Only when its will aligns with the universal will does it function in a wise Jupiterian way.
- **TRI-0900** · nak Mula · TRI p263 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** The individualisation process that began in Ashvini and lionised in Magha culminates here; still proud and haughty, and the native is unable to comprehend their own power and abilities — which sometimes restricts the damage they can do.
- **TRI-0905** · nak Mula · TRI p264 · -/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** Displays one form of arrogance or another in dealings with others; sarcasm, boldness and reservedness go together, with a tendency to over-dramatise or underplay situations.
- **TRI-0906** · nak Mula · TRI p264 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mula prominent
  **THEN** Peculiar ways of viewing any situation that put the native at odds with others; over-eager to take drastic action to resolve problems. Its talent is getting to the root of any matter without beating about the bush — which lands it in trouble when its judgement is skewed.
- **AVA-2-11253** · nak Moola · AVA-2 p451 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola placement anywhere
  **THEN** Barhana shakti, the power to break apart in order to reveal; natives are penetrating, philosophical, intense, unafraid of depths and drawn to research, healing and the occult. The governing word is root.
- **AVA-2-11255** · nak Moola · AVA-2 p451 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola placement, higher versus lower expression
  **THEN** Improves a department only when the native learns to destroy ignorance rather than people, cut attachment rather than compassion, and uproot falsehood rather than love.
- **AVA-2-11256** · nak Moola · AVA-2 p452 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola placement, reformer versus destroyer
  **THEN** The same root-cutting force can heal or wreck: its higher face gives truth-seeking, reform, healing, research, courage and liberation, its lower gives chaos, impulsiveness, arrogance, broken relationships and self-destruction; the planet's own dignity decides which.
- **AVA-2-11259** · nak Moola, pada 1 · AVA-2 p452 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 1, 00-03d20' Sagittarius, Aries navamsha, Mars, gandanta
  **THEN** The penetrating quarter: deeply probing, investigative and root-seeking, action-oriented, aggressive and impulsive, with a strong internal conflict between moral and material values; direct, blunt and fearless, in constant search of deeper meaning.
- **AVA-2-11267** · nak Moola, pada 1 · AVA-2 p453 · -/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 1, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Erratic egotism and self-centredness in the lower expression, with fiery tendencies producing impulsive action; the gandanta compounds every difficulty at the opening degrees.
- **AVA-2-11268** · nak Moola, pada 2 · AVA-2 p453 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 2, 03d20'-06d40' Sagittarius, Taurus navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** The grounded quarter: a blend of material and occult tendencies, hardworking and persistent with real tenacity, an inclination towards the occult, mysticism and hidden knowledge and creative instincts in the arts, music and performance, though meeting internal struggle and repeated obstacles.
- **AVA-2-11275** · nak Moola, pada 2 · AVA-2 p453 · -/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 2, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Repeated obstacles wear at the persistence, alongside property and sibling disputes and health that gives way under sustained strain.
- **AVA-2-11276** · nak Moola, pada 3 · AVA-2 p453 · +/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 3, 06d40'-10d00' Sagittarius, Gemini navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** The intellectual quarter, the lightest of the four: strong communication, wordplay and expressive skill, a genuine sense of humour and a logical, discriminating, analytical mind, with a balanced approach between material and moral values and noticeably less outward aggression.
- **AVA-2-11280** · nak Moola, pada 3 · AVA-2 p454 · -/classical
  **IF** Moola pada 3 (karmic clue)
  **THEN** A fondness for unconventional habits and association with questionable indulgences and a tendency towards excess desire.
- **AVA-2-11282** · nak Moola, pada 3 · AVA-2 p454 · -/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 3, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Detachment from material accumulation turns to instability and, where afflicted, real poverty.
- **AVA-2-11283** · nak Moola, pada 4 · AVA-2 p454 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 4, 10d00'-13d20' Sagittarius, Cancer navamsha, Moon
  **THEN** The emotional quarter, the most emotionally intense and unstable of the four: highly emotional, sensitive and reactive with frequent emotional disturbance, carrying strong ambition alongside emotional sacrifice, though internally practical and self-aware to a degree others do not suspect.
- **AVA-2-11289** · nak Moola, pada 4 · AVA-2 p454 · -/stated
  **IF** Moola pada 4, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Emotional stress leads directly to health disturbance and turmoil that undoes what the ambition builds; cruel tendencies appear in the lower expression.
- **AVA-2-11306** · nak Moola, planet Moon · AVA-2 p456 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Moola aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Comfort, artistic feeling and attachment to pleasure.
- **AVA-2-11310** · nak Moola, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p456 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Moola pada 3
  **THEN** Articulate, humorous and socially adaptable, with fluctuating means.
- **AVA-2-11311** · nak Moola, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p456 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Moola pada 4
  **THEN** The most emotionally intense placement in the nakshatra: turmoil, ambition and real self-awareness together, stabilised by Jupiter where present.
- **AVA-2-11317** · nak Moola, planet Mars · AVA-2 p457 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Moola aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Endurance and harshness together and a life of long effort.
- **AVA-2-11318** · nak Moola, pada 1, planet Mars · AVA-2 p457 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Moola pada 1
  **THEN** Strong in its own navamsha here: the most penetrating placement in the star and the most exposed to the gandanta's instability.
- **AVA-2-11323** · nak Moola, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p457 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Moola aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Quick, expressive and adaptable.
- **AVA-2-11341** · nak Moola, pada 4, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p458 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Moola pada 4
  **THEN** Stabilises this quarter's turmoil and channels its ambition, and is the placement most needed here.
- **AVA-2-11343** · nak Moola, planet Venus · AVA-2 p458 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Moola aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Artistic feeling and attachment to comfort.
- **AVA-2-11350** · nak Moola, pada 3, planet Venus · AVA-2 p458 · ~/stated
  **IF** Venus in Moola pada 3
  **THEN** Expressive, sociable and charming, with fluctuating means.
- **AVA-2-11358** · nak Moola, pada 1, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p458 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Moola pada 1
  **THEN** Hardship at the opening degrees, compounded by the gandanta, with authority earned only after struggle.
- **AVA-2-11362** · nak Moola, pada 1, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p458 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Moola pada 1
  **THEN** Ambition at the gandanta: unstable, forceful and needing conscious handling.
- **AVA-2-11367** · nak Moola, pada 2, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p459 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Moola pada 2
  **THEN** Detachment applied to acquisition, with ritual and occult inclination.
- **AVA-2-11380** · nak Moola, pada 4 · AVA-2 p460 · +/classical
  **IF** Birth in Moola pada 4
  **THEN** Held very good, letting the native enjoy life fully.
- **AVA-2-11381** · nak Moola, house 1 · AVA-2 p460 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Moola
  **THEN** The personality becomes investigative, intense and root-seeking, dismantling and rebuilding its identity more than once; higher gives fearless truth-seeking, courage, healing capacity and spiritual depth, lower gives instability, impulsiveness, arrogance and self-destruction wearing the clothes of honesty. The native rises when he turns the uprooting inward.
- **AVA-2-11393** · nak Moola, planet Sun · AVA-2 p466 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun as karaka (soul, ego, authority, father) placed in Moola
  **THEN** Identity is repeatedly dismantled and rebuilt and authority is questioned rather than assumed; the relationship with the father carries real karmic weight. Well placed it gives a leader who reforms from the root; afflicted it gives arrogance, conflict with authority and an identity that never settles.
- **AVA-2-11394** · nak Moola, planet Moon · AVA-2 p466 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon as karaka (mind, emotions, mother, comfort) placed in Moola
  **THEN** The mind turns penetrating and restless, unwilling to accept the surface of anything; the bond with the mother carries difficulty. Well placed it gives psychological depth and healing capacity; afflicted it gives emotional turmoil, instability and health disturbance following stress.
- **AVA-2-11395** · nak Moola, planet Mars · AVA-2 p467 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars as karaka (courage, action, energy, conflict) placed in Moola
  **THEN** Mars becomes the root-cutter itself: action aimed at causes rather than symptoms, suited to surgery, investigation, defence, enforcement and crisis work. Well placed it is the reformer who dismantles a broken system; afflicted it is the destroyer, impulsive and aggressive.
- **AVA-2-11400** · nak Moola, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p468 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu as karaka (desire, obsession, foreign, amplification) placed in Moola
  **THEN** Desire fixes on what is hidden, forbidden or foreign. Well placed this gives research into the unorthodox and real originality; afflicted it gives obsession, moral erosion and destruction pursued for its own sake.
- **AVA-2-11402** · nak Moola, house 1 · AVA-2 p468 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Moola
  **THEN** The self is dismantled and rebuilt several times, each version more essential than the last; the manner is direct and searching, vitality is strong and unsettled, and the nerves and stomach carry the strain. Nothing inherited is accepted without testing.

## Purvashadha — nature (85)

- **ASH-1519** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p393 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, energetic, confident, sympathetic, helpful, friendly, romantic, highly principled, futuristic, a deep thinker, strong over enemies, dedicated, optimistic, caring and honest - but a spendthrift.
- **ASH-1523** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p396 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent (ruled by Venus)
  **THEN** Compassionate and caring, assisting those in need; well-liked and very popular through kindness and helpfulness, and sophisticated in social interaction.
- **ASH-1524** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p397 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent (the name meaning invincibility)
  **THEN** A spirit of resistance and a never-say-die mentality; advancing, persevering, brave and optimistic, a guiding light who promotes information and awareness.
- **ASH-1527** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p397 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Always willing to offer advice but never to accept it; despite claims to the contrary he avoids testing his courage and is not truly brave, though pushed to the edge he acts with purpose.
- **ASH-1531** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p398 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Highly cooperative and free of prejudice by ethnicity or religion; sympathetic, humanistic and giving to people who believe in justice and equity, with a powerful ability to inspire.
- **ASH-1534** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p398 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha native challenged to decide by others
  **THEN** He never decides on his own, but when challenged he acts without weighing the pros and cons, which almost always works against him.
- **ASH-1540** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p399 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent, confidence going too far
  **THEN** Becomes overly obstinate and haughty and will not move from his position; draws conclusions on incomplete knowledge without considering others' viewpoints, so he is not good at decision-making.
- **ASH-1541** · nak Purvashadha · ASH p400 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha male native
  **THEN** Exceptionally perceptive and intelligent, portraying himself as bold and daring yet frequently failing to act bravely when it matters; readily persuaded into things that ultimately hurt him.
- **ASH-1543** · nak Purvashadha, pada 1 · ASH p400 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 1 (Leo navamsha, Sun-ruled)
  **THEN** Extremely active, theatrical and creative with a strong sense of ethics, spirituality, pride and confidence; skilled at winning people over, and prioritising self-respect over financial standing.
- **ASH-1551** · nak Purvashadha, pada 4 · ASH p401 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 4
  **THEN** Highly enigmatic and secretive, haughty by nature with a suspicious personality; dishonest, despising intelligent people and bent on wrongdoing, perpetually angry.
- **ASH-1553** · nak Purvashadha, house 1 · ASH p401 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha rising as the ascendant
  **THEN** Strong sense of purpose, tenacity and drive to achieve goals; perceived as ambitious, independent and willing to confront challenges for success.
- **ASH-1560** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon · ASH p403 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Approaches emotions with strong will and resolve and does not give up an emotional commitment quickly; upbeat and cheerful, a jovial presence in social settings with very high moral standards.
- **ASH-1561** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon · ASH p403 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvashadha, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Irritable, sharp, domineering, corrupt in behaviour, possessive over others and egoistic; a lover of aquatic things who drinks water from constant thirst.
- **ASH-1562** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon · ASH p403 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Kind yet shrewd, with sparkling eyes; self-respecting and independent, adored by women, fearless, craving fame, indispensable in the family, aiding the underprivileged, intensely sentimental and philosophical.
- **ASH-1566** · nak Purvashadha, planet Mercury · ASH p404 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Purvashadha, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Makes the native cruel, fond of women, haughty, inebriated, aggressive and craving recognition.
- **ASH-1573** · nak Purvashadha, planet Jupiter · ASH p405 · -/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Purvashadha, negatively disposed
  **THEN** The native is of clouded temperament - good to those he agrees with, but damaging, dictatorial and self-willed toward anyone he is upset with.
- **ASH-1582** · nak Purvashadha, planet Saturn · ASH p406 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purvashadha, negatively disposed
  **THEN** The native becomes incredibly angry and shameless, destructive and short-tempered, given to revenge.
- **NAT-0628** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon · NAT p395 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvashadha (13°20'-26°40' Sagittarius)
  **THEN** Victory, invincibility and perseverance; moves forward with unwavering clarity and adapts with resilience.
- **NAT-0629** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p395 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha governed by Apah, the waters
  **THEN** An unstoppable current that washes away stagnation and refines the spirit; sheds impurities inner and outer for personal and spiritual evolution.
- **NAT-0631** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p396 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha's water-like nature
  **THEN** Does not wait for a path but creates one; never stagnant, always carving new directions and refining what moves forward.
- **NAT-0633** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p397 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha symbolised by the winnowing basket
  **THEN** Discernment and refinement; sheds illusion, emotional baggage and karmic weight to reveal what is truly valuable.
- **NAT-0634** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p398 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha symbolised by the handheld fan
  **THEN** Adaptability and regulation; despite driving ambition the native must pace themselves, keep equilibrium and know when to cool intensity.
- **NAT-0635** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p398 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent (archetypes of the Purifier and Determined Seeker)
  **THEN** Treats obstacles as stepping stones; like water carving rock, navigates trials with fluid determination and unwavering pursuit of higher truth.
- **NAT-0636** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p400 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha's urge to cleanse without self-compassion
  **THEN** Slips into rigidity and harshness, mistaking strict control for spiritual progress.
- **NAT-0637** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p400 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha's steady momentum
  **THEN** Movement that is enduring rather than fast, like water carving stone; success is the natural outcome of constancy, courage and emotional maturity.
- **NAT-0646** · nak Purvashadha, pada 1 · NAT p404 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 1 (13°20'-16°40' Sagittarius), Leo navamsa, Sun-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Confidence, ambition and faith in one's purpose; boldness and courage to overcome obstacles, with excessive pride and dominance as the risk.
- **NAT-0647** · nak Purvashadha, pada 2 · NAT p405 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 2 (16°40'-20°00' Sagittarius), Virgo navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Discerning intellect grounding expansive drive; practicality, strategic planning and structured growth, hindered by over-analysis and rigid perfectionism.
- **NAT-0648** · nak Purvashadha, pada 3 · NAT p405 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 3 (20°00'-23°20' Sagittarius), Libra navamsa, Venus-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Harmony, artistic refinement and impactful connection; thrives in diplomatic and collaborative spaces, risking superficiality and validation-seeking.
- **NAT-0649** · nak Purvashadha, pada 4 · NAT p406 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha pada 4 (23°20'-26°40' Sagittarius), Scorpio navamsa, Mars-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Intense, resilient and transformative; purges attachments and faces the shadow, emerging fortified — true victory through mastery of the self.
- **NAT-0652** · nak Purvashadha · NAT p407 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha's rajasic guna in excess
  **THEN** Ungrounded, restless or overly controlling behaviour.
- **RBS-1-1606** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-1 p215 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvashadha, where Jupiter the spiritual adviser and Venus the political adviser conflict
  **THEN** Great conflict in decision making, so that with time the native acts after little thought and avoids deeper reflection because it confuses him.
- **RBS-1-1608** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-1 p215 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Unbeatable in argument with extraordinary convincing power, never submitting to others whether right or wrong, full of advice for others, very poor in decision-making yet too dominant; hates external show, god-fearing, honest, humble and free of hypocrisy.
- **RBS-1-1615** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-1 p216 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Energy, enthusiasm, vigour and vitality; she takes a final decision only after long deliberation, makes promises she does not fulfil, is a leader among her relatives, and is determined and truthful.
- **RBS-1-1619** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-1 p216 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvashadha — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Helpful and empathetic, confident, self-made and true to himself, full of advice and ideas useful to others; but arrogant, a poor decision maker, and impossible to deter once he has decided.
- **RBS-2-0588** · nak Purvashadha, planet Venus · RBS-2 p147 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Purvashadha Nakshatra, ruled by Venus, symbol the elephant's tusk
  **THEN** Good-looking, creative and artistically inclined, bright and intelligent, good at advising and leading others, but hasty and impulsive in decisions.
- **RBS-2-1649** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p348 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha native deciding, Jupiter being the spiritual and Venus the political adviser
  **THEN** Great conflict arises in decision-making, so that with time he acts after little thought and avoids deeper reflection because it confuses him.
- **RBS-2-1656** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p349 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Unbeatable in argument with extraordinary convincing power, never subduing to others whether right or wrong and full of advice for them, but very poor and yet dominant in decision-making; hates outward show, is God-fearing, honest and humble, highly religious and devoting much time to puja, a collector of antiques who may write poetry.
- **RBS-2-1662** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p350 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Purvashadha
  **THEN** Energetic, enthusiastic and vigorous, greedy but not obstinate, deciding only after deep consideration and straightforward in speech; fond of dogs and pets, makes promises she does not fulfil, hates her parents and brothers, is a leader among her relatives, and is determined and truthful.
- **RBS-2-1666** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p350 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Purvashadha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Helpful and empathetic, confident, self-made and true to himself and the world, giving much advice and many ideas that often help people.
- **RBS-2-1667** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p350 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Purvashadha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Arrogant and a poor decision-maker who cannot be deterred once he has decided to do a thing.
- **RBS-2-1787** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon, sign Aquarius · RBS-2 p378 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada native (this book's second Purvashadha-Aquarius chapter)
  **THEN** Mystery, supernaturalism and occult phenomena, honesty, principle and benevolence, sincerity, determination, discipline and ability to confront physical and mental hardships; worshipper, teacher, broad-minded, respectable, above controversy, soft-spoken and loving toward relatives; endowed with children, sleeps excessively, helping in nature and has the knack of trade.
- **RBS-2-1794** · nak Purvashadha · RBS-2 p379 · +/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Purva Bhadrapada Nakshatra (Aquarius)
  **THEN** Honest and sincere, unwilling to deviate from the right path even at great cost, a born leader capable of extracting work from others and successful once given power and authority; humanitarian and polite.
- **RBS-2-1801** · nak Purvashadha, pada 1 · RBS-2 p381 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 1 (Aquarius 20°00' to 23°20')
  **THEN** Tall and heavy, envious, close to others' mates, a worrier and a fighter; highly aggressive and goal-oriented, works hard for material ends, fond of the opposite sex but does not know how to enjoy bed pleasures; sheep-like face and eyes.
- **RBS-2-1803** · nak Purvashadha, pada 3 · RBS-2 p381 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 3 (Aquarius 26°40' to Pisces 0°00')
  **THEN** Relaxed, peaceful, jolly, nicely dressed and honoured; highly intelligent and knowledgeable, one of the best writers with great creativity and material success; dark complexion, shining attractive body and excellent communication skills.
- **RBS-2-1804** · nak Purvashadha, pada 4 · RBS-2 p381 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 4 (Pisces 0°00' to 3°20')
  **THEN** Humble but miserly, good-looking, of good longevity, busy with his own plans; very sensitive to the external atmosphere and could go either way in life depending on circumstance, needing to channel energy positively; fair complexion, small neck and slim waist.
- **TAM-RA-0563** · nak Purvashadha, planet Moon · TAM-RA p349 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Shadha, the last and most positive nakshatra of Venus
  **THEN** Native is joyous, bubbling, talkative, quick to help others and happy-go-lucky - compassionate, kind, empathetic and forgiving.
- **TAM-RA-0570** · nak Purvashadha, planet Rahu, sign Sagittarius · TAM-RA p355 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purva Shadha, a krur (cruel) nakshatra set in the warrior sign Sagittarius
  **THEN** Invokes the qualities of a warrior - one from a good background makes his mark on society, while one from the lower strata may use the same energy to spread harm and fear as an underworld figure.
- **TAM-RA-0571** · nak Purvashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p355 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purva Shadha for natives born on Aquarius, Libra or Cancer ascendant
  **THEN** Does extremely well.
- **TAM-RA-0575** · nak Purvashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p359 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purva Shadha nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is interested in presenting his art graciously and takes effort to be gracious, hospitable and humane in behaviour.
- **TRI-0946** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent — fan as decoration
  **THEN** The showy, glitzy, glamorous Venusian aspect; feels superior to all nakshatras before it, especially the earlier Venusian ones.
- **TRI-0948** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent — fan as fanning a fire
  **THEN** Knows how to keep a thing going and the fire burning, making it very expressive and expansive; negatively this becomes exaggeration or over-expansion.
- **TRI-0949** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p276 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent — fan as a mask
  **THEN** Conceals facts, information, feelings and personality; a shy and sensitive nakshatra that hides its enthusiasm until it gains confidence.
- **TRI-0951** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p277 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha ruled by Apah, goddess of water
  **THEN** Mysterious, hiding, secretive and shy; flamboyant, enticing and invincible in approach, and like all primordial forces often cruel and harsh in her ways.
- **TRI-0953** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p278 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent — the mermaid archetype
  **THEN** Vain, alluring, sensitive, beautiful, mysterious, enticing, musical, freedom-loving and adventurous.
- **TRI-0954** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p278 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Intense conviction that it cannot lose — a strong notion of invincibility, though the notion can be misplaced, as with Don Quixote.
- **TRI-0955** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p278 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Source of the Sagittarian qualities — leaps of faith, ambitiousness, adventurous spirit, wild exuberance, philosophical zeal and wanderlust; one of the most optimistic nakshatras, but patient enough to bide its time.
- **TRI-0956** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p279 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Completely undaunted by setbacks and does not ponder on failure — which negatively means it hardly ever learns from its mistakes.
- **TRI-0959** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p279 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Slow to jump into new things, but once set in motion almost always ends up going overboard.
- **TRI-0960** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p279 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha at its best
  **THEN** An inspiration to those around; a fresh unique approach that instils life into the hearts and minds of others. Jyeshta is unhappy not knowing the root, Mula finds the root, but only Purvashadha holds the key to joy after the centre is found.
- **TRI-0961** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p279 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Most associated with the joy of living; lives each moment fully without care for past or future, and is never fazed by adverse circumstances.
- **TRI-0963** · nak Purvashadha · TRI p280 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvashadha prominent
  **THEN** Exuberant on achieving its goals and a born natural show-off; how the exuberance expresses depends on the refinement of Jupiter and Venus in the chart.
- **AVA-2-11428** · nak Purva Ashadha · AVA-2 p475 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha placement anywhere
  **THEN** Varchograhana shakti, the power of invigoration to lift and energise; natives are confident, ambitious, idealistic and persuasive, sure of their own cause and able to carry others with them, rising again after every defeat.
- **AVA-2-11430** · nak Purva Ashadha · AVA-2 p475 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha placement, purification through trial
  **THEN** Each planet is not merely intensified but washed - the Sun's ego, the Moon's emotion, Mars's aggression, Venus's desire are all refined through the star's trials; the higher expression is the purified one and the shadow is what still needs washing.
- **AVA-2-11432** · nak Purva Ashadha · AVA-2 p475 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha placement, pride versus ethical conduct
  **THEN** Confidence is the gift of this nakshatra and pride is its shadow; real honour comes from ethical conduct, service and dignified effort rather than ego, and the planet's own dignity decides which way the hinge swings.
- **AVA-2-11433** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p476 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 1, 13d20'-16d40' Sagittarius, Leo navamsha, Sun
  **THEN** The visible quarter: proud, confident, royal and self-assured, with a strong urge to stand out, to be respected and to bring attention to whatever the planet governs; natural dignity, self-belief and ethical pride.
- **AVA-2-11440** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p476 · -/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 1, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Pride turning to ego, the wish for fame at any cost, corruption through relationships, difficulties with wealth and education, delayed success and the over-display of self.
- **AVA-2-11441** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p476 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 2, 16d40'-20d00' Sagittarius, Virgo navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** The refining quarter: intellectual, analytical, alchemical and hardworking, focused on refinement, skill, discipline, learning and career-building; a great deal of hard work is required, with the promise of real spiritual or material accomplishment.
- **AVA-2-11448** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p477 · -/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 2, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Conceit and short temper, narrow-mindedness, criticism and moral confusion where unrefined, along with weakness through excessive dependence on others.
- **AVA-2-11449** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p477 · +/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 3, 20d00'-23d20' Sagittarius, Libra navamsha, Venus, Pushkara
  **THEN** The graceful quarter, a Pushkara pada, auspicious for growth and prosperity: luxury-loving, easy-going, artistic and relationship-oriented, emphasising reaping the fruit rather than heavy labour, with a relaxed and graceful Venusian nature.
- **AVA-2-11455** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p478 · -/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 3, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Illness, loss of wealth through government, disease in the family, excessive luxury, laziness, wealth-obsession and the avoidance of effort.
- **AVA-2-11456** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p478 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 4, 23d20'-26d40' Sagittarius, Scorpio navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** The hidden quarter: secretive, mysterious and intense, holding two extremes - great occult wisdom or material perversity; can lead to deep spiritual research, hidden knowledge, foreign affairs, healing, tantra and genuine occult attainment, or misused, to revenge, secrecy and destruction.
- **AVA-2-11463** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p478 · -/stated
  **IF** Purva Ashadha pada 4, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Revenge, grumbling, secretive behaviour, material perversion, skin trouble, sorrow through the father, hidden enemies and emotional intensity.
- **AVA-2-11479** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p480 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Ashadha aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Comfort, artistic sensibility and attachment to pleasure.
- **AVA-2-11481** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p480 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Ashadha pada 1
  **THEN** Confident feeling and public warmth, with the desire for recognition running through the emotional life.
- **AVA-2-11482** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p480 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Ashadha pada 2
  **THEN** Analytical emotion, careful and self-critical, with the delicate constitution of the quarter.
- **AVA-2-11484** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p480 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Ashadha pada 4
  **THEN** Good in this quarter: depth, intuition and occult sensitivity, with secret suffering beneath the surface.
- **AVA-2-11490** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p480 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Purva Ashadha aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Endurance and harshness together.
- **AVA-2-11496** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p481 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Purva Ashadha aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Quick and expressive, with popular appeal.
- **AVA-2-11516** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Venus · AVA-2 p481 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Purva Ashadha aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Artistic feeling, charm and attachment to comfort.
- **AVA-2-11522** · nak Purva Ashadha, pada 2, planet Venus · AVA-2 p482 · -/stated
  **IF** Venus in Purva Ashadha pada 2
  **THEN** The exception here, doing less well in Virgo's exacting navamsha; the refinement turns critical rather than graceful.
- **AVA-2-11547** · nak Purva Ashadha, house 1 · AVA-2 p484 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Purva Ashadha
  **THEN** The personality becomes proud, confident, idealistic and persuasive, with a real refusal to accept defeat, rising after every setback; higher gives leadership earned through ethics and genuine charisma, afflicted gives arrogance, self-display and the hunger for recognition.
- **AVA-2-11559** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Sun · AVA-2 p488 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun as karaka (soul, ego, authority, father) placed in Purva Ashadha
  **THEN** Becomes proud, dignified and visible, wanting to be respected rather than merely obeyed, with authority sought openly and carried with dignity. Well placed it gives a leader of genuine standing and influence used for dharma; afflicted it gives vanity, the hunger for fame and conflict with the father.
- **AVA-2-11560** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p488 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon as karaka (mind, emotions, mother, comfort) placed in Purva Ashadha
  **THEN** Becomes hopeful, idealistic and remarkably resilient, recovering reliably from emotional injury, though feeling that sees the best in people is also its exposure. Well placed it gives popularity and emotional generosity; afflicted it gives disappointment through misplaced trust and a hope that outruns the evidence.
- **AVA-2-11561** · nak Purva Ashadha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p489 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars as karaka (courage, action, energy, conflict) placed in Purva Ashadha
  **THEN** Becomes purposeful rather than merely forceful, action aimed at a goal believed in and sustained past the point where most energy fails, suited to competition, defence and surgery. Well placed it gives the fighter who wins by continuing; afflicted it gives aggression justified by conviction and revenge.
- **AVA-2-11568** · nak Purva Ashadha, house 1 · AVA-2 p491 · +/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Purva Ashadha
  **THEN** The self separates what matters from what does not, applying the same sorting to itself over a lifetime of reversals; the bearing is dignified and genuinely confident, and setbacks land hard but are survived without exception.

## Uttarashadha — nature (64)

- **ASH-1596** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p410 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Benevolent, sincere, dependable, intelligent, dutiful, friendly, righteous, bold, grateful, helping, invincible, talented, determined, clever, organised, courageous, ingenious and artistic, with team spirit - but socially limited.
- **ASH-1600** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p413 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent (deity the Vishvadevas, the universal gods)
  **THEN** Associated with group prayer and blessings for everybody; being a mridu (soft) nakshatra it gives a kind, forgiving and nurturing temperament, with fortitude and the capacity to overcome challenges.
- **ASH-1602** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p413 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Excessive obstinacy and rigidity of conviction, and a propensity to be authoritarian or domineering.
- **ASH-1608** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p413 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Very quiet and modest, amiable and naive; sincere, taking every task with the utmost care and seeing it through, never lying to anyone.
- **ASH-1609** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p414 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Extremely modest, respecting people for who they are regardless of social standing, with the utmost respect for women, never saying anything hurtful despite strong differences of opinion.
- **ASH-1610** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p414 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha native lacking praise or recognition for his efforts
  **THEN** Lacks the drive to take action and displays unhappiness and depression; his excessive humility and inability to criticise others frequently gets him into trouble.
- **ASH-1613** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p414 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Encourages initiative, success and a sense of responsibility, with remarkable adherence to custom and a practical approach to starting new work - but must be careful not to overcommit or become fixated on constant stimulation.
- **ASH-1615** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p414 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Will not yield to pressure and gives no one his whole trust, but once trust is given it cannot be shaken; consults his reliable people extensively before deciding and never makes rash decisions - though generally a lazy person.
- **ASH-1616** · nak Uttarashadha · ASH p414 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Unable to speak harshly to anyone even in a heated argument, will not voice discontent, and never harbours animosity - but is in some ways a slave to flattery.
- **ASH-1627** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 2, planet Moon · ASH p416 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon afflicted in Uttarashadha pada 2
  **THEN** The native becomes exceedingly egoistic and short-tempered.
- **ASH-1636** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Sun · ASH p418 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** Great sense of duty and accountability with loyalty to community, family and social causes; strong respect for custom, culture and accepted convention.
- **ASH-1640** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Mars · ASH p419 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** Quite aggressive and competitive, but trustworthy and reliable with a strong sense of obligation, confident and courageous in the face of difficulty.
- **ASH-1648** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Saturn · ASH p423 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** May experience major difficulties and impediments in life, but views them as opportunities for improvement; able to defer gratification and make long-term plans demanding tenacity and patience.
- **NAT-0655** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Moon · NAT p411 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttarashadha (26°40' Sagittarius - 10°00' Capricorn)
  **THEN** Transition from inspiration to implementation; quiet, enduring, grounded strength that overcomes obstacles with dignity and steady determination.
- **NAT-0657** · nak Uttarashadha · NAT p412 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha spanning Sagittarius and the pragmatic sign Makara
  **THEN** Bridges the spiritual and material, channelling dharmic virtue into a secular goal-oriented domain; associated with divine favour and victory.
- **NAT-0659** · nak Uttarashadha · NAT p413 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha symbolised by the elephant tusk and the last quarter of a wheel
  **THEN** Power wielded responsibly and guided by higher wisdom rather than brute force; the culmination of effort through endurance and moral integrity.
- **NAT-0660** · nak Uttarashadha · NAT p413 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha's wheel symbol and its karma Cakra link
  **THEN** Success that is not fleeting but built on righteous action and enduring values; victory sustained through continuous growth.
- **NAT-0663** · nak Uttarashadha · NAT p415 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha carrying great responsibility
  **THEN** Isolation, being misunderstood, and the solitude that accompanies leadership; choices are irreversible like the broken tusk.
- **NAT-0674** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 1 · NAT p421 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha pada 1 (26°40' Sagittarius - 0°00' Capricorn), Sagittarius navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Truth-seeking idealism and fearless pursuit of higher knowledge; principled leadership, but excessive zeal risks impracticality and overextension.
- **NAT-0675** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 2 · NAT p421 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha pada 2 (0°00'-3°20' Capricorn), Capricorn navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Most industrious placement of the naksatra: persistence, patience and endurance building legacies that last, with rigidity and status-fixation as the danger.
- **NAT-0676** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 3 · NAT p421 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha pada 3 (3°20'-6°40' Capricorn), Aquarius navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Shift from personal ambition to collective progress; organisational ability and reform of outdated systems — ideal for visionaries, policymakers and strategists.
- **NAT-0677** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 4 · NAT p422 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha pada 4 (6°40'-10°00' Capricorn), Pisces navamsa, Jupiter-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Compassion, intuition and spiritual insight; leadership turns from external success to guiding others toward higher awareness, with escapism as the risk.
- **RBS-1-1630** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-1 p218 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarashadha, meaning the latter unvanquished one, with Vishvedevas as deity
  **THEN** Talented, ambitious, authoritative and clean-hearted, respecting traditional culture and norms, though outwardly egoistic and rude; honest and sincere in dealings and generally fulfilling promises without harming others for gain.
- **RBS-1-1635** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-1 p218 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** He respects all and fears God, deceives and troubles nobody, takes no hasty decision and cannot utter harsh words even in conflict; he shoulders many responsibilities at a young age and swings between maximum happiness at one stage and maximum unhappiness at the next.
- **RBS-1-1640** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-1 p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Female born in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** An obstinate daredevil whose utterances are not good and who jumps into conflict with others, yet who leads a very simple life.
- **RBS-1-1645** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-1 p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarashadha — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Very humble and respectful of others regardless of social or financial position, giving utmost respect to women and never uttering anything bitter despite strong disagreement; but lacking motivation and expecting appreciation, without which he feels unhappy and depressed.
- **RBS-1-1654** · nak Uttarashadha, pada 1 · RBS-1 p220 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarashadha pada 1
  **THEN** Very honest, intelligent, idealistic and truthful, an asset to society with excessive righteousness, never accepting people outside his class; very egoistic and sometimes adamant; whitish pink complexion, bright shining long horse-like face, impressive personality and frank nature.
- **RBS-2-1682** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p353 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Uttarashadha Nakshatra, the latter unvanquished one, whose deity is the Vishvedevas
  **THEN** Talented, ambitious, authoritative and clean-hearted, respecting the traditional culture and norms of society.
- **RBS-2-1684** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p353 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha native under pressure to decide
  **THEN** It is very difficult to force any decision on him; he likes people who take his orders without question and is prone to flattery.
- **RBS-2-1690** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Moon · RBS-2 p354 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttarashadha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Well behaved with many friends, grateful and amiable.
- **RBS-2-1694** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p355 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** Respectful to all and God-fearing, with a black mole around the waist or on the face; he deceives and troubles no one, takes no hasty decision and cannot utter harsh words even in conflict; shoulders many responsibilities at a young age and is subject to maximum happiness at one stage and maximum unhappiness at the next.
- **RBS-2-1699** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p355 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Uttarashadha
  **THEN** An obstinate daredevil whose utterances are not good and who jumps into conflict with others, yet leads a very simple life.
- **RBS-2-1703** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p355 · +/stated
  **IF** Positive side of Uttarashadha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Very humble and respectful to others regardless of their social or financial position, giving utmost respect to women, and so modest that he utters nothing bitter against anyone despite strong differences of opinion.
- **RBS-2-1704** · nak Uttarashadha · RBS-2 p355 · -/stated
  **IF** Negative side of Uttarashadha Nakshatra
  **THEN** Lacks motivation and expects appreciation of his work, failing which he feels unhappy and depressed.
- **TAM-RA-0577** · nak Uttarashadha · TAM-RA p361 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** 'Duty comes first' is the native's essence - he is highly service-oriented, works from definite values and principles, and loves to expand his network of friends.
- **TAM-RA-0580** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p362 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttarashadha in the chart of an evolved soul
  **THEN** Native understands the significance of living a balanced life - the taller the tree, the deeper its roots.
- **TAM-RA-0590** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p366 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttarashadha, ruled by the Sun which cannot stand lies
  **THEN** Native seeks and believes in the truth.
- **TAM-RA-0592** · nak Uttarashadha · TAM-RA p366 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha, a nakshatra of the Sun
  **THEN** Brings a marked individuality - the native loves to live his life by being himself.
- **TAM-RA-0594** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p367 · +/stated
  **IF** Attempting to demotivate or discourage a Rahu in Uttarashadha native
  **THEN** It is very difficult - he attends not to what people say about him but to what he knows about himself.
- **TAM-RA-0595** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p370 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttarashadha nakshatra
  **THEN** A perfect placement signifying a clever person - Rahu cleverly uses the Sun's qualities on Saturn's Capricorn foundation.
- **TAM-RA-0599** · nak Uttarashadha, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p371 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttarashadha for natives born on Virgo, Gemini, Libra, Capricorn, Taurus or Aquarius ascendant, and especially Scorpio, Leo or Pisces
  **THEN** Does well for the first group and exceptionally well for Scorpio, Leo and Pisces ascendants.
- **TRI-0996** · nak Uttarashadha · TRI p291 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha versus Purvashadha
  **THEN** Willpower and firmness give it far greater permanence than its predecessor, and it is more introspective where Purvashadha is extroverted and flamboyant.
- **TRI-0999** · nak Uttarashadha · TRI p291 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha compared with Magha
  **THEN** Shares Magha's elegance, traditionalism, authority and respect for courtesy, but with a more positive, introspective and enduring aspect; shows a person of very good qualities valued for their advice.
- **TRI-1001** · nak Uttarashadha · TRI p292 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha under Saturnine influence
  **THEN** Can make the native rigid and harsh, piercing like the tusk, with intolerance for anything that does not follow normal respectable lines.
- **TRI-1004** · nak Uttarashadha · TRI p292 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarashadha prominent
  **THEN** Courageous and clever in finding ways forward through difficult, dangerous or complex situations; the native gravitates toward or attracts situations presenting many challenges.
- **AVA-2-11596** · nak Uttara Ashadha · AVA-2 p497 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha placement, responsibility before reward
  **THEN** Nothing is handed over early or easily; the native is made to carry duty, bear pressure and prove himself and the reward is proportional to the burden accepted.
- **AVA-2-11598** · nak Uttara Ashadha · AVA-2 p498 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha placement, dharma or downfall
  **THEN** Victory is conditional on righteousness: won through dharma the success is protected and lasting, pursued through ego or unethical means the same rise ends in a fall.
- **AVA-2-11600** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p498 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 1
  **THEN** Lordly, wise, clever, broad-minded, charitable and spiritually inclined, dear to teachers and learned in the arts, sincere in speech, self-confident and righteous, with a strong desire to rise and a natural leadership that works through wisdom rather than force.
- **AVA-2-11605** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p498 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 1, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Pride in one's own knowledge and the drift from channel of guidance to egoistic preacher.
- **AVA-2-11607** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p499 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 2
  **THEN** Hostile to the unrighteous, yet charitable, eloquent, tactful in speech and devout - and equally greedy, conceited and miserly; a mixed nature, disciplined and hardworking while struggling with ego, possessiveness and attachment to worldly success, with a strong focus on goals and love of power.
- **AVA-2-11612** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p499 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 2, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Greed, pride, miserliness and over-attachment to status, with finances that fluctuate through pride, expenditure or ambition.
- **AVA-2-11614** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p500 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 3
  **THEN** Honourable, with a commanding presence and equally arrogant and boastful; clever, talkative, thirsty for knowledge and worldly success, faithful to family and successful through association with it.
- **AVA-2-11620** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p500 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 3, where it goes wrong
  **THEN** Arrogance, boasting and the use of people for gain.
- **AVA-2-11622** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p500 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttara Ashadha pada 4
  **THEN** Religious, devout, charitable, wealthy, truthful and dear to all, strong in vitality, engaged in diverse activities, business-minded and persevering, intelligent, with a love of music and singing, support from friends and financial trustworthiness.
- **AVA-2-11635** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 1, planet Sun · AVA-2 p502 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Ashadha pada 1
  **THEN** Short-tempered, stubborn, religious, famous and wealthy, with strong willpower and a rigid temperament and a tendency to lumbago or lower back pain.
- **AVA-2-11637** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 2, planet Sun · AVA-2 p502 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Ashadha pada 2
  **THEN** Quiet, melancholic, impartial, industrious and suspicious, skilled in music and may earn through music or the fine arts.
- **AVA-2-11639** · nak Uttara Ashadha, pada 3, planet Sun · AVA-2 p502 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Ashadha pada 3
  **THEN** Clever in every kind of exercise and physical discipline, alert, agile and practically intelligent.
- **AVA-2-11660** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Mars · AVA-2 p503 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttara Ashadha aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Charitable, especially towards the opposite sex, though with a mixed motive.
- **AVA-2-11671** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p504 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Uttara Ashadha aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Cunning and cruel, with an unhappy existence.
- **AVA-2-11677** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p504 · ~/stated
  **IF** Jupiter placed in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** Because most of the nakshatra falls in Capricorn where Jupiter is debilitated, its wisdom is tested through practicality and worldly burden; the native must learn to keep dharma alive in a material world.
- **AVA-2-11691** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Venus · AVA-2 p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Uttara Ashadha aspected or conjunct the Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and artistic feeling.
- **AVA-2-11721** · nak Uttara Ashadha, house 1 · AVA-2 p507 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** The personality becomes dignified, disciplined and quietly ambitious, carrying an authority others recognise before it is claimed, trusted with responsibility young and rewarded for it long afterwards. Higher gives leadership earned through integrity and endurance under pressure; afflicted gives rigidity, pride and stubbornness.
- **AVA-2-14255** · nak Uttara Ashadha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p511 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Ashadha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** The mind becomes disciplined, serious and durable - feeling is present but governed, and this native is emotionally reliable in ways others come to depend on; comfort matters less than order. The mother is dignified and demanding. Well placed: honour, learning, contentment, attachment to spouse and children and real emotional stability. Afflicted: melancholy, miserliness and coldness, with charity performed rather than felt.
- **AVA-2-14263** · nak Uttara Ashadha, house 1 · AVA-2 p514 · +/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Uttara Ashadha
  **THEN** This native is serious early and respected late, carrying responsibility before he is old enough to have chosen it; the bearing is dignified and unhurried and people trust it. Dashas touching this degree add duty first and standing afterwards - the lesson is that the position must be deserved before it can be enjoyed.

## Shravana — nature (64)

- **ASH-1658** · nak Shravana · ASH p426 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, well-educated, obedient, socially involved, artistic, prudent, occult-loving, a good listener, eloquent, focused, observant, goal-oriented, intuitive, attractive and generous - but nervous and over-emotional.
- **ASH-1663** · nak Shravana · ASH p428 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent, a nakshatra of the gods
  **THEN** Mostly cheerful and prosperous in life, but troubled by arrogance and ego.
- **ASH-1665** · nak Shravana · ASH p429 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent
  **THEN** All the traits of a gentleman - wisdom, patience, forbearance and tolerance; a noble character popular with followers, and a sincere seeker of truth guided by intuition even when his route diverges from the norm.
- **ASH-1667** · nak Shravana · ASH p430 · -/stated
  **IF** Shravana native whose high intellect is not restrained by humility
  **THEN** Forms extreme, rigid and constricting opinions, believing only he has the correct viewpoint, and develops animosity against those who disagree.
- **ASH-1670** · nak Shravana · ASH p430 · -/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent
  **THEN** Propensity for gossip and jealousy over others' achievements; takes insults and criticism very personally.
- **ASH-1675** · nak Shravana · ASH p431 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana female native
  **THEN** Giving and benevolent, kind to the poor and unable to bear others' suffering - but with a tendency to flaunt her accomplishments and make sure everyone knows of them.
- **ASH-1680** · nak Shravana · ASH p432 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent
  **THEN** Religious, pious and honest, big-hearted and sympathetic, knowledgeable and helpful; recognised by society for courage, intelligence, forgiveness and thankfulness.
- **ASH-1683** · nak Shravana · ASH p432 · -/stated
  **IF** Shravana native manipulating, spreading rumours or disparaging others
  **THEN** The capacity to listen, learn and give meaning is lost; because Shravana is goal-focused, immoral or poorly understood goals turn detrimental.
- **ASH-1684** · nak Shravana · ASH p432 · -/stated
  **IF** Shravana prominent, negatively disposed
  **THEN** Narrow-mindedness; the native may not always make the appropriate decision because his ego is insufficiently restrained.
- **ASH-1686** · nak Shravana, house 1 · ASH p433 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana rising in the kundali
  **THEN** The native becomes cunning, vigilant and receptive - but being excessively generous and humble can spell disaster for him.
- **ASH-1688** · nak Shravana, pada 1 · ASH p433 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 1
  **THEN** Great ambition and dedication, but not consistent in words and deeds.
- **ASH-1695** · nak Shravana, pada 4 · ASH p434 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 4
  **THEN** Kind and helpful but sometimes lets feelings get the better of him and loses control of his emotions; a strong sense of religion and generous giving.
- **ASH-1702** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · ASH p436 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana
  **THEN** Emotional sensitivity that interweaves his feelings with those around him, making him sympathetic and compassionate; dependable and honest with a strong sense of duty.
- **ASH-1706** · nak Shravana, planet Mars · ASH p437 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Shravana
  **THEN** Great tenacity and resolve, sticking with what he sets his sights on; but may be aggressive, especially in formal or work-related environments.
- **AVA-2-14303** · nak Shravana · AVA-2 p520 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana's working rule: the Moon-Saturn field
  **THEN** Shravana is the Moon's star but falls wholly in Capricorn, Saturn's sign, giving it a Moon-Saturn nature - warm receptivity underneath, serious discipline on the surface; the mind is soft and observant yet burdened, patient and responsibility-bound, feeling deeply and showing little, rising slowly and lasting.
- **AVA-2-14305** · nak Shravana · AVA-2 p520 · -/stated
  **IF** Shravana's working rule: the ear misused
  **THEN** The shadow of this star is always the same - gossip, rumour, manipulation through speech and listening to the wrong voices; the dividing line in every reading is whether the listening serves truth or distorts it. Shravana connects a placement but does not purify a dishonest one.
- **AVA-2-14306** · nak Shravana, pada 1 · AVA-2 p521 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 1, 10 deg00'-13 deg20' Aries navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** The ambition quarter - active, ambitious and initiative-oriented, with a strong desire to move ahead, career-conscious and focused on achievement, thinking logically and acting decisively; highly honourable, virtuous, enthusiastic, clever, intelligent and well-connected, but a fault-finder and conceited.
- **AVA-2-14310** · nak Shravana, pada 2 · AVA-2 p522 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 2, 13 deg20'-16 deg40' Taurus navamsha, Venus, Pushkara pada
  **THEN** The refinement quarter and a Pushkara pada, giving comparatively good results for any planet - a strong Venusian grace: diplomatic, polite and tactful, with artistic and musical talent, a design sense, a graceful personality and organisational skill; devout and materially supported, but lustful, greedy and miserly, keeping few true friends.
- **AVA-2-14314** · nak Shravana, pada 3 · AVA-2 p523 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 3, 16 deg40'-20 deg00' Gemini navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** The communication quarter - clever and mentally active, flexible and adaptable, eager to learn and skilled in conversation, with an attractive speaking style; scholarly, wealthy and materially successful, well-versed in speech and writing, but lustful, dishonest, cunning and sickly and full of debts.
- **AVA-2-14317** · nak Shravana, pada 4 · AVA-2 p524 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shravana pada 4, 20 deg00'-23 deg20' Cancer navamsha, Moon
  **THEN** The receptive quarter and the most lunar and emotional pada, bringing sympathy and real understanding of people; the most mass-oriented quarter, connecting with public life, public office and social responsibility, giving emotional intelligence, charity and concern for others; charitable, religious, wealthy, an analyst with a scholarly disposition and an elegant speaker, but questionable in conduct where afflicted and fond of pleasure.
- **AVA-2-14324** · nak Shravana, planet Sun · AVA-2 p524 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Shravana aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** May behave in a socially awkward manner, interested in causing trouble to others and discarded by good people - intelligence or speech misused.
- **AVA-2-14332** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana aspected or conjunct Sun
  **THEN** Fame joined to feeling, with public warmth.
- **AVA-2-14333** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Emotional force, with ambition driving the mind.
- **AVA-2-14334** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana aspected or conjunct Mercury
  **THEN** Intelligent, expressive and popular, gifted in speech.
- **AVA-2-14336** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Comfort, artistic feeling and attachment to pleasure.
- **AVA-2-14338** · nak Shravana, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana pada 1
  **THEN** Ambitious feeling, with the mind directed at achievement and the trust-karma of the quarter applying.
- **AVA-2-14339** · nak Shravana, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana pada 2
  **THEN** Refined and diplomatic emotion, with artistic sensitivity and the Pushkara strength supporting it.
- **AVA-2-14340** · nak Shravana, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana pada 3
  **THEN** Quick, communicative feeling, with skill in speech and a tendency to debt.
- **AVA-2-14341** · nak Shravana, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p525 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana pada 4
  **THEN** The Moon in its own navamsha, in its own star - the deepest receptivity in the nakshatra, giving public understanding and emotional intelligence.
- **AVA-2-14350** · nak Shravana, pada 2, planet Mars · AVA-2 p526 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars in Shravana pada 2
  **THEN** Force softened by refinement, working through organisation and diplomacy rather than confrontation.
- **AVA-2-14354** · nak Shravana, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p526 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Shravana aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Quick and expressive, with popular appeal and a writer's gift.
- **AVA-2-14364** · nak Shravana, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p526 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Shravana aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Name, fame and generosity.
- **AVA-2-14374** · nak Shravana, planet Venus · AVA-2 p527 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Shravana aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and artistic feeling.
- **AVA-2-14384** · nak Shravana, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p527 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Shravana aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Learning joined to discipline, with emotional reserve.
- **AVA-2-14398** · nak Shravana, pada 2, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p528 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Shravana pada 2
  **THEN** Refinement held lightly, with artistic gift and little attachment to its rewards; Ketu's own point at the 282nd degree of the zodiac falls here, shared with Venus.
- **AVA-2-14403** · nak Shravana, house 1 · AVA-2 p529 · ~/stated
  **IF** First lord placed in Shravana
  **THEN** The personality becomes receptive, observant and quietly capable - this native listens more than he speaks and knows a great deal more than he discloses; maturity comes early and recognition late. Supported: real wisdom, popularity through counsel and a reputation for discretion. Afflicted: over-sensitivity, a mind burdened by what it has absorbed and a silence that others misread as coldness or disinterest.
- **AVA-2-14417** · nak Shravana, planet Moon · AVA-2 p534 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Shravana (karakatwa reading), its own nakshatra
  **THEN** The Moon rules Shravana and gives the star its whole character - a mind that receives, retains and understands; emotional intelligence is genuine and unusually accurate, reading a room and knowing what people mean rather than what they state. Public appeal is strong, most fully in the fourth quarter. Supported: popularity, counsel, public standing and real compassion. Afflicted: emotional heaviness, a mind burdened by absorbed grief and manipulation dressed as understanding. The Moon holds a sensitive degree here at 20deg00' Capricorn.
- **AVA-2-14425** · nak Shravana, house 1 · AVA-2 p537 · +/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Shravana
  **THEN** This native takes the world in before he acts on it and knows considerably more than he says; the manner is courteous and reserved, warm underneath, and people confide in him without quite deciding to. Dashas touching this degree bring information, responsibility and slow recognition. The lesson is to speak as carefully as he listens.
- **NAT-0684** · nak Shravana · NAT p428 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana, per Vamana and King Bali
  **THEN** Perceives beyond the surface and influences through wisdom rather than force; knows when to act on guidance even at great personal cost.
- **NAT-0687** · nak Shravana · NAT p430 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana symbolised by the ear
  **THEN** Extraordinary capacity for deep listening and heightened perception, often a refined intuition or sixth sense tuned to frequencies beyond the ordinary mind.
- **NAT-0690** · nak Shravana · NAT p432 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana's power of silence and contemplation
  **THEN** Strength lies not in speaking often but in choosing words wisely and letting insight take form before expression; reads unspoken meanings and subtleties.
- **NAT-0693** · nak Shravana · NAT p433 · +/stated
  **IF** Shravana's lesson of measured presence
  **THEN** Strength lies in perception and adaptability, not force; lasting change comes through understanding, patience and precise action rather than confrontation.
- **NAT-0704** · nak Shravana, pada 1 · NAT p438 · +/stated

- **RBS-1-1672** · nak Shravana · RBS-1 p222 · ~/stated

## Dhanishta — nature (63)

- **ASH-1730** · nak Dhanishta · ASH p443 · +/stated

- **NAT-0716** · nak Dhanishta · NAT p446 · +/stated
