- **NAT-0719** · nak Dhanishta · NAT p448 · -/stated

- **NAT-0720** · nak Dhanishta · NAT p449 · -/stated

- **NAT-0722** · nak Dhanishta · NAT p450 · +/stated

- **NAT-0725** · nak Dhanishta · NAT p451 · -/stated

- **RBS-2-1747** · nak Dhanishta · RBS-2 p367 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Dhanishta

- **TAM-RA-0618** · nak Dhanishta · TAM-RA p385 · +/stated

- **TRI-1087** · nak Dhanishta · TRI p322 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishta prominent, from Pratyusha, Prabhasa and Soma

- **TRI-1098** · nak Dhanishta · TRI p323 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishta prominent

- **AVA-2-14457** · nak Dhanishtha · AVA-2 p543 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishtha's working rule: two signs, two shades
  **THEN** The first two quarters fall in Capricorn and are worldly, ambitious and intense; the last two fall in Aquarius and are social, artistic and group-oriented, though the fourth turns intense again through its Scorpio navamsha.
- **AVA-2-14459** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 1 · AVA-2 p543 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishtha pada 1, 23 deg20'-26 deg40' Capricorn, Leo navamsha, Sun
  **THEN** The fame quarter - a strong urge for worldly accomplishment, easy success in undertakings and a place in the limelight: assertive, competitive and hungry to achieve something visible; long-lived and ambitious, famous and successful, rising through courage and risk from humble beginnings, but lustful, worrisome and unscrupulous where ambition dominates. Not favourable for marital harmony.
- **AVA-2-14463** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 2 · AVA-2 p544 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishtha pada 2, 26 deg40'-30 deg00' Capricorn, Virgo navamsha, Mercury
  **THEN** The skill quarter and the most adaptable pada of the star - success in chosen pursuits, practical intelligence and fine coordination of mind and hand, promoting musical ability and athletic skill alike; scholarly, enthusiastic, truthful, wise and determined, good at socialising, but of fluctuating wealth, fight-prone and cunning, capable of cheating when afflicted. Mercury's mutability is not good for marital steadiness.
- **AVA-2-14467** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 3 · AVA-2 p545 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishtha pada 3, 00 deg00'-03 deg20' Aquarius, Libra navamsha, Venus
  **THEN** The harmony quarter and the most amicable and optimistic portion of the star, carrying the subtlest gifts of music, the fine arts, drama, poetry, group participation and refined social expression; attractive, happy and friendly, courteous, truthful and wealthy, socially graceful, but timid, conceited and stubborn - polite outside, proud and rigid within. Alone among the four, it promotes marital harmony.
- **AVA-2-14471** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 4 · AVA-2 p545 · ~/stated
  **IF** Dhanishtha pada 4, 03 deg20'-06 deg40' Aquarius, Scorpio navamsha, Mars
  **THEN** The intensity quarter - an excess of Martian energy, giving the rhythmic, athletic pada of drumming and the energetic arts, sporting prowess, a warrior's attitude and real sympathy for the underdog; wealthy, tall and strong, helpful to the needy and charitable, able to lead in a crisis, but cruel, harsh and irreligious when afflicted, a talebearer and unscrupulous. This is the hardest quarter for marital harmony.
- **AVA-2-14487** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p547 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Dhanishtha aspected or conjunct Sun
  **THEN** Public warmth and visible standing.
- **AVA-2-14489** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p548 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-14491** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p548 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-14493** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p548 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Ambitious feeling, the mind set on visible achievement and quick to feel a slight; public recognition comes and the emotional life carries the quarter's marital strain. Age-cautions of the fifth, tenth and twelfth years are recorded classically, with long life indicated alongside them.
- **AVA-2-14494** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p548 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Dhanishtha pada 2
  **THEN** Skilled and sociable emotion, with jaundice noted classically at twenty and affliction at twenty-five.
- **AVA-2-14496** · nak Dhanishtha, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p548 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Dhanishtha pada 4
  **THEN** Intense and protective feeling, with classical cautions of poisoning at twelve and theft at twenty-eight.
- **AVA-2-14508** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p549 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Dhanishtha aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Quick and expressive, with popular appeal.
- **AVA-2-14518** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p549 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Dhanishtha aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Name, fame and generosity.
- **AVA-2-14528** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Venus · AVA-2 p550 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Dhanishtha aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and artistic feeling.
- **AVA-2-14538** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p550 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Dhanishtha aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Learning joined to discipline, with emotional reserve.
- **AVA-2-14569** · nak Dhanishtha, planet Moon · AVA-2 p556 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Dhanishtha (karakatwa reading)
  **THEN** The mind becomes ambitious, rhythmic and socially attuned - this native reads a crowd accurately and performs to it naturally; feeling is warm and quick to flare, with the transient anger the star describes, and the third quarter gives the finest emotional refinement in the nakshatra. Supported: popularity, artistic feeling and real emotional intelligence in groups. Afflicted: restlessness, temper and the long ladder of age-cautions recorded in Part Two, which belong chiefly to this planet.
- **AVA-2-14577** · nak Dhanishtha, house 1 · AVA-2 p558 · +/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Dhanishtha
  **THEN** This native moves with timing and is noticed for it - energetic, ambitious and drawn to visible achievement, with a rise that is usually self-made and often from modest beginnings; the presence is commanding, the temper quick and short-lived. Dashas touching this degree bring recognition and the pressure that accompanies it.

## Shatabhisha — nature (86)

- **ASH-1791** · nak Shatabhisha · ASH p458 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Enduring, future-oriented, independent, wealthy, generous, clever, a natural healer, analytical, respectable, influential, a good decision-maker, courageous, outspoken and truthful - but lethargic, addicted, merciless and struggleful.
- **ASH-1796** · nak Shatabhisha · ASH p461 · ~/stated
  **IF** Shatabhisha prominent
  **THEN** Described as secretive and clairvoyant, a synthesis of logical and magical elements; equally prone to a scientific mindset and to a philosophical, meditative outlook through an inherent inclination for solitude.

  **IF** Shatabhisha prominent, negatively disposed

- **ASH-1805** · nak Shatabhisha · ASH p462 · +/stated

- **ASH-1902** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · ASH p483 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Unusual ambitions and pursuits, lured by unconventional routes, beliefs or ways of living; strong urge to transform himself, seeking experiences that produce major life change.
- **ASH-1904** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · ASH p484 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Ideals can become obsessional, with fixation on certain causes; acts and conduct appear odd or unpredictable, and unexpected hurdles arise that nevertheless offer growth and self-discovery.
- **NAT-0785** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Moon · NAT p483 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvabhadrapada (20°00' Aquarius - 3°20' Pisces)
  **THEN** A gateway between worlds: spiritual evolution through intense inner churning, cutting through illusion and surrendering ego-driven limits.
- **NAT-0786** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p483 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada thriving on duality
  **THEN** Growth requires embracing discomfort, navigating extremes and finding harmony within life's contradictions.
- **NAT-0788** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p484 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada as a form of Rudra
  **THEN** Both remover of stagnation and foundation of awakening; destruction, renewal and the dissolution of falsehood.
- **NAT-0789** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p484 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada, per the fall and rebirth of Daksa Prajapati
  **THEN** Rigid structure and arrogance meet obliteration of ego, followed by metamorphosis into humility and devotion; renunciation and singular focus balancing material and spiritual.
- **NAT-0790** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p485 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada symbolised by the two-faced man
  **THEN** An internal split between attachment and detachment, worldly responsibility and inner liberation; multidimensional perception holding opposing truths at once.
- **NAT-0791** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p486 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada symbolised by the sword
  **THEN** Severs illusion and attachment through radical discernment; refinement comes through sharp, sometimes painful removal of what no longer serves.
- **NAT-0793** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p487 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada prominent (archetype of the Transcendent Seeker)
  **THEN** Thrives in crisis and uses it as a catalyst for awakening; deep questioning, intense trials and radical shifts of consciousness, pushing past convention and safety.
- **NAT-0795** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p488 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada's intensity unchecked
  **THEN** Fanaticism, self-destruction and descent into chaos; radicalism, extreme idealism or martyrdom for lost causes, with passion turning to consuming obsession.
- **NAT-0798** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p489 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada's inner contradictions
  **THEN** Ambition must be tempered with humility, control balanced with trust, individuality integrated with interconnectedness; growth comes in cycles, never in a straight line.
- **NAT-0806** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 1 · NAT p492 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada pada 1 (20°00'-23°20' Aquarius), Aries navamsa, Mars-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Courage, resilience and the drive to break boundaries; bold pioneering action that disrupts stagnation, with recklessness and conflict as the risk.
- **NAT-0807** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 2 · NAT p492 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada pada 2 (23°20'-26°40' Aquarius), Taurus navamsa, Venus-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Visionary force grounded into material reality; refinement, patience and structured growth, though fixation on security dilutes the deeper call.
- **NAT-0808** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 3 · NAT p493 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada pada 3 (26°40'-30°00' Aquarius), Gemini navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Intellectual curiosity, quick thinking and innovation; excels at knowledge synthesis and social influence, but prone to overanalysis and emotional detachment.
- **NAT-0809** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 4 · NAT p494 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada pada 4 (0°00'-3°20' Pisces), Cancer navamsa, Moon-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** Deep intuition, compassion and spiritual surrender; drawn to meditation, esoteric study and selfless service, with mood swings, escapism and withdrawal as the risk.
- **NAT-0812** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p495 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada's tamasic guna unchecked
  **THEN** Emotional heaviness, lethargy and existential despair, especially alongside aggravated vata.
- **NAT-0815** · nak Purvabhadrapada · NAT p496 · ~/stated
  **IF** Any graha placed in Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Undergoes intense purification, like Daksa's fall and rebirth, where ego is stripped away to reveal humility and devotion.
- **RBS-1-1747** · nak Purvabhadrapada · RBS-1 p232 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Sincere and hard working at his work, but easily misled and trapped in bad deeds and occult; he spends all his hard-earned money on siblings, spouse and children, for whom alone he earns; he believes in simple living and adheres to traditional culture while modifying it with the times.
- **RBS-1-1750** · nak Purvabhadrapada · RBS-1 p232 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Usually peace loving with occasional outbursts, enjoying good food and a voracious eater, highly principled and religious, balanced in approach, rarely reaching the pinnacle of riches and living a moderate life.
- **RBS-1-1756** · nak Purvabhadrapada · RBS-1 p232 · +/stated
  **IF** Male born in Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Peaceful, loving, simple and highly principled, fond of good food and a voracious eater; impartial in opinion and rejecting blind religious principles; always ready to lend a helping hand to the needy even where he has met hatred and resistance; god-fearing and moderately rich, preferring respect and honour from the learned to accumulating money.
- **RBS-1-1766** · nak Purvabhadrapada · RBS-1 p233 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvabhadrapada — positive and negative sides
  **THEN** Sincerity, dutifulness, endurance, strength, helpfulness, protectiveness and concern for family; but innate tendencies to harm and malice, socially destructive ways and terrorism, and being misled into dishonesty, negativity and immorality.
- **RBS-1-1776** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 4 · RBS-1 p235 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Purvabhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Very sensitive to the external atmosphere and liable to go either way according to circumstance, with a much higher probability of the negative side, so his energies must be channelled positively; fair complexion, small neck and slim waist.
- **RBS-2-0598** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Jupiter · RBS-2 p149 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Purvabhadrapada Nakshatra, ruled by Jupiter, symbol the sword
  **THEN** Dedicated to a cause and willing to fight for what is just and truthful; very caring and considerate, ever ready to help those in need.
- **TAM-RA-0688** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TAM-RA p421 · +/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada nakshatra occupied
  **THEN** Being human is the native's most striking quality; he is a humanist to whom humanity matters above all.
- **TAM-RA-0689** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p422 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** Native chases excellence rather than perfection, and Rahu fires up that pursuit - he seeks to be an excellent example of being human.
- **TAM-RA-0690** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu, sign Aquarius · TAM-RA p423 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the first three padas of Purvabhadrapada, falling in Aquarius
  **THEN** Native is more social and far more interested in networking than one with Rahu in the fourth pada.
- **TAM-RA-0691** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 4, planet Rahu, sign Pisces · TAM-RA p424 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth pada of Purvabhadrapada, falling in Pisces
  **THEN** Native is far more religious and conservative, where the Aquarius portion is dashing, dynamic and humanist.
- **TAM-RA-0692** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p424 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada for natives born on Aries, Sagittarius, Virgo, Gemini, Aquarius or Libra ascendant
  **THEN** A positive placement.
- **TAM-RA-0694** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Moon, sign Aquarius · TAM-RA p424 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purvabhadrapada compared with Moon in Shatabhisha, both in humanistic Aquarius
  **THEN** Shatabhisha makes the native unconventional; Purvabhadrapada makes him louder in righteousness, fighting for equality and human rights.
- **TAM-RA-0696** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p427 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** A humanistic view combined with being total in one's action is what this placement stands for.
- **TAM-RA-0697** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p428 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** Native has the potential to live life in its totality, tasting both its bitterness and its sweetness, and so attains real balance and authenticity.
- **TAM-RA-0698** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu, sign Aquarius · TAM-RA p428 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada in Aquarius
  **THEN** Brings marked maturity to the native, and in an evolved soul produces a thinker of the first rank.
- **TAM-RA-0706** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p432 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** Native is very vocal about issues that matter and would never suffer silently but voices his concerns and feelings to the world.
- **TAM-RA-0715** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Rahu, sign Aquarius · TAM-RA p435 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purvabhadrapada in Aquarius
  **THEN** Native understands the essence of friendship; overall a good human being - unconventional, practical, and able to understand the deeper aspects of human life.
- **TRI-1183** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TRI p350 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada — two-faced man symbol
  **THEN** A Jekyll and Hyde character; always able to put up a normal, socially acceptable, cheerful front while holding its darker side within at all times.
- **TRI-1185** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TRI p350 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada — single ray of Sun symbol
  **THEN** The gloomy desolate aspect where all hope is at a minimum and the light at the end of the tunnel is very faint.
- **TRI-1189** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TRI p352 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** 'Diabolical' sums it up — paranoia, pessimism, debauchery, violence, hedonism, thirst for the macabre, lying, deceit, cruelty and morbidity; the most intense of all the nakshatras.
- **TRI-1195** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TRI p353 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Often indistinguishable from ordinary people, with a strong capacity to fit contemporary society; keeps its idiosyncrasies and nefarious activities secret.
- **TRI-1200** · nak Purvabhadrapada · TRI p354 · -/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Regretfulness is prominent — being contemplative inwardly, the native broods over the past, tormenting himself for the wrongs he has done.
- **TRI-1208** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 2 · TRI p356 · -/stated
  **IF** Planet in Purvabhadrapada pada 2 (23°20'-26°40' Aquarius, Taurus navamsa, Venus)
  **THEN** The more indulgent side; the energy is earthy and the native is likely to give physical expression to this nakshatra's dark sides.
- **TRI-1210** · nak Purvabhadrapada, pada 4 · TRI p356 · ~/stated
  **IF** Planet in Purvabhadrapada pada 4 (0°00'-3°20' Pisces, Cancer navamsa, Moon)
  **THEN** Extremely diabolical — either extremely benign or extremely dangerous; relates both to the persecuted and the persecutor, and its natives are the most socially adept of the four padas.
- **TRI-1220** · nak Purvabhadrapada, planet Saturn · TRI p359 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purvabhadrapada's Saturn influence
  **THEN** Relates to its grief-causing and disciplined aspect, its tendency to physically carry out its dark functionings, and its detachment — the self-tormenting ascetic standing on one foot.
- **AVA-2-17393** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 1 · AVA-2 p588 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Bold, direct and competitive, fearless and courageous, with leadership instinct, strong willpower and quick decision, treating life as a battlefield and refusing to accept defeat; when afflicted, anger, impulsiveness and harsh speech, quarrels and domination, with judgement disturbed by attraction.
- **AVA-2-17397** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 2 · AVA-2 p589 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada pada 2
  **THEN** Patient, wise, stable and genuinely hospitable, liking to host, feed and support others, attracted to good food, luxury and social respect, with a kingly, visible, well-known quality; when afflicted, overindulgence, attachment to comfort and status, wealth becoming ego and comfort quietly reducing tapasya.
- **AVA-2-17401** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 3 · AVA-2 p589 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada pada 3
  **THEN** A brilliant, curious mind with poetic and lyrical ability, cheerful, relaxed and honourable, peaceful in manner and well known, drawn to astrology, philosophy, psychology, the occult, scripture, science and social questions; when afflicted, restlessness and scattered attention, cleverness turning to manipulation and sarcasm, and intellectual arrogance.
- **AVA-2-17405** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 4 · AVA-2 p590 · ~/stated
  **IF** Purva Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Soft-hearted, sentimental, humble and truthful, absorbing the feeling of whatever surrounds him, gentle and long-lived, with real capacity for bhakti, meditation and surrender; when afflicted, emotional unpredictability, secret suffering, hidden fears, self-deception and escapism, with weak boundaries and the absorbing of others' negativity.
- **AVA-2-17409** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p591 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sun in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Public standing joined to a hidden inner life, with intensity kept from view.
- **AVA-2-17413** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p591 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Refinement joined to intensity, with artistic and imaginative capacity.
- **AVA-2-17419** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Sun
  **THEN** Public appeal with a guarded interior.
- **AVA-2-17420** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Emotional force, quick reaction and a fierce protectiveness.
- **AVA-2-17423** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Artistic and imaginative feeling, with attachment to comfort.
- **AVA-2-17425** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Fiery feeling - the mind combative and easily provoked, with courage in a crisis and regret afterwards.
- **AVA-2-17426** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 2
  **THEN** Settled and hospitable emotion, drawn to comfort, food and social respect.
- **AVA-2-17427** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 3
  **THEN** The mildest placement of the four - curious, articulate and cheerful, with poetic feeling.
- **AVA-2-17428** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p592 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada pada 4 (own navamsha)
  **THEN** The Moon in its own navamsha, in Pisces - the deepest emotional placement in the star, giving genuine devotion, intuitive reach and real capacity for bhakti and surrender, with dreams and inner experience carrying meaning; it also carries the sharpest exposure to secret suffering, the absorption of other people's pain and escape.
- **AVA-2-17434** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Mars · AVA-2 p592 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Endurance joined to harshness and sustained effort.
- **AVA-2-17438** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 4, planet Mars · AVA-2 p593 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Purva Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Force turned inward, with protective feeling and the risk of emotional volatility.
- **AVA-2-17450** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p593 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Name, generosity and devotional depth.
- **AVA-2-17460** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Venus · AVA-2 p594 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and imaginative feeling.
- **AVA-2-17470** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p594 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Discipline joined to isolation, with emotional reserve.
- **AVA-2-17471** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p594 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purva Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Endurance and harshness, with sustained conflict.
- **AVA-2-17475** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 1, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p594 · -/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Purva Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Discipline contending with fire - the hardest working placement of the four and the most frictional.
- **AVA-2-17482** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, pada 4, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p595 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Purva Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Imagination amplified, with genuine mystical reach and the sharpest addiction and illusion risks.
- **AVA-2-17488** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p596 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Purva Bhadrapada
  **THEN** The personality becomes intense, dual and hard to read - an ordinary exterior over a deep and often turbulent inner world; life brings crises that burn away what was assumed and the person who emerges is more essential each time, with wisdom won through experience, research capacity and genuine spiritual depth.
- **AVA-2-17489** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p596 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord in Purva Bhadrapada, afflicted
  **THEN** Extremism, secret suffering, self-destructive habit and a public self so unlike the private one that neither feels real.
- **AVA-2-17513** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p600 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Purva Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Arrogance, fanaticism, conflict with authority and an ego that mistakes intensity for principle.
- **AVA-2-17514** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p600 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Purva Bhadrapada (karakatwa)
  **THEN** The mind carries the star's duality most sharply - calm outside, turbulent within, with feeling running far deeper than it shows; intuition is strong and devotional capacity genuine, giving real emotional depth and the capacity to sit with what others cannot face.
- **AVA-2-17519** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p601 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Purva Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Sarcasm, manipulation, deceptive speech, intellectual arrogance and a mind that scatters across too many subjects.
- **AVA-2-17529** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p602 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Purva Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Isolation, confusion, withdrawal from life without arriving anywhere and renunciation used as a name for escape.
- **AVA-2-17530** · nak Purva Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p603 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Purva Bhadrapada
  **THEN** The self stands at the funeral cot - this native is rebuilt by crisis rather than by growth and each burning leaves someone more essential, with vitality carrying heat, acidity, inflammation and digestive trouble.

## Uttarabhadrapada — nature (78)

- **ASH-1911** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p486 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Cheerful, a wanderer, supportive, a public speaker, intelligent, clever, humble, peaceful, wise, a good communicator, analytical, philosophical, active, self-possessed, generous, prosperous and affluent - an introvert with an alluring figure.
- **ASH-1919** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p489 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** A short fuse whose rage lasts only seconds, always on the tip of the tongue - but his ferocious rage when harmed can make anyone cringe with dread.
- **ASH-1930** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p491 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent (Saturn's influence)
  **THEN** An unusual blend of paradoxical features - as much sloth as hardness and drive; unpredictability is the most notable characteristic, and the violently volatile character makes the native a force to contend with.
- **ASH-1931** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · ASH p492 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada with benefic influence from other planets
  **THEN** Kindness, logic and skill in calculation, tranquillity and a sense of equality and justice; some natives have intrinsic innocence and a natural ability to mesmerise people, and a distinct ability to learn through experience.
- **ASH-1936** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 2 · ASH p493 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 2
  **THEN** Strong desire to read ancient classics and to organise life and goals meticulously; intellect trumps wisdom to some extent, and the native is naturally introverted, preferring to share knowledge with the other sex for professional development.
- **ASH-1937** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 3 · ASH p493 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 3 (Libra navamsha, Venus-ruled)
  **THEN** Generous and kind, speaking in a nice tone yet directly and to the point; highly rational with a balanced inclination, leading a remarkably well-adjusted, grounded life.
- **ASH-1939** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 4 · ASH p493 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Reserved and knowledgeable but can be quite sluggish, easily losing control of the senses and becoming agitated over minor concerns; the concealed and cryptic nature gives extremes of wisdom or disorientation, and he can aggressively externalise his energies.
- **ASH-1946** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Moon · ASH p495 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** Idealistic and kind, truly concerned for others' well-being; struggles with internal conflict or inconsistency yet willing to sacrifice for a greater cause, and determined and resilient despite sensitivity.
- **ASH-1958** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Rahu · ASH p498 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** A tendency to escape reality, using spiritual activity or meditation to avoid the harsh facts of life; restlessness and craving make it difficult to retain focus and discipline, with distractions and temptations along the way.
- **NAT-0816** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Moon · NAT p499 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttarabhadrapada (3°20'-16°40' Pisces)
  **THEN** Inner stillness, integration and stability; self-mastery, endurance and profound acceptance of impermanence, with endings held as sacred transitions.
- **NAT-0817** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p499 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada ruled by Ahirbudhnya, serpent of the deep
  **THEN** Governs hidden knowing, unseen forces and the mysteries of the subconscious; power works beneath the surface, ensuring long-term balance.
- **NAT-0818** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p500 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada native, per Ahirbudhnya's rejection and redemption
  **THEN** May face rejection, hardship or the burden of great responsibility, yet finds strength through patience, endurance and inner mastery rather than dominance.
- **NAT-0819** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p500 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada, per Sesa upholding the universe
  **THEN** Carries the weight of past actions, legacies or long-term obligation; must develop inner steadiness and wield influence with patience.
- **NAT-0820** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p500 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada, per Sesa's cyclical shedding of skin
  **THEN** Masters the art of reinvention, learning from past trials rather than being consumed by them; reshapes itself through gradual conscious transformation.
- **NAT-0821** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p501 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada's link to subterranean waters
  **THEN** Hidden reserves of wisdom, endurance and adaptability; ability to work behind the scenes ensuring stability amid upheaval, letting transformation unfold rather than forcing it.
- **NAT-0823** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p502 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada symbolised by the back legs of the funeral cot
  **THEN** Marks the completion of transformation — the final dissolution of attachment before renewal; every ending is a sacred pause for spiritual preparation.
- **NAT-0824** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p502 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada's Ahirbudhnya compared with other serpent naksatras
  **THEN** Silent strength, unseen protection and deep introspection; a force that endures rather than a guardian that reacts.
- **NAT-0825** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p503 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent (archetype of the Depth Keeper)
  **THEN** Guardian of the unseen and silent stabiliser; masterful at operating behind the scenes, shaping institutions and history without standing at the forefront.
- **NAT-0826** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p504 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada's power in action
  **THEN** Comes from timing, patience and understanding of cycles rather than impulsive action; persists through adversity and emerges stronger.
- **NAT-0827** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p504 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada tested by isolation and unrecognised effort
  **THEN** Long-term responsibility weighs on them, but withstanding these trials makes them pillars of resilience and transformation.
- **NAT-0828** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p504 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada's stability turning to stagnation
  **THEN** Hesitation that prevents necessary action; withdraws too deeply, preferring to observe rather than engage, and clings to the status quo when change is needed.
- **NAT-0830** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p505 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada holding onto outdated identities, relationships or ambitions
  **THEN** Stagnation; surrendering what no longer serves is what lets renewal take root.
- **NAT-0838** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 1 · NAT p509 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 1 (3°20'-6°40' Pisces), Leo navamsa, Sun-ruled, Dharma
  **THEN** Courageous spiritual leadership with moral conviction, leading by example; excessive assertion or self-righteousness can overshadow intuitive wisdom.
- **NAT-0839** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 2 · NAT p510 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 2 (6°40'-10°00' Pisces), Virgo navamsa, Mercury-ruled, Artha
  **THEN** Analytical precision refining deep insight into practical application; excels in teaching, research and holistic healing, bridging mystical and material.
- **NAT-0840** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 3 · NAT p510 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 3 (10°00'-13°20' Pisces), Libra navamsa, Venus-ruled, Kama
  **THEN** Relational harmony, artistic creativity and diplomacy; a powerful unifier and mediator, at risk of losing authenticity in the effort to keep peace.
- **NAT-0841** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 4 · NAT p510 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada pada 4 (13°20'-16°40' Pisces), Scorpio navamsa, Mars-ruled, Moksa
  **THEN** The most intense and spiritual segment: inner purification, profound healing and dissolution of ego attachment; the placement of mystics, seekers and transformative healers.
- **NAT-0844** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · NAT p512 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada's tamasic guna dominating without clarity
  **THEN** Inertia, confusion and escapism.
- **NAT-0950** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Saturn · NAT p584 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** Deep contemplation, solitude and mastery of hidden wisdom; the native's greatest achievements require patience, isolation and years of silent methodical thought.
- **RBS-1-0688** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Mars · RBS-1 p98 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** Wound scars on the body, tough speech, dependence on others for livelihood, results only through hard work, impatience, controversy, confrontation and despair.
- **RBS-1-1777** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · RBS-1 p235 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarabhadrapada, which indicates the end of the journey of life and the control of anger and emotion
  **THEN** The native steadily pursues his goals and is cool headed, never in a hurry, understanding that nature takes its own time.
- **RBS-1-1778** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · RBS-1 p235 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** Highly secretive and capable of using his energies even destructively, yet with a softer approach in decisions and an emphasis on justice; very thoughtful, god-fearing, intelligent and concerned for the needs of others.
- **RBS-1-1779** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · RBS-1 p235 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** Impossible to categorise since he fits into most situations, emotional and romantic with the opposite sex yet equally at ease with unemotional friends; a bag of mixed qualities used at different times to win people over.
- **RBS-1-1782** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · RBS-1 p236 · ~/stated
  **IF** Male born in Uttarabhadrapada
  **THEN** He keeps equal relations with high and low, has a spotless heart and troubles nobody; his temper sits on the tip of his nose but is not permanent; he would sacrifice his life for those who love him yet becomes a lion when hurt; wise, practical in speech, and capable of conquering his enemies and attaining a fairly high position.
- **RBS-2-0600** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Saturn · RBS-2 p149 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Uttarabhadrapada Nakshatra, ruled by Saturn, symbol the twins
  **THEN** Wisdom and detachment from Saturn with a dual personality; unassuming and low-key yet very intelligent and sharp, deeply spiritual, calm and collected, endowed with leadership and treating everyone as equal.
- **TAM-RA-0719** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p438 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** Native takes great effort in raising the quality of his thoughts, knowing that quality of thought decides quality of life.
- **TAM-RA-0724** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p441 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada for natives born on Capricorn, Libra, Taurus or Gemini ascendant
  **THEN** More positive for these ascendants.
- **TAM-RA-0725** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p441 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada, whose deity Ahir Budhyana guards the treasures of the deep
  **THEN** Protecting and safeguarding are the nakshatra's nature, and Rahu makes the native excessively protective of his own people.
- **TAM-RA-0727** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p442 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada nakshatra
  **THEN** Native makes a good preacher because he is open-minded, loves to share, is never stingy, and is understanding and social.
- **TRI-1238** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · TRI p366 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Very hard to pin down; wisdom is its keyword and the native acts according to the needs of the moment rather than from any instinctual archetype. Its real motives are always hard to know.
- **TRI-1241** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · TRI p367 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Wisdom gained from experience; calculative in achieving its ends but keeping the big picture in mind; does everything in a controlled fashion with care and restraint, with benevolent, non-self-serving intentions full of empathy and sympathy.
- **TRI-1245** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · TRI p368 · -/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Produces shy, reluctant and passive personalities in no hurry, which at its lowest becomes laziness and inaction at times when action is indispensable.
- **TRI-1248** · nak Uttarabhadrapada · TRI p368 · +/stated
  **IF** Uttarabhadrapada prominent
  **THEN** Reasonability is another key; it seeks the best solution fair to all parties, and though it has an eye for the needs of others it almost always ends up benefiting from them.
- **TRI-1258** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 3 · TRI p370 · +/stated
  **IF** Planet in Uttarabhadrapada pada 3 (10°00'-13°20' Pisces, Libra navamsa, Venus)
  **THEN** The passive, balance-seeking and equilibrium-oriented side; planets here seek wisdom through balance and objectivity.
- **TRI-1259** · nak Uttarabhadrapada, pada 3 · TRI p370 · -/stated
  **IF** Planet in Uttarabhadrapada pada 3, negatively expressed
  **THEN** The native may become too passive and lack the energy to let their findings be known.
- **AVA-2-17568** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 1 · AVA-2 p610 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Kingly and radiant, with an illuminating presence, proud and self-respecting, popular and well liked, result-oriented and wise in carrying out tasks, truthful, charitable, firm on objectives and fond of travel; when afflicted, pride, short temper and a wavering mind, with too much concern for recognition and action taken from ego.
- **AVA-2-17572** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 2 · AVA-2 p611 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Bhadrapada pada 2
  **THEN** Courteous and righteous in conduct, knowledgeable with vast learning, intelligent, clever, analytical and calculating, a good planner and strategic thinker, generous, wise, submissive in manner and respectful of religion; when afflicted, slow grasping in some situations, short temper, a wavering mind and meagre or uneven wealth.
- **AVA-2-17576** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 3 · AVA-2 p611 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Bhadrapada pada 3
  **THEN** Virtuous, honest and intelligent, with a balanced mind and genuine humility before elders, active, wise and religious, fond of music, endowed with sons and aspiring to wide recognition sought through virtue rather than force; when afflicted, envy and tale-carrying, debt that may become submerging, short temper and a passivity that fails to assert what it knows.
- **AVA-2-17580** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 4 · AVA-2 p611 · ~/stated
  **IF** Uttara Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Contented and broad-minded, a storyteller fond of poetry, a collector of cash, with a large family, mysterious and intense with hidden or occult orientation, confused at times and wise at times, both being true of him; when afflicted, confusion and aggression with violence, lust, envy, extreme behaviour, short temper, agitation and a loss of standing.
- **AVA-2-17584** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p613 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Public standing joined to a private nature, with compassion in the manner.
- **AVA-2-17588** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p613 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Refinement joined to dignity, with artistic and social grace.
- **AVA-2-17594** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p613 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Sun
  **THEN** Public warmth over a guarded interior.
- **AVA-2-17595** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p613 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Emotional force, with protective instinct and a quick private temper.
- **AVA-2-17598** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p613 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Artistic and harmonious feeling, with attachment to comfort.
- **AVA-2-17600** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p613 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Warm and visible feeling, with community affection and pride in the emotional life.
- **AVA-2-17601** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p614 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 2
  **THEN** Reserved, analytical emotion - the mind planning while the surface stays still.
- **AVA-2-17602** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p614 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 3
  **THEN** Balanced and harmonious feeling, the most socially graceful of the four, with passivity as the risk.
- **AVA-2-17603** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p614 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** The deepest emotional placement in the star - powerful intuition, occult sensitivity and real psychological reach, with confusion and agitation where unpurified.
- **AVA-2-17609** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mars · AVA-2 p614 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Saturn
  **THEN** Endurance joined to harshness, with sustained solitary effort.
- **AVA-2-17612** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 3, planet Mars · AVA-2 p614 · +/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 3
  **THEN** Energy softened by Venus, working through diplomacy rather than confrontation.
- **AVA-2-17615** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p614 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Quick and intuitive, with counselling ability.
- **AVA-2-17625** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p615 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Name, generosity and devotional depth.
- **AVA-2-17635** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Venus · AVA-2 p615 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and harmonious feeling.
- **AVA-2-17640** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 1, planet Venus · AVA-2 p615 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 1
  **THEN** Refinement joined to dignity, with social standing and artistic feeling.
- **AVA-2-17645** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p616 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Discipline joined to compassion, with emotional reserve.
- **AVA-2-17646** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p616 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Uttara Bhadrapada aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Endurance and harshness, with solitary effort.
- **AVA-2-17657** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 4, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p616 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 4
  **THEN** Rahu's strongest ground here - occult attainment, psychological depth and real transformative power, with the sharpest risks of confusion and extremity.
- **AVA-2-17663** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p617 · +/stated
  **IF** First lord in Uttara Bhadrapada
  **THEN** The personality becomes calm, deep and hard to read - soft in manner and strong in will, with feelings and motives kept private; this native is trusted with what others will not say aloud and is often underestimated for years before his capability becomes visible, with the second half of life markedly better than the first.
- **AVA-2-17664** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p618 · -/stated
  **IF** First lord in Uttara Bhadrapada, afflicted
  **THEN** Suppression, withdrawal, emotional isolation and a privacy so complete that nobody knows what he is carrying.
- **AVA-2-17688** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Sun · AVA-2 p622 · -/stated
  **IF** Sun in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Pride beneath the calm, recognition withheld too long and an ego that suffers quietly from being overlooked.
- **AVA-2-17689** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Moon · AVA-2 p622 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa)
  **THEN** The mind is deep, compassionate and largely hidden - this native feels a great deal and shows little, with intuition strong and reliable and the capacity to sit with other people's suffering genuine, making for a natural counsellor.
- **AVA-2-17692** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mars · AVA-2 p622 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Suppressed anger that surfaces suddenly, confusion, agitation and force applied to a depth the native was not prepared for.
- **AVA-2-17694** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p623 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Hidden cleverness turning manipulative, slow grasping under pressure and knowledge accumulated without ever being applied.
- **AVA-2-17702** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p624 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Obsession with the depths, confusion, extremity, secrecy and a descent undertaken without the preparation this star normally insists on.
- **AVA-2-17704** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p624 · -/stated
  **IF** Ketu in Uttara Bhadrapada (karakatwa), afflicted
  **THEN** Withdrawal without arrival, isolation and a renunciation that was really an avoidance.
- **AVA-2-17705** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, house 1 · AVA-2 p625 · ~/stated
  **IF** First bhava madhya in Uttara Bhadrapada
  **THEN** The self rests on still water - this native is calm on the surface and considerable beneath it, trusted quickly and understood slowly, with vitality durable rather than vigorous and chronic rather than acute complaints; the second half of life is markedly better than the first.
- **RBS-2-1807** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · RBS-2 p383 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada Nakshatra
  **THEN** Wise, prominent among his clan, wears jewels, works as a lawyer or arbitrator, always does constructive work, earns wealth, a giver, endowed with children, religious, wins over enemies, happy, determined, sexy, learned, greatly sacrificing, loved and respected in all circles, fickle and suffers from fund fluctuations; rich, famous, attractive, charming, loyal to friends, follows the virtuous path, always ready to help.
- **RBS-2-1810** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada · RBS-2 p384 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the male born in Uttara Bhadrapada
  **THEN** Treats high and low alike with a spotless heart, dislikes troubling others, is quick-tempered though not lastingly so, sacrifices himself for those who love him but becomes fierce once hurt; wise, expert at attractive speech, capable of vanquishing enemies and attaining high position, and always desirous of the opposite sex's company.
- **RBS-2-1823** · nak Uttara Bhadrapada, pada 1 · RBS-2 p386 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Uttara Bhadrapada pada 1 (Pisces 3°20' to 6°40')
  **THEN** Lustrous, handsome, rich, truthful and popular; works hard to attain and spread knowledge and gain respect, silences opponents' counter-questions, likes non-vegetarian food but avoids eating it in public; attractive body and large head.

## Revati — nature (82)

- **ASH-0092** · nak Revati, pada 1 · ASH p56 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth in Revati pada 1
  **THEN** Happy and successful life.
- **ASH-0095** · nak Revati, pada 4 · ASH p56 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth in Revati pada 4
  **THEN** Problematic for the native himself.
- **ASH-1965** · nak Revati · ASH p501 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** Intelligent, flexible, fortunate, sympathetic, confident, wealthy, cultured, charming, religious, a seeker, polished, artistic, nurturing, conversant, clever, spiritual, creative, sensitive, spontaneous and scholarly.
- **ASH-1969** · nak Revati · ASH p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent (the name meaning wealth and prosperity)
  **THEN** Holy, lovely and affluent with the power to transcend; inherent optimism able to look beyond the ordinary mediocrities of daily life.
- **ASH-1970** · nak Revati · ASH p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent (Kshiradyapani shakti)
  **THEN** Idealism, kindness and sustenance - the power to protect and feed despite fundamental separation; one of the most pleasant constellations, giving a finely polished delicacy of demeanour.
- **ASH-1971** · nak Revati · ASH p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** Helpful and gracefully sociable yet able to withstand situational pressure; composure, cordiality, wisdom and comprehension are the most prominent traits.
- **ASH-1977** · nak Revati · ASH p505 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** Warm and caring, reaching out to others easily; a compassionate attitude that inspires wonder, adoration and love, and a seemingly magical influence over others.
- **ASH-1985** · nak Revati · ASH p506 · ~/stated
  **IF** Revati female native
  **THEN** Dominant and determined both at home and at work, yet observing every stringent ritual and ceremony and fearing God.
- **ASH-1995** · nak Revati, planet Sun · ASH p508 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Revati
  **THEN** Strong empathy and care for others, with a natural inclination to spiritual pursuit, meditation and higher knowledge; nurturing and protective, acting as guardian of those he holds dear.
- **ASH-2000** · nak Revati, planet Moon · ASH p509 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati (symbol the fish swimming in water)
  **THEN** Adaptable and flexible, easily overcoming obstacles and adjusting to changing situations; extremely sensitive and emotionally attuned, profoundly affected by others' emotions and surroundings.
- **ASH-2015** · nak Revati, planet Saturn · ASH p512 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Revati
  **THEN** Hardship and failure are experienced yet the native is resilient enough to push through and finally achieve; conservative in attitude with reluctance to accept novelty or change, and high regard for cultural heritage.
- **AVA-2-20254** · nak Revati · AVA-2 p630 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati placement, general nature (shakti: kshiradyapani, the power to nourish)
  **THEN** Gentle, intelligent, imaginative and unusually good at helping others through a transition, whether a journey, a crisis or an ending; guides, nourishes, accompanies and lets go, and knows how to finish things well.
- **AVA-2-20255** · nak Revati · AVA-2 p630 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati - Mercury rules it, Jupiter owns the sign Pisces
  **THEN** Joins a quick practical intelligence to a wide compassionate wisdom; works by guidance rather than by pressure.
- **AVA-2-20258** · nak Revati · AVA-2 p631 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati afflicted (the boundary theme)
  **THEN** Almost every affliction under this star has the same shape - too much sympathy and not enough discrimination: weak boundaries, excessive sacrifice, money lent emotionally, the partner idealised and a chapter kept open past its close.
- **AVA-2-20260** · nak Revati, pada 1 · AVA-2 p632 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 1, Sagittarius navamsha, Jupiter
  **THEN** Hopeful, generous and wide-minded, drawn to philosophy, teaching, scripture and long journeys; honest and trusting, gives direction easily and is often at his best somewhere other than where he started.
- **AVA-2-20262** · nak Revati, pada 1 · AVA-2 p632 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 1, afflicted expression
  **THEN** Over-optimism and promises made larger than they can be kept; may preach rather than listen, scatter effort across too many interests and trust the wrong person because trusting is easier than checking.
- **AVA-2-20263** · nak Revati, pada 2 · AVA-2 p632 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 2, Capricorn navamsha, Saturn
  **THEN** Contemplative, practical and energetic together; patient, systematic and reliable, working steadily without needing to be watched, more reserved and more capable of saying no than the other quarters.
- **AVA-2-20266** · nak Revati, pada 2 · AVA-2 p632 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 2, Saturn afflicted
  **THEN** Dryness and a tendency to postpone feeling in favour of work; pessimism and a heaviness that crowds out the star's natural warmth.
- **AVA-2-20267** · nak Revati, pada 3 · AVA-2 p633 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 3, Aquarius navamsha, Saturn
  **THEN** Social and religious at once, independent-minded and humanitarian, interested in mysteries and hidden knowledge; drawn to long journeys and usually living away from the place of birth.
- **AVA-2-20270** · nak Revati, pada 3 · AVA-2 p633 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 3, afflicted
  **THEN** Emotional distance and detachment used as an escape from intimacy; restlessness that keeps him moving so that nothing ever has to be settled.
- **AVA-2-20271** · nak Revati, pada 4 · AVA-2 p633 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 4, Pisces navamsha, Jupiter, vargottama and gandanta
  **THEN** Compassionate, dreamlike and intuitive, with strong imagination, a vivid dream life and genuine feeling for other people's suffering; naturally inclined to moksha, and kind to animals and strangers.
- **AVA-2-20273** · nak Revati, pada 4 · AVA-2 p634 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 4, afflicted, at the gandanta threshold
  **THEN** Weak boundaries, escapism and confusion about which life is actually his; may sacrifice past the point of usefulness and find it very hard to end anything cleanly.
- **AVA-2-20275** · nak Revati, planet Sun · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Revati aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Public warmth joined to a compassionate nature, with gain through people.
- **AVA-2-20276** · nak Revati, planet Sun · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Revati aspected or conjunct Mars
  **THEN** Courage and capability, with a sharper edge than this star usually gives.
- **AVA-2-20279** · nak Revati, planet Sun · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Revati aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Refinement and social grace, with artistic capacity.
- **AVA-2-20285** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati aspected or conjunct Sun
  **THEN** Public appeal joined to genuine kindness.
- **AVA-2-20286** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated

  **THEN** Emotional force, with protective instinct and quick sympathy.
- **AVA-2-20287** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated

  **THEN** Intelligent and expressive feeling, gifted in counsel.
- **AVA-2-20289** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati aspected or conjunct Venus
  **THEN** Artistic and imaginative feeling, with charm and attachment to comfort.
- **AVA-2-20291** · nak Revati, pada 1, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 1
  **THEN** Hopeful and expansive feeling, with faith supplying the emotional anchor.
- **AVA-2-20292** · nak Revati, pada 2, planet Moon · AVA-2 p635 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 2
  **THEN** Reserved and practical emotion, steadier than the star usually gives.
- **AVA-2-20293** · nak Revati, pada 3, planet Moon · AVA-2 p636 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 3
  **THEN** Wide, humane feeling, warm towards the many and distant with the few.
- **AVA-2-20294** · nak Revati, pada 4, planet Moon · AVA-2 p636 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 4, the vargottama gandanta
  **THEN** The most sensitive placement in the nakshatra and in the zodiac's final degrees; gives profound compassion, vivid dream life and real spiritual feeling, with the sharpest exposure to dissolution, absorption and escape.
- **AVA-2-20305** · nak Revati, pada 4, planet Mars · AVA-2 p636 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mars in Revati pada 4
  **THEN** Energy dissolving in the gandanta - protective and compassionate where governed, confused or self-defeating where not.
- **AVA-2-20307** · nak Revati, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p636 · +/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Revati aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Quick, intuitive and popular, gifted in counsel.
- **AVA-2-20326** · nak Revati, planet Venus · AVA-2 p638 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Revati aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Charm, comfort and imaginative feeling.
- **AVA-2-20331** · nak Revati, pada 1, planet Venus · AVA-2 p638 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Revati pada 1
  **THEN** Refinement joined to breadth, with artistic feeling and social generosity.
- **AVA-2-20337** · nak Revati, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p638 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saturn in Revati aspected or conjunct Moon
  **THEN** Discipline joined to compassion, with emotional reserve.
- **AVA-2-20349** · nak Revati, pada 4, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p638 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Revati pada 4

- **AVA-2-20358** · nak Revati, house 1 · AVA-2 p640 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-20359** · nak Revati, house 1 · AVA-2 p640 · -/stated
  **IF** 1st lord in Revati, afflicted

- **AVA-2-20389** · nak Revati, planet Sun · AVA-2 p644 · -/stated

- **AVA-2-20391** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p644 · +/stated

- **AVA-2-20393** · nak Revati, planet Moon · AVA-2 p645 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati, afflicted

- **AVA-2-20395** · nak Revati, planet Mars · AVA-2 p645 · -/stated
  **IF** Mars in Revati, afflicted

- **AVA-2-20398** · nak Revati, planet Mercury · AVA-2 p645 · -/stated
  **IF** Mercury in Revati, afflicted

- **AVA-2-20400** · nak Revati, planet Jupiter · AVA-2 p646 · -/stated

- **AVA-2-20404** · nak Revati, planet Saturn · AVA-2 p646 · -/stated

- **AVA-2-20406** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · AVA-2 p646 · -/stated

- **AVA-2-20408** · nak Revati, planet Ketu · AVA-2 p646 · -/stated

- **AVA-2-20409** · nak Revati, house 1 · AVA-2 p647 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0847** · nak Revati, planet Moon · NAT p516 · +/stated

- **NAT-0848** · nak Revati · NAT p516 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0849** · nak Revati · NAT p517 · -/stated

- **NAT-0852** · nak Revati · NAT p518 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0853** · nak Revati · NAT p518 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati symbolised by the fish
  **THEN** Fluidity, adaptability and movement through unseen realms; returns to integrate past lessons rather than merely moving forward.
- **NAT-0854** · nak Revati · NAT p519 · -/stated

- **NAT-0855** · nak Revati · NAT p519 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent (archetype of the Cosmic Wayfinder)

- **NAT-0856** · nak Revati · NAT p520 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati's boundless openness

- **NAT-0858** · nak Revati · NAT p521 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati's kindness unbalanced
  **THEN** Cycles of over-giving that provide for others without replenishing the self; nourishment must flow in both directions.

- **NAT-0872** · nak Revati, pada 3 · NAT p528 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati pada 3 (23°20'-26°40' Pisces), Aquarius navamsa, Saturn-ruled, Kama

- **NAT-0873** · nak Revati, pada 4 · NAT p528 · +/stated

- **NAT-0949** · nak Revati, planet Venus · NAT p583 · +/stated
  **IF** Venus in Revati
  **THEN** Appreciation for beauty in complexity and love of music, art and harmony in nature; sees interconnectedness in everything.
- **RBS-2-1827** · nak Revati · RBS-2 p389 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati Nakshatra
  **THEN** Power to transcend, idealism, benevolence and sustenance, helpfulness, graceful sociability, streaks of knowledge and educational excellence; endowed with good qualities, home-loving, wise, wealthy, learned, fickle, earns good money, sexy, pure-minded, capable, bright and moves in foreign lands; extremely likeable and enjoys great prosperity, but should be careful with finances or could incur much debt.
- **RBS-2-1832** · nak Revati · RBS-2 p389 · ~/stated

- **RBS-2-1838** · nak Revati · RBS-2 p390 · ~/stated
  **IF** Character of the female born in Revati

  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 1 (Pisces 16°40' to 20°00')
  **THEN** Clever, happy, learned, worried and perverted in thinking, oily skin; possessive about professional growth, spiritual and generous but needs to control an urge to overhelp others, often misunderstood for it; handsome or beautiful and egoistic.
- **RBS-2-1850** · nak Revati, pada 4 · RBS-2 p392 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Revati pada 4 (Pisces 26°40' to Aries 0°00')
  **THEN** Barren, clean, courageous, truthful and brave with sharp intellect; a day-dreamer, not among the successful, with an unstable life easily carried away by illusions; medium to short height and slightly overweight.
- **TAM-RA-0745** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p460 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Revati nakshatra

- **TAM-RA-0747** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p461 · +/stated

- **TAM-RA-0748** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p461 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Revati in an evolved meditative individual
  **THEN** There is never a conflict between thinking and feeling; such a native is very clear about his feelings and says 'I love you', never 'I think I love you'.
- **TAM-RA-0751** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p463 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Jupiter's sign and Mercury's nakshatra with the other aspects positive and supportive
  **THEN** Can work wonders for the native's overall growth.
- **TAM-RA-0755** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p466 · +/stated

  **THEN** Everything goes right for the native - the disciple stands in the master's own house.
- **TAM-RA-0757** · nak Revati, planet Rahu · TAM-RA p467 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in Revati with Mercury retrograde
  **THEN** A very sensitive combination - the positive results of Rahu in Revati are not seen.
- **TRI-1284** · nak Revati · TRI p381 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** One of the most pleasing and benevolent nakshatras and extremely fortunate; the eternal optimist, unflustered even by big setbacks, with an innate trust in divine providence.
- **TRI-1285** · nak Revati · TRI p381 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati negatively disposed
  **THEN** Tends to do nothing except keep the faith; relies too much on belief, and those beliefs are not always real.
- **TRI-1287** · nak Revati · TRI p381 · -/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** The nakshatra of the dreamer; through its association with Maya it creates whole worlds around itself, and natives tend to live in universes of their own creation — Maya within Maya, not conducive to enlightenment. The best thing they can do is see things as they are.
- **TRI-1289** · nak Revati · TRI p381 · +/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** The apex of civilisation; places special emphasis on refinement, sophistication, grooming and civilised behaviour, and abhors primitive behaviour.
- **TRI-1295** · nak Revati · TRI p382 · ~/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** Soft but indulgent, deriving the most fun and joy from the drama of life; evolved souls keep observer status while younger souls immerse themselves completely in maya. It has no sense of limits and easily does too much of anything.
- **TRI-1297** · nak Revati · TRI p382 · ~/stated
  **IF** Revati prominent
  **THEN** Has the strongest ability of all nakshatras to cast illusions, while in its lower aspect it just as easily gets caught up in illusions itself; the most tricky and clever of the nakshatras, a chameleon able to put on any act.

## Abhijit — nature (2)

- **ASH-2028** · nak Abhijit · ASH p517 · +/stated

- **ASH-2031** · nak Abhijit, planet Sun · ASH p517 · +/stated
  **IF** Sun in Abhijit, male native
  **THEN** Wisdom, insight and steadiness; well-liked and respected by all, disciplined and keeping to a set schedule, a quick mathematician with a bright mind, gentle, compassionate, sincere and impressive in speech.

## _general — nature (288)

- **ASH-0028** · planet Moon · ASH p37 · ~/stated

- **ASH-0034** · sign Leo · ASH p41 · -/stated

- **ASH-0059** · ASH p53 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Highly creative, intelligent, deeply observant, argumentative, blunt, analytical, hyperactive, hasty, quick-deciding, restless, communicative, sharp memory, but lacking concentration and troubled with family members.
- **ASH-0113** · ASH p60 · -/stated

- **ASH-0145** · ASH p66 · +/stated

- **ASH-0146** · ASH p67 · ~/stated

- **ASH-0147** · ASH p67 · -/stated

- **NAT-0001** · planet Moon · NAT p21 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0004** · planet Moon · NAT p26 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0032** · planet Mars · NAT p68 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0035** · NAT p70 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0885** · NAT p539 · +/stated

- **NAT-0907** · NAT p557 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0914** · NAT p565 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0922** · planet Mars · NAT p566 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0924** · planet Ketu · NAT p567 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0954** · planet Moon · NAT p559 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0956** · planet Sun · NAT p559 · +/stated

- **NAT-0957** · planet Sun · NAT p559 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0958** · planet Mars · NAT p560 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0960** · planet Mars · NAT p560 · -/stated

- **NAT-0963** · planet Mercury · NAT p561 · ~/stated

- **NAT-0965** · planet Jupiter · NAT p561 · +/stated
  **IF** Jupiter influencing the Moon
  **THEN** Fosters emotional stability, an expansive mindset, and the ability to see life's challenges from a higher vantage point.
- **NAT-0974** · planet Rahu · NAT p563 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in any nakshatra
  **THEN** Represents ambition, innovation and disruption, pushing boundaries and seeking unorthodox paths; karaka of obsession, foreign influences, innovation and illusion. Intensifies the nakshatra's energy, amplifying desires and creating an insatiable need for exploration.
- **RBS-1-0005** · RBS-1 p22 · -/stated

- **RBS-1-0006** · RBS-1 p23 · +/stated

- **RBS-1-0007** · RBS-1 p23 · -/stated

  **THEN** It is uncomfortable there and operates with the least strength.
- **RBS-1-0008** · RBS-1 p26 · +/stated

  **THEN** It is the winning planet and prevails in that association.
- **RBS-1-0022** · sign Aries · RBS-1 p37 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Hot-headed and impulsive; reacts suddenly without analysing consequences; a good initiative-taker but tends to follow the crowd.
- **RBS-1-0023** · sign Taurus · RBS-1 p37 · ~/stated

  **THEN** Intelligently switches sides as the situation demands; can win trust and then trick others for personal benefit.

  **IF** Cancer, watery and movable, ruled by the Moon, as the sign of the native
  **THEN** Highly emotional and easily affected; craves security; the mind wavers so decisions are hard to hold to.
- **RBS-1-0026** · sign Leo · RBS-1 p37 · +/stated
  **IF** Leo, fiery and fixed, ruled by the Sun, as the sign of the native

  **THEN** Hardworking and intelligent; works for himself rather than waiting on others and persists at what he begins.
