- **RBS-2-0070** · RBS-2 p29 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Vikrama Samvatsara
  **THEN** Violent in nature, brave, accomplisher of difficult tasks.
- **RBS-2-0072** · RBS-2 p29 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Vilamba Samvatsara
  **THEN** Cunning, greedy, lazy, phlegmatic, weak and fatalistic.
- **RBS-2-0076** · RBS-2 p29 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Vrisha Samvatsara
  **THEN** Boastful, of deplorable traits, keeps mean company, dirty and lazy.
- **RBS-2-0079** · RBS-2 p30 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Vasanta (Spring) Ritu
  **THEN** Beautiful and intelligent, famous, expert in music and arithmetic, follows scripture.
- **RBS-2-0081** · RBS-2 p30 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Varsha (Rainy) Ritu
  **THEN** A winner, intelligent, famous and powerful, beautiful, loves to own vehicles.
- **RBS-2-0083** · RBS-2 p30 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Hemant Ritu
  **THEN** Clever, generous, full of qualities, religious-minded and philosophical.
- **RBS-2-0084** · RBS-2 p30 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Shishir (Winter) Ritu
  **THEN** Loves sweets, large appetite, happy family life, virtuous, handsome, but short-tempered.
- **RBS-2-0088** · RBS-2 p33 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Sunday
  **THEN** Brave, thin-haired, a winner, enthusiastic, brilliant, excess bile, dark complexion.
- **RBS-2-0091** · RBS-2 p33 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Wednesday
  **THEN** Beautiful, sweet-spoken, wealthy, expert in art forms, well-conducted.
- **RBS-2-0092** · RBS-2 p33 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Thursday
  **THEN** A scholar of many qualities, wealthy, beautiful, acquires siddhis, loved by elders.
- **RBS-2-0093** · RBS-2 p33 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Friday
  **THEN** Curly hair, jolly, intelligent, loves white and light clothes, inclined to virtue.
- **RBS-2-0097** · RBS-2 p36 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Atiganda Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Arrogant, cunning, quarrelsome, revengeful, hypocritical; throat pain; a difficult life of obstacles and accidents, and trouble caused to his mother and to family honour.
- **RBS-2-0100** · RBS-2 p36 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Dhriti Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Determined and patient, enjoys the wealth, goods and spouses of others; sound health, wise, peaceful family life and social success.
- **RBS-2-0104** · RBS-2 p37 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Indra Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Powerful, radiant, excess phlegm, best in his family, wealthy and fortunate, but the yoga may limit longevity.
- **RBS-2-0105** · RBS-2 p37 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Parigha Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Gives false witness, bails people out of prison, little appetite, defeats enemies; meets many obstacles, irritable and meddlesome, yet learned and famous.
- **RBS-2-0107** · RBS-2 p37 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Sadhya Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Humble, clever, jolly, efficient in occupation, always a winner, gains the blessing of his deity through mantra; patient and deliberate.
- **RBS-2-0110** · RBS-2 p38 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Siddhi Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Generous, clever, sweet-behaved, fortunate, self-knowing, proficient in every occupation and charitable to the poor, but has health problems.
- **RBS-2-0115** · RBS-2 p39 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Shukla Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Truthful, sense-controlled, strong debater and winner; garrulous, impatient and changeable of mind; expert in art and poetry, educated and financially secure.
- **RBS-2-0116** · RBS-2 p39 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Sukarma Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Jolly, master of arts, brave, a benefactor, virtuous, charitable and wealthy, with a magnificent fortune.
- **RBS-2-0117** · RBS-2 p39 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Vaidhriti Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Restless, critical and scheming, cruel and impious, yet laborious, helpful and generous without getting due credit.
- **RBS-2-0118** · RBS-2 p39 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Vajra Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Intelligent, powerful, truthful, judges jewels, loves ornaments; lecherous, unpredictable and forceful, strong-handed and skilled with weapons, free of financial crisis.
- **RBS-2-0122** · RBS-2 p40 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Vyaghata Nitya Yoga
  **THEN** Cruel, lying, abusive and violent, intent on harming others; yet expert in many works and famous for that skill.
- **RBS-2-0124** · RBS-2 p41 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Deva Gana
  **THEN** Attractive physique, intelligent and sharp, simple and wise, compassionate and humble, helps the poor and distressed, frugal eater, pleasant voice, wealthy.
- **RBS-2-0125** · RBS-2 p41 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Manushya Gana
  **THEN** Religious, proud, wealthy, kind-hearted, proficient in arts, disciplined; tall attractive build with large eyes, warm and helpful.
- **RBS-2-0127** · sign Aries · RBS-2 p42 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mesha Lagna (Aries Ascendant)
  **THEN** Proud, wealthy, virtuous, excessively angry, valiant, yet dependent on others.
- **RBS-2-0128** · sign Taurus · RBS-2 p42 · +/stated
  **IF** Vrishabha Lagna (Taurus Ascendant)
  **THEN** A pleasant talker, a scholar, and loved by all.
- **RBS-2-0129** · sign Gemini · RBS-2 p42 · ~/stated
  **IF** Mithuna Lagna (Gemini Ascendant)
  **THEN** Proud, loves friends, charitable, sensual and lusty, wealthy, destroys enemies, but progresses slowly in life.
- **RBS-2-0130** · sign Cancer · RBS-2 p42 · +/stated
  **IF** Karka Lagna (Cancer Ascendant)
  **THEN** Religious yet sensual, loves sweets, handsome, admires virtuous people.
- **RBS-2-0132** · sign Virgo · RBS-2 p42 · +/stated
  **IF** Kanya Lagna (Virgo Ascendant)
  **THEN** Beautiful, learned in scriptures, fortunate, but lusty.
- **RBS-2-0135** · sign Sagittarius · RBS-2 p42 · +/stated
  **IF** Dhanu Lagna (Sagittarius Ascendant)
  **THEN** Expert in policy matters, religious, intelligent, an important person in his family.
- **RBS-2-0136** · sign Capricorn · RBS-2 p42 · -/stated
  **IF** Makara Lagna (Capricorn Ascendant)
  **THEN** Inclined to evil deeds, many children, greedy and lazy yet hard working.
- **RBS-2-0137** · sign Aquarius · RBS-2 p42 · ~/stated
  **IF** Kumbha Lagna (Aquarius Ascendant)
  **THEN** Steady mind, promiscuous, slow at work, but leads a happy contented life.
- **RBS-2-0144** · RBS-2 p44 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashadha Maasa
  **THEN** Wastes time in useless talk, careless and negligent, respects his teacher, little appetite, virtuous but very proud.
- **RBS-2-0152** · RBS-2 p45 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Phalguna Maasa
  **THEN** Benevolent, efficient in occupation, kind-hearted, powerful, delicate body, a flirt who talks illogically.
- **RBS-2-0155** · RBS-2 p46 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Dwitiya tithi
  **THEN** Charitable, kind-hearted, discriminative, jolly and virtuous.
- **RBS-2-0162** · RBS-2 p46 · -/stated
  **IF** Born on Navami tithi
  **THEN** Uninterested in the affairs of friends, bitter of speech, opposes learned people, mannerless.
- **RBS-2-0163** · RBS-2 p46 · +/stated
  **IF** Born on Dashami tithi
  **THEN** Religious, wealthy, learned in many scriptures, generous, humble, handsome and lusty.
- **RBS-2-0167** · RBS-2 p47 · -/stated
  **IF** Born on Chaturdashi tithi
  **THEN** Cruel, brave and clever, loves humour, lusty, intolerant, angry and opposes others.
- **RBS-2-0174** · RBS-2 p48 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Shakuni Karana
  **THEN** Calm, composed, intelligent, intuitive and full of good deeds; alert, fortunate, knows auspicious moments, follows law and justice, may become a doctor or physician.
- **RBS-2-0177** · RBS-2 p49 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Kimstughna (Kaustuva) Karana
  **THEN** Intelligent but bent on bad deeds, heartless and unpopular, restless and angry, spoils the family reputation; yet fortunate, well educated and prosperous through humanitarian work.
- **RBS-2-0179** · RBS-2 p49 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Balava Karana
  **THEN** Handsome, courageous and sacrificing, keen on sports; brave, a poet, charitable, religious and given to pilgrimage, well educated and successful.
- **RBS-2-0180** · RBS-2 p50 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Kaulava Karana
  **THEN** Loved by all, independent, soft-spoken and sociable; a large friend circle from which he gains favours, with strong self-esteem that never bows to others.
- **RBS-2-0184** · RBS-2 p51 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Vishti (Bhadra) Karana
  **THEN** Heartless, friendless and bent on bad deeds; restless, suspicious, sleeps too much, may engage in immoral acts and goes to extremes to avenge an enemy.
- **RBS-2-0185** · RBS-2 p53 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Ashva (Horse) Yoni
  **THEN** Independent, full of qualities, expert in playing musical instruments, and devotional.
- **RBS-2-0187** · RBS-2 p53 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Gaja (Elephant) Yoni
  **THEN** Honoured by authority, powerful, a sensualist and an enthusiast.
- **RBS-2-0188** · RBS-2 p53 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Marjara (Cat) Yoni
  **THEN** Efficient in his occupation, loves sweetmeats, and is unkind.
- **RBS-2-0190** · RBS-2 p53 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Mesha (Ram) Yoni
  **THEN** Valiant, loves battle, wealthy and benevolent.
- **RBS-2-0192** · RBS-2 p53 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Mriga (Deer) Yoni
  **THEN** Independent, earns by excellent means, always truthful, affectionate to dependants, brave.
- **RBS-2-0194** · RBS-2 p53 · -/stated
  **IF** Born in Sarpa (Snake) Yoni
  **THEN** Angry, cruel, unfaithful, and misappropriates the wealth of others.
- **RBS-2-0195** · RBS-2 p53 · ~/stated
  **IF** Born in Shwan (Dog) Yoni
  **THEN** Enthusiastic, a rebel against his caste, devoted to his parents, religious, virtuous, practical and the best person in his family.
- **RBS-2-0198** · RBS-2 p54 · +/stated
  **IF** Born in Simha (Lion) Yoni, i.e. birth Nakshatra Dhanishta or Purvabhadrapada
  **THEN** Religious, virtuous, practical, full of qualities and the best person in his family.
- **RBS-2-0351** · planet Moon, sign Aries · RBS-2 p102 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in Aries Rashi (Ashvini, Bharani, Krittika pada 1)
  **THEN** Restless, diseased eyes, unfaithful yet pleasing to his wife, fears drowning, hard working, tranquil in old age; adventurous, changeable, moody and flirtatious, meeting many disappointments.
- **RBS-2-0355** · planet Moon, sign Leo · RBS-2 p103 · +/stated
  **IF** Moon in Leo Rashi (Magha, Purvaphalguni, Uttaraphalguni pada 1)
  **THEN** Forgiving, loves travel, eats meat, fearful yet humble and excessively angry, devoted to parents and achieves fame; generous, romantic, decisive, a natural leader who cannot be swayed against his will.
- **RBS-2-0359** · planet Moon, sign Sagittarius · RBS-2 p105 · ~/stated
  **IF** Moon in Sagittarius Rashi (Mula, Purvashadha, Uttarashadha pada 1)
  **THEN** Pious, wealthy and virtuous, loving, knows fine arts, drawing and painting, a wife of good qualities, sweet-spoken, heavy build, and in rare cases a destroyer of his family; an eternal student, blunt and philosophical, drawn to astrology, a heavy gambler and risk-taker, a great spender pursuing many love affairs.
- **RBS-2-0384** · RBS-2 p118 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth with the Moon in a Gandanta junction (end of Revati, Ashlesha or Jyeshta, or start of Ashvini, Magha or Mula)
  **THEN** The native suffers numerous miseries in life, and the parents and other relations may be severely affected.
- **RBS-2-0530** · RBS-2 p135 · +/classical
  **IF** Child survives the Gandanta-arishta
  **THEN** Becomes very strong and powerful, exceedingly brave and fearless, and enjoys much Raja Yoga.
- **RBS-2-0645** · RBS-2 p178 · +/stated
  **IF** Nakshatra of predominantly Sattva guna
  **THEN** Truthful, pure of purpose, self-righteous and drawn to eternal consciousness; fearless, generous, self-controlled, tranquil, charitable and open-minded, finding it hard to hurt others.
- **RBS-2-0646** · RBS-2 p178 · ~/stated
  **IF** Nakshatra of predominantly Rajas guna
  **THEN** Great inner thirst, passionate and ambitious, restless and insecure, wanting recognition yet never satisfied, clinging to pleasant tasks and avoiding difficulty, fickle and easily distracted.
- **RBS-2-0647** · RBS-2 p179 · -/stated
  **IF** Nakshatra of predominantly Tamas guna
  **THEN** Sensuous and materialistic, tied to self-gratification and held back from higher learning by their own needs; yoga and meditation are required to move toward higher consciousness.
- **RBS-2-0665** · RBS-2 p180 · ~/stated
  **IF** Saamudaayika Nakshatra, the 18th constellation from the Janma Nakshatra
  **THEN** Shows group activities and friendship.
- **RBS-2-0666** · RBS-2 p180 · ~/stated
  **IF** Sanghaatika Nakshatra, the 16th constellation from the Janma Nakshatra
  **THEN** Shows group and social activities.
- **RBS-2-0673** · RBS-2 p181 · ~/stated
  **IF** Maanasa Nakshatra, the 25th constellation from the Janma Nakshatra
  **THEN** Shows one's mental state.
- **RBS-2-0726** · RBS-2 p193 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth Nakshatra belonging to Deva Gana
  **THEN** Calmness of mind, maturity of perspective and goodness of behaviour; soft-spoken, good-natured, intelligent and sharp, compassionate and humble, helping the poor, and a frugal eater.
- **RBS-2-0727** · RBS-2 p193 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth Nakshatra belonging to Manushya Gana
  **THEN** Mixed nature holding both the goodness of the Devas and the illnesses of the demons, generally good in conduct but prone to anger and enmity.
- **RBS-2-0728** · RBS-2 p193 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth Nakshatra belonging to Rakshasa Gana
  **THEN** Aggressive in behaviour, rude in communication and rough in dealings, finding it difficult to get on with people and often meeting unpleasant interpersonal experiences.
- **RBS-2-0806** · RBS-2 p219 · +/stated
  **IF** Birth star among the Deva Nakshatra — Ashvini, Mrigashira, Punarvasu, Pushya, Hasta, Swati, Anuradha, Shravana, Revati
  **THEN** Religious by nature, of Sattvic character and charitable.
- **RBS-2-0807** · RBS-2 p219 · ~/stated
  **IF** Birth star among the Manusha Nakshatra — Bharani, Rohini, Purvaphalguni, Uttaraphalguni, Purvashadha, Uttarashadha, Purvabhadrapada, Uttarabhadrapada, Ardra
  **THEN** A mixture of good and bad.
- **RBS-2-0808** · RBS-2 p219 · -/stated
  **IF** Birth star among the Rakshasa Nakshatra — Krittika, Ashlesha, Magha, Chitra, Vishakha, Jyeshta, Mula, Dhanishta, Shatabhisha
  **THEN** Dominance, selfishness and aggression.
- **RBS-2-1856** · RBS-2 p400 · ~/stated
  **IF** A planet's placement in its Nakshatra
  **THEN** A positive placement draws out the good themes of that Nakshatra, while a negative placement draws out its challenging themes.
- **RBS-2-1950** · house 8 · RBS-2 p435 · -/stated
  **IF** House group for depression
  **THEN** Primary house 8 with supporting houses 1, 3 and 12.
- **TAM-RA-0207** · planet Rahu, sign Cancer · TAM-RA p141 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu anywhere in Cancer, unless the native is meditative or has a Ketu-Moon conjunction
  **THEN** The mind's control over the native is very tight and he is constantly engulfed in its clutches.
- **TAM-RA-0209** · planet Moon, house 8 · TAM-RA p143 · -/stated
  **IF** Moon in the 8th house
  **THEN** Native has focused for many births on 8th house matters - sorcery, investigation, research, the underworld, treasuries - and its trademark is fear: he is always fearful and lacks courage unless another planet joins the Moon there.
- **TAM-RA-0213** · TAM-RA p144 · ~/stated
  **IF** Planets placed in the 6th, 8th or 12th house whose house lord is yogakaraka and well placed by house and sign
  **THEN** Such placements are not always bad; never judge them quickly.
- **TAM-RA-0309** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p198 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu granting a desire in any chart
  **THEN** Rahu gives nothing free - grant of money costs peace, grant of sexual pleasure costs awareness, grant of power costs the love life.
- **TAM-RA-0462** · house 1 · TAM-RA p284 · ~/stated
  **IF** Any planet, or Rahu or Ketu, placed in the Ascendant
  **THEN** It is always more powerful there and can either make or break the native's life.
- **TAM-RA-0711** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p433 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in the 3rd, 7th or 11th house
  **THEN** Rahu does exceptionally well there, these being the trikona of the signs he loves.
- **TAM-RA-0746** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p460 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the emotional signs Cancer, Scorpio or Pisces
  **THEN** Generally makes the person an emotional fool; but Rahu in Ashlesha, Jyeshta or Revati uplifts that emotional nature by making the native realise the significance of emotional intelligence.
- **TAM-RA-0756** · planet Mercury · TAM-RA p466 · -/stated
  **IF** Retrograde Mercury in the birth chart
  **THEN** The most difficult of natives to deal with - a boss or a subordinate with retrograde Mercury brings great trouble; only Jupiter's 5th, 7th or 9th aspect (or a positive Sun aspect) softens it, and that in only about one case in ten.
- **TAM-RA-0765** · planet Rahu, house 1 · TAM-RA p475 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the first house
  **THEN** Much focus on the self and a powerful personality; the subtle desire is to shine in society and emerge as a hero, and the native loves to talk about himself - it is always about me.
- **TAM-RA-0767** · planet Rahu, house 1 · TAM-RA p476 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the first house with a negative and poor horoscope
  **THEN** Rahu gives so solid a mask that the real person cannot be known, and such a native can become a fraud and a cheat who is never caught - a master thief.
- **TAM-RA-0769** · planet Rahu, house 1 · TAM-RA p476 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the first house
  **THEN** Whatever level of society the native is born into he always makes his mark and appears outstanding and attractive to others.
- **TAM-RA-0788** · planet Rahu, house 3 · TAM-RA p478 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the third house
  **THEN** A safe Rahu - it is not very destructive in this position, and gives a highly active, dynamic and dashing nature.
- **TAM-RA-0827** · planet Rahu, house 7 · TAM-RA p484 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the seventh house
  **THEN** These natives are social animals with a huge following on social forums and a large network of professional contacts and friends.
- **TAM-RA-0835** · planet Rahu, house 8 · TAM-RA p485 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the eighth house
  **THEN** Native feels himself a special person born with a special mission, and the placement gives real depth of understanding - he grasps what a common person cannot.
- **TAM-RA-0855** · planet Rahu, house 10 · TAM-RA p488 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the tenth house
  **THEN** The fire of ambition raises the native to high positions, but he is never happy with what he has and wants to fly higher, and this ambitiousness ultimately brings many miseries.
- **TAM-RA-0863** · planet Rahu, house 11 · TAM-RA p488 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the eleventh house
  **THEN** Even when his desires are fulfilled the native remains dissatisfied, for a new desire arrives as soon as the last is met, and he spends his life running after them.
- **TAM-RA-0908** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p500 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu-Moon period
  **THEN** Fear engulfs the native - fear of losing friends and precious assets; he lives in doubt and fear and feels constantly insecure.
- **TAM-RA-0955** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p535 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house, the house of material happiness
  **THEN** Native is fired up to seek happiness but is never found happy, because the 'want more' tendency instilled in him never brings satisfaction.
- **TAM-RA-0956** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p535 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house
  **THEN** Brings too many distractions - the native is so haunted by illusion and fantasy that his meeting with the reality of life never happens.
- **TAM-RA-0959** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p535 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house, which is directly connected with the emotions
  **THEN** Native is too emotional and therefore ruled by the mind, dragging himself into emotional situations that leave him more tense and disturbed.
- **TAM-RA-0965** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p536 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in the 1st, 4th, 7th or 10th house
  **THEN** You are dealing with an utterly Rahuish person.
- **TAM-RA-0967** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p536 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu-Moon conjunction in the fourth house
  **THEN** An emotional fool - loud, impulsive and prone to anger over the smallest matters.
- **TAM-RA-0972** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p537 · ~/stated
  **IF** Rahu conjunct Venus, with other strong planets holding the upper hand in the horoscope
  **THEN** The only somewhat safe conjunction that Rahu makes.
- **TAM-RA-0975** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p537 · -/stated
  **IF** Dealing with a native who has Rahu in the fourth house
  **THEN** He is difficult to trust - he appears to be somebody and in reality is something else, and that is the cause of many of his miseries.
- **TAM-RA-0976** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p537 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house
  **THEN** There is no harmony or alignment between what the native thinks, what he feels and what he does - he plans algebra, feels like geometry and ends up doing chemistry.
- **TAM-RA-0977** · planet Rahu, house 4 · TAM-RA p538 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house
  **THEN** Native does not walk the talk - he talks a great deal and then takes an exact U-turn.
- **TAM-RA-0979** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Aries · TAM-RA p538 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Aries
  **THEN** Native is too blunt and aggressive by nature.
- **TAM-RA-0982** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Cancer · TAM-RA p538 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Cancer
  **THEN** Very emotional, very sensitive and very revengeful - and in general Rahu in the 4th gives revengeful tendencies in whatever sign it falls.
- **TAM-RA-0984** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Virgo · TAM-RA p538 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Virgo
  **THEN** Too analytical and too much in the head to be helped; the native distorts the meaning of what was said, leading to many misunderstandings and mishaps - not a good position.
- **TAM-RA-0985** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Libra · TAM-RA p539 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Libra
  **THEN** A good position, far better than in any of the six preceding signs.
- **TAM-RA-0986** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Scorpio · TAM-RA p539 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Scorpio
  **THEN** Not a good sign for Rahu - he is very uncomfortable here and creates many miseries in life.
- **TAM-RA-0987** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Sagittarius · TAM-RA p539 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Sagittarius
  **THEN** Like a drunkard in a temple - the house is badly affected and happiness is at stake.
- **TAM-RA-0989** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Aquarius · TAM-RA p539 · +/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Aquarius
  **THEN** Aquarius is home to Rahu and he is comfortable there; of all the fourth-house positions Libra, Aquarius and Gemini are overall the better ones.
- **TAM-RA-0990** · planet Rahu, house 4, sign Pisces · TAM-RA p539 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu in the fourth house in Pisces
  **THEN** Like a thief in a kindergarten - Rahu is uncomfortable and creates more confusion in life than otherwise.
- **TAM-RA-0996** · planet Rahu · TAM-RA p541 · -/stated
  **IF** Rahu placed in a kendra - the 1st, 4th, 7th or 10th house
  **THEN** The tendency to manipulate matters by every possible means exists within the native.
- **TAM-RA-1009** · planet Ketu, sign Sagittarius · TAM-RA p550 · ~/stated
  **IF** Ketu exalted in Sagittarius, the sign of the warrior
  **THEN** Be careful dealing with such a native - he or she has the courage to fight back and to find freedom.
